Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.21

Chapter 2001
In the Domain of the Gods, any set of Sanskrit characters was considered to have extraordinary value.
The Luo River flowed endlessly, and it had existed in the Domain of the Gods for countless years.
Tens of thousands of years had passed since the seventeenth Divine Era, when the Luo River was located in the Mingchen Region, a heavy rain suddenly erupted.
Because of that heavy rain, a section of the Luo River collapsed.
And in that collapsed section, a huge black rock appeared, and on this black rock were seven golden Sanskrit characters.
This was the origin of the Luo River's Seven Sanskrit characters.
The news spread like wildfire, and it immediately attracted the attention of many powers in the Domain of the Gods. Even the rich and powerful families revealed a strong interest …
The first to receive the news, and the first to act was the Bai Family, ranked eighteenth among the rich and powerful families.
This was because the Mingchen Region belonged to the Bai Family.
Even though the Bai Family sealed off all channels of information in the shortest amount of time possible, the seven Sanskrit characters were still leaked out.
Many rich and powerful families, and many first-rate families, bought these seven Sanskrit characters at a high price. No matter how strict the Bai Family's blockade was, it was impossible for them to completely destroy these Sanskrit characters.
After all, a single Sanskrit character could be copied an unlimited number of times …
There was no way to find out who had taken out the first Sanskrit character, but the Bai Family had indeed been furious at the start.
But when the powerful figures of the Domain of the Gods obtained the Sanskrit characters, they immediately began to decipher them. But when they truly began to study them, the True Gods, Paragons, and Sages of the Domain of the Gods discovered the difficulty.
Although some of the more obscure Sanskrit characters were strange, there were always people who could decipher a few words from them. After all, generation after generation, the True Gods' understanding of Sanskrit would slowly increase.
But the Luo River's Seven Sanskrit characters were far too complicated compared to ordinary Sanskrit characters, and there was almost no way to decipher them.
This problem had troubled the True Gods of the Domain of the Gods, and to this day, there was still no solution.
The vast majority of the True Gods who were interested in Sanskrit had more or less attempted to decipher this problem. After all, not being able to decipher it wasn't anything shameful. With so many powerful figures attempting to decipher it, wouldn't they have struck it rich?
Those who were knowledgeable could tell at a glance that the seven golden Sanskrit characters were the Luo River's Seven Sanskrit characters. Even if the middle-aged man had blurred the characters, the white-robed elder and the youth could still recognize them.
In their opinion, Luo Zheng shouldn't be interested in this famous and unsolvable problem at all!
However, the guy they thought was a celestial being actually showed a strong interest in these seven Sanskrit tablets. Looking at how he restrained himself, he most likely thought that he had picked up a treasure.
Therefore, the two of them were speechless and devastated.
Where did this guy come from? He looked like he didn't know anything about Sanskrit, but he could casually give such accurate insights.
The young man even faintly hoped that this young man was just trying to flirt with the stall owner …
"Stall owner, are you going to sell these seven Sanskrit tablets?" Luo Zheng squinted and asked.
The special thing about Sanskrit was that if you couldn't decipher it, it was worthless. Other than being used to appreciate it, it was useless.
Based on Luo Zheng's current understanding, Devanagari characters that had been completely deciphered were extremely rare in God's Domain. He was currently considering how to obtain this set of Devanagari characters at a lower price.
The middle-aged man's thoughts were probably similar to the white-robed elder's and the youth's thoughts.
In God's Domain, those who didn't recognize the Luo River's Seven Sanskrit characters were all rookies among rookies. Seeing that a fish had taken the bait, he naturally had to serve it well.
The middle-aged man's head drooped and his eyelids opened slightly. He glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently and sneered. "It wasn't easy for me to obtain these seven Sanskrit tablets. However, since I've set up a stall here, I naturally have to sell them. However, I'll only sell them to people who know their value!"
"Name your price." Luo Zheng couldn't be bothered to waste time with him and simply said, "Name your price."
The middle-aged man stretched out a hand and lazily said, "Five thousand Shenwu coins!"
Hearing the middle-aged man's words, the surrounding stall owners were shocked.
When they saw Luo Zheng's intense interest in the Luo River's Seven Sanskrit characters, they all looked at Luo Zheng as if they were looking at an idiot.
If it weren't for the tacit rule of their profession that they couldn't casually start a competition, they would have immediately brought the Luo River's Seven Sanskrit characters to Luo Zheng. Not five hundred, not fifty, just five Shenwu coins would be enough!
However, at the same time, these stall owners were inwardly cursing the middle-aged man for being brainless. This youth was only a Lesser True God. Even if they killed him, he probably wouldn't be able to fork out five thousand Shenwu coins. No matter how black-hearted he was, he wouldn't be able to scam someone like that.
In truth, the middle-aged man also knew that Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to fork out five thousand Shenwu coins. He had only named this price to extort a little and test Luo Zheng's reaction.
Normally, rookies like this would reveal a depressed or disappointed expression.
However, Luo Zheng's expression made the middle-aged man's heart beat wildly …
This fellow was clearly hesitant and moved!
"No way, he can really fork out a few thousand Shenwu coins?" The middle-aged man's expression didn't change as he stared at Luo Zheng's expression.
The white-robed old man and the youth continued to watch without batting an eyelid. The two of them were also whispering to each other.
"He really wants to buy it."
"Five thousand Shenwu coins isn't enough to scare him off. How many Shenwu coins does this fellow have?"
Luo Zheng frowned slightly, then smiled and said, "Don't you think your price is a little high?"
The middle-aged man watched Luo Zheng's every move and continued to speak in a lazy voice. "It's hard to say how much this item will cost. For those who know their stuff, these seven Sanskrit characters are priceless treasures. Forget five thousand Shenwu coins, even five hundred thousand Shenwu coins would be the same. For those who don't know their stuff, even five Shenwu coins would be considered expensive."
He wasn't wrong.
However, the standard for knowing their stuff was to be able to decipher these seven Sanskrit characters …
Five thousand Shenwu coins wasn't worth mentioning to Luo Zheng. With his current net worth, even ordinary families couldn't compare to him. Moreover, his inner world was producing an endless supply of Shenwu coins.
"It's too expensive." Luo Zheng shook his head.
"As you wish." The middle-aged man also grasped Luo Zheng's thoughts. He judged that as long as Luo Zheng planned to leave, he would immediately plunge the price … Let alone five thousand, even if it was a hundred Shenwu coins, he would still laugh his head off.
Naturally, he didn't know that he had met a true nouveau riche today.
"Alright then, five thousand it is." Luo Zheng pursed his lips.
As soon as he said this, the middle-aged man's narrowed eyes widened. When he saw Luo Zheng flip his hand and a huge pile of black Shenwu coins poured out, his heart almost flew out of his chest. He suddenly felt that this world was a little unrealistic.
The surrounding stall owners also had their mouths wide open. They stared dumbly at Luo Zheng and the Shenwu coins that were pouring out …
"A, a deal?" The white-clothed old man couldn't maintain his composure.
The young man exchanged glances with him. Just as the middle-aged man was about to pounce on the pile of Shenwu coins, the two of them rushed over like the wind.
"Wait!" The white-clothed old man blocked Luo Zheng's path.
The young man used a fiendish expression to glare fiercely at the middle-aged man.
These two people who suddenly dashed out made Luo Zheng rather puzzled.
"What's the matter?" Luo Zheng asked.
"These seven Sacred Glyphs aren't worth this price. If you really need them, this old man can give them to you for free!" The white-clothed old man said to Luo Zheng with a smile.
Hearing the white-clothed old man's words, the middle-aged man immediately became anxious. That was five thousand Shenwu coins! He shouted, "Do you know the rules? How can you do this! I'm the one who negotiated this deal with him! "
The young man coldly sneered, "Rules? The rules of Mingsword God City are set by my family. If you still want to stay here, don't talk to me about rules! "

Chapter 2002
The faces of the surrounding stall owners turned solemn.
Although they couldn't do anything in non-offensive areas, the Sword Tribe still had an unparalleled influence in non-offensive areas.
On the chest of this young man, there were alternating black and white stripes. This was one of the unique symbols of the Sword Clan, and the Sword Clan enjoyed absolute privileges in all the divine cities in the Ruomu Region.
Seeing that the middle-aged man didn't say anything, the young man collected the Shenwu coins on the ground. When he turned around, his originally cold face was filled with warmth.
"You're giving it to me for free?" Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback.
In truth, Luo Zheng had considered that the price of these Sanskrit characters might be inflated.
But with Luo Zheng's current wealth, he didn't care too much about it …
But no matter how inflated the price was, these seven unique Sanskrit characters were still worth a lot. The white-robed old man had said that he would give it to him for free, as if it were as simple as buying cabbage. Luo Zheng couldn't wrap his head around it.
"Indeed, I can give it to you for free. This is because the rubbing of the Luo Shui Seven Sanskrit characters is very widespread in the divine territory. It can't be considered a secret, so naturally, it's not worth much," the young man said with a smile.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng suddenly understood. But his gaze went past the young man and saw the middle-aged man's pitiful appearance, his eyes full of annoyance. Luo Zheng smiled and casually grabbed a handful of Shenwu coins. "As you said, in the eyes of the right people, these Sanskrit characters are priceless. Five thousand Shenwu coins isn't impossible … but since someone has exposed it today, can you sell it to me for a hundred Shenwu coins?"
The middle-aged man had already lost all hope. He was about to become rich overnight, but someone had forcefully disrupted his hopes.
Now that he saw that Luo Zheng was willing to pay a hundred Shenwu coins, he was flabbergasted. Even a hundred Shenwu coins wasn't a small sum to many True Gods, let alone the fact that the Luo Shui Seven Sanskrit characters weren't worth much.
"This …"
The young man and the white-robed old man also widened their eyes.
They had already said that they would give it to the young man for free, but he was still willing to spend a hundred Shenwu coins. Did he really have so much money that he didn't know where to spend it?
Although this price was still a little ridiculous, it wasn't enough to make them feel heartache for Luo Zheng. Moreover, Luo Zheng had insisted on it, so they couldn't just stop him.
"Yes, yes …"
The middle-aged man hurriedly nodded, as if he was afraid that Luo Zheng would go back on his word. He then folded the Luo Shui Seven Sanskrit characters and handed over to Luo Zheng …
The surrounding stall owners were rather envious at first. With five thousand Shenwu Coins in front of them, it was enough to make many True Gods risk their lives.
After being stopped by the young man, they felt a little better.
Now that he saw Luo Zheng settle the deal with a hundred Shenwu coins, he was naturally envious of the middle-aged man.
..............................
In the beginning, they had treated Luo Zheng as a fat sheep who didn't know the ways of the world. Now, they understood that he was a true nouveau riche, a person who didn't care about Shenwu coins at all. If this person could visit their stall, they would make a fortune.
After Luo Zheng collected the remaining Shenwu coins, the young man took the opportunity to say, "Friend, since you're fated to visit my Famous Sword God City, would you do me the honor of having a chat?"
However, at this moment, the surrounding stall owners began to shout out one after another.
"Great Golden Heavenly Witchcraft characters, from the Heavenly Witchcraft Region, twin Sanskrit characters …"
"Three-eyed Golden Sanskrit, three pieces in a set …"
"…"
Ever since Sanskrit was passed down to the world, it had formed a complicated faction.
Based on the surface characteristics of Sanskrit, countless names and interpretations had been born.
Although these names were laughable and those who truly understood Sanskrit wouldn't even bother to talk about them, to these low-level True Gods, it was a way to make a living.
As a secondary life form from the universe, Luo Zheng didn't know whether to laugh or cry when he saw these True Gods with unlimited lifespans selling things like street vendors.
The rules of the divine territory had completely given birth to a different kind of society.
He also knew that these stall owners were shouting for him to patronize them.
He had just entered Famous Sword God City, so he could indeed choose some Sanskrit characters to bring back …
Luo Zheng glanced at the young man and smiled faintly. "Sure, but I want to choose some Sanskrit characters first."
The young man and the old man in white revealed a trace of helplessness on their faces. The rubbings of the common goods on the street were either undecipherable Sanskrit characters or Sanskrit characters that everyone knew. How could there be anything good?
Since Luo Zheng had said so, the young man and the old man in white could only accompany him.
The pitiful two of them couldn't even be bothered to look at these True Gods selling Sanskrit characters in Famous Sword God City. Now, they had to accompany Luo Zheng to sweep up the goods …
Luo Zheng himself didn't have a deep understanding of Sanskrit. Everything he knew about Sanskrit came from Luo Nian.
Along the way, he chose Sanskrit characters that were strangely shaped or pleasing to the eye.
The young man and the old man in white shook their heads. Luo Zheng's performance was completely that of a novice. Then how could the scene at the city gate be explained?
"This Pear Blossom Sanskrit, two hundred Shenwu coins …"
A stall owner just stretched out his hand.
The young man smiled faintly. "We have this Sanskrit. We'll give you one free later."
The stall owner's face stiffened, but he didn't know what to say …
"This Sanskrit is called Dragon Body Sanskrit. Look at the front and back of this brush, it's like a dragon flying in the sky … It's priced at one hundred and fifty coins …"
"We have this too. Worthless little thing. I'll remember it for Brother Luo!" The young man laughed mischievously.
Along the way, the young man, the old man in white and Luo Zheng introduced themselves to each other.
The old man in white was called Mo Dian, and the young man was called Mo Po 'er.
What surprised Luo Zheng even more was that this Mo Po 'er was actually the city lord of Mingjian God City. According to him, Mingjian God City had just changed city lords. He was the new city lord and was only the acting city lord. He still needed to observe for a while before he could truly take over Mingjian God City.
Along the way, Luo Zheng chose about twenty Sanskrit characters. Under Mo Po 'er's obstruction, he didn't let Luo Zheng spend a single Shenwu coin. He only said that these Sanskrit characters would be given to Luo Zheng, and the quality of the copies would be far better than the ones sold here!
Not long after, Luo Zheng was invited to the tallest sword-shaped building in Mingjian God City.
All the lines in the Sword Clan's main hall were horizontal and vertical. The sword-shaped decorations were filled with killing intent. In Ruomu Domain, sword-related culture occupied an extraordinary position.
"These are the Sanskrit characters that Brother Luo chose just now." An attendant placed a stack of Sanskrit characters in front of Luo Zheng.
Just as Mo Po 'er had said, these Sanskrit characters were also printed, but the quality was incomparable to the ones sold on the streets. Every Sanskrit character was gilded with a golden frame, and the surface was covered with a layer of pure glass. The design was exquisite like a work of art.
Luo Zheng didn't stand on ceremony and accepted these Sanskrit characters.
Then, he heard Mo Po 'er ask, "Brother Luo, you came to Ruomu Domain at this time. You must be here for the Dao Battle?"
Luo Zheng nodded. "Of course."
In his heart, Luo Zheng wasn't here for the Dao Battle …
It was purely based on Jing Wuhuan's analysis that Yu Taibai would bring Luo Yan here.
But Ruomu Domain was a large domain in the divine territory. God knew how many divine cities like Mingjian God City there were. He didn't know where to find Yu Taibai and the others.
"That's good." Mo Po 'er smiled. "The Dao Battle this time is very, very important to our Sword Clan …"
She emphasized the word "very" twice, which made Luo Zheng feel even more strange.

Chapter 2003
Before coming to Ruomu Region, Jing Wuhuan had explained the process of the Dao Contest to Luo Zheng in detail.
There were many True Gods who wanted to participate in the Dao Contest.
Most True Gods didn't even have the chance to come to Ruomu Region because the journey was too far.
Some of the more resolute people had traveled thousands of miles to come here. They had even used up all of their family's wealth.
Even so, these True Gods still didn't have the right to participate in the Dao Contest …
Almost 90% of the True Gods stopped at the God City. Even the West Pole Ruomu didn't have the right to go up!
Even if they climbed up Ruomu, they still had to face the Sword race's tests.
In the end, only a few hundred True Gods were qualified to participate in the Dao Contest. Most of these True Gods came from wealthy families.
Hence, there were very few True Gods who could sit in the middle of Ruomu Region and discuss the Dao!
This Ruomu Region extended countless roots into the sea. 87 of them extended all the way to the shore.
The Sword Race built eighty-seven divine cities beside the eighty-seven roots, and Ming Sword God City was one of them.
Every time the Dao Contest started, these God Cities would act as a temporary base to select candidates and send those with the most potential to Ruomu Region.
This was also one of the missions of every City Lord.
Mo Po 'er had just been appointed as the acting City Lord of Sword God City. He desperately wanted to achieve some results to strengthen his position.
He hoped to find one or two people from Sword God City who had the right to participate in the Dao Contest …
This wasn't so easy.
There were simply too few grassroots True Gods who had a solid foundation. According to the previous years, out of the 87 True Gods who were recommended, there were only about 50 to 60 people who could truly participate in the Dao struggle. Divided among the various True Gods, there was less than one person on average!
The Sword God City was actually a relatively remote god city, so this time, he had to make a move personally.
He didn't expect that he and Mo Dian would notice Luo Zheng's monstrous actions when they were observing from the city gate.
Not to mention that Luo Zheng had the original golden Sanskrit words, just seeing that he could accurately read the meaning of the two Sanskrit words was enough to shock them.
However, Luo Zheng's actions after entering the city made him seem like a rookie. This really confused Mo Po 'er.
"I think so," Luo Zheng nodded and replied.
Mo Dian glanced at Luo Zheng and asked, "Since Brother Luo is here to fight for the Dao, you must have prepared a lot. I also admire Brother Luo's speech in front of the city gate. However, why is Brother Luo unable to read normal Sanskrit? You don't seem to understand Sanskrit? "
Not to mention the seven Sanskrit characters of Luo River, Luo Zheng did not even know the famous Sanskrit characters that could be seen everywhere. This was their greatest doubt.
Luo Zheng only smiled faintly at this. "In my opinion, Sanskrit is just a language used by those creatures who are above their level. Those who can recognize it are those who can recognize it. Those who can't recognize it are those who can't recognize it. However, these True Gods in the Domain of the Gods have forcefully added their own understanding and classified it into mysterious categories. Let me ask you, are they really able to recognize this Sanskrit?"
Luo Zheng's question stunned Mo Dian.
Mo Po 'er clapped her hands and said with a smile, "Not bad! .... You've jumped out of the fence! Thinking about it, these Sanskrit words shouldn't have made up so many strange names. It's a mistake! "
Luo Zheng's words were often said by true masters of Sanskrit.
After all, those masters had their own understanding of Sanskrit, and even had experience in deciphering Sanskrit. However, such people were few and far between. Thus, they didn't care about the sects and names of the True Gods.
Mo Po 'er and Mo Dian naturally didn't know.
Luo Zheng had already come into contact with golden Sanskrit when he was still a Xiantian lifeform.
He did not study Sanskrit in the same system as the True Gods in the Divine Realm.
Even though Gu Bei had left behind many insights on Sanskrit, Luo Zheng didn't have time to digest them. He spent most of his time cultivating.
Later on, Luo Nian, who was only in his teens, seemed to have been enlightened. He began to casually decipher these Sanskrit words, giving Luo Zheng the illusion that these Sanskrit words weren't that difficult.
Now that he'd arrived at Mingjian City, Luo Zheng realized that it wasn't that Sanskrit was easy to decipher, but that Luo Nian was too monstrous.
Thus, before Luo Zheng could figure out the situation, he'd already been treated as a "Master" ….
… …
Under Mo Po 'er's warm invitation, Luo Zheng stayed the night in the manor.
The next day, Mo Po 'er personally led Luo Zheng to the west gate.
The west gate of Mingjian City was at the edge of the sea. Once you stepped out of this gate, you'd be able to step onto one of Ruo Mu's roots.
Under normal circumstances, the west gate was closed.
Only the disciples of the sword race had the right to enter.
After the battle of Dao began, all the divine cities would undergo a first round of screening. Those who didn't meet the requirements would be barred from entering. Those who passed the first round of screening would be able to successfully climb up Ruo Mu.
When Mo Po 'er brought Luo Zheng to the west of Mingjian City, they saw a long line!
Countless True Gods were lined up, waiting for Mingjian City's screening. Among them, there were hundreds of guards who were maintaining order.
"So many people …." Looking at the long line, Luo Zheng was stunned.
The winding line from the west gate was probably tens of miles long. How long would it take to screen them one by one?
Seeing Luo Zheng's shocked expression, Mo Po 'er acted as if this was nothing out of the ordinary. "The first round of screening will last for at least half a year. There aren't many people now, but after a while, there will be more. This line will stretch all the way from inside the city to outside the city …."
Hearing Mo Po 'er's words, Luo Zheng was also in disbelief.
"Wouldn't it take a few months for these people to line up?" Luo Zheng asked.
Mo Po 'er chuckled. "More than a few months. When the battle of Dao began, the True Gods at the top all started lining up here two or three years in advance. The ones at the front started lining up after the last battle of Dao ended …. They've been lining up for about ten years."
Luo Zheng was completely speechless.
"There's no choice. Some people aren't even here to participate in the battle of Dao. They're lining up so that they can transfer their spots to the True Gods who need them. The ones at the front of the line usually get a few hundred Shenwu Coins if they transfer their spots to the disciples of the big families!" Mo Po 'er said helplessly.
Many True Gods worked hard for many years and couldn't earn much Shenwu Coins.
Time wasn't worth much to True Gods. Anyway, they could cultivate while lining up. Naturally, there would be some weirdos who would take advantage of this loophole ….
Mo Po 'er glanced at Luo Zheng and smiled. "Brother Luo, I personally recommended you. Of course, you don't have to follow behind these people."
After he finished speaking, he led Luo Zheng past the long line of people that was like a dragon …
In front of the west gate, three True Gods in white robes sat at the entrance. They carved a very strange circular array on the ground.
The array was ten feet in diameter. The array pattern was made up of long swords. They were placed together, leaving a small path in the middle.
A peculiar aura was being emitted from the formation.
One of the three white-robed True Gods waved at the True God at the front of the line, indicating that True God should enter.
That True God took a deep breath, then stepped into the array.
The moment that True God stepped in, all the long swords in the array began to hum. That True God seemed to have entered a certain state. His body began to tremble.
After that True God took the first step, he couldn't take the second step no matter what.

Chapter 2004
The Real God's action instantly caused the other Real Gods to burst into laughter.
The Real God blushed and glared at the people around him angrily. Then, he quickly walked away.
To be able to be at the front of the queue, the Real God had also queued for a long time. In the end, he had only taken one step. No one would be in a good mood.
Luo Zheng fixed his gaze on the formation that was formed by the longswords on the ground. After looking at it for a while, he didn't notice anything special.
He knew that the Sword Tribe's Path Struggle was definitely related to the True Meaning of Path. However, he didn't comprehend any True Meaning of Path …
After the Real God left, another young man appeared in front of the queue.
The young man wore a long robe that was embroidered with purple and gold silk. There was also a beast badge that was fastened to his waist. He reckoned that it was the symbol of a big family.
He looked disdainful and waved at the three Real Gods in white robes. He then asked calmly, "Is it my turn?"
"Please!"
One of the Real Gods in white robes waved his hand.
The young man walked forward confidently. The moment he stepped into the formation, the longswords started buzzing again.
Most of the people who were confident had real abilities.
The young man's performance was much better than the previous Real God. After he took a step forward, he closed his eyes and walked back and forth in the formation as though he was taking a stroll in a courtyard.
"Buzz …"
He had only taken a few steps when one of the longswords floated up and flew toward the young man at a slow speed.
At that moment, Luo Zheng felt a unique conception from the longsword.
"The aura … has become stronger …"
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
Ever since Luo Zheng had entered the non-attacking area, he had been suppressed by an irresistible aura.
According to Jing Wuhuan, the aura should be the True Meaning of Path that was given off by the skeleton on Ruo Mu.
If the Sword Tribe wanted to test it, they would make an issue out of the True Meaning of Path. However, Luo Zheng didn't see any clues previously.
After the sword floated up, Luo Zheng felt that the True Meaning of Path that was given off by the formation had become stronger. In the end, he was able to confirm it.
"This test … is to test one's understanding of the True Meaning of the Dao?" Luo Zheng's face revealed an astonished expression.
According to his knowledge, the True Dao Intent was absolutely not something that an ordinary True God could come into contact with. Even if it was a disciple of a wealthy family, the number of people who had the qualifications to come into contact with the True Dao Intent was extremely small.
However, hearing Luo Zheng's words, Mo Po 'er was slightly surprised. Her eyes lit up as she stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "Brother Luo, have you heard of the True Meaning of Dao?"
In the Divine Domain, there were many people who studied Sanskrit. The reason was that Sanskrit was spread everywhere. Everyone hoped to find some clues from it and become rich overnight or be put in an important position.
However, most of the True Gods didn't know that the True Gods of the aristocratic families, and even the Sages, were most concerned about the few golden Sanskrit characters that recorded the true meaning of the Dao!
Ordinary True Gods simply didn't know what the True Meaning of Dao meant, much less the world beyond the end of the divine path. In the eyes of the True Gods, the so-called Dao Struggle had become a grand event about Sanskrit …
"En." Luo Zheng nodded in response.
Mo Po 'er blinked and then shook her head with a smile. "The True Meaning of Dao isn't something that these True Gods can understand. Our Sword Tribe only uses these' True Meaning Spirit Swords' to test the True Gods' compatibility with the True Meaning of Dao …"
"True Meaning Spirit Swords?" Luo Zheng looked at those swords, and his eyes flashed slightly. When one of the swords floated up, Luo Zheng gradually felt that it wasn't ordinary.
"En, these True Meaning Spirit Swords were once grandmist treasures." Mo Po 'er said, "However, after we forged these grandmist treasures, we didn't fully condense the grandmist dipper within them. Instead, we placed them in Ruo Mu's core and left them there for several divine epochs …"
True Meaning Spirit Swords weren't much of a secret in the Sword Tribe. After all, only the Sword Tribe had the method to create these spirit swords. Mo Po 'er didn't hide anything from Luo Zheng.
These True Meaning Spirit Swords would absorb the True Meaning of Dao emitted from the skeleton over the several divine epochs.
After being processed by the Sword Tribe's secret method, these True Meaning Spirit Swords would be able to sense the compatibility of normal martial artists …
As long as they could make three True Meaning Spirit Swords float up, they would be considered to have passed the formation and qualified to open the West Gate and climb to Ruo Mu.
"Therefore, those who can activate the True Meaning Spirit Swords don't mean that they have the ability to comprehend the True Meaning of Dao. They only have a certain compatibility." Mo Po 'er laughed. "As for testing their understanding of the True Meaning of Dao, haha, that's too difficult even for the disciples of the Noble Families. How can it be used on these True Gods?"
Luo Zheng nodded. He also realized this.
If any True God could comprehend the True Meaning of Dao, how difficult would it be to surpass the Divine Dao?
At this time, another True Meaning Spirit Sword floated up from the sword formation and stopped the youth with its tip.
"Two," Mo Po 'er said plainly. "This person seems to have the ability to climb Ruo Mu."
Just as Mo Po 'er predicted, two more True Meaning Spirit Swords floated up. The four True Meaning Spirit Swords circled around the youth and followed him all the way until he passed through the small path in the center of the formation. The four True Meaning Spirit Swords slowly returned to their positions.
"Wow!"
"Wang Family's Eldest Young Master passed!"
"I'm so envious, he can climb Ruo Mu!"
"Congratulations, congratulations …"
The young man turned his head. Although his eyes were filled with excitement, he did his best to restrain his joy. A faint smile appeared on the corner of his mouth. He then said, "Second brother, we agreed that if we pass, we will all be rewarded."
The True God at the front of the group turned out to be the young man's younger brother. Hearing his elder brother's words, the second brother smiled, turned around and said, "Elder brother said that everyone will be rewarded!" With that said, he slapped his waist and handfuls of shenwu coins were thrown towards the group!
"Wow! Wang Family is really rich! "
"So many Shenwu coins, hurry and snatch …"
"This is mine!"
The Wang Family's second young master took out handfuls of Shenwu coins and threw them out. Instantly, many True Gods snatched them up. In just a short while, he had already thrown out three to four hundred Shenwu coins.
"These two from Wang Family are really …" Mo Po 'er's face had a trace of disdain.
Ruo Mu Region's Wang Family was originally a top tier family. They relied on the sword race to be a tyrant.
These two young masters were of the Wang Family's direct line of descent. Naturally, they were rich.
The two brothers naturally wouldn't line up obediently. After spending some Shenwu coins, they naturally moved to the front of the line.
Now that their elder brother passed, they were throwing Shenwu coins to celebrate.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng only smiled and asked, "Four True Meaning Spirit Swords, what level is that?"
"What level? Barely passable, "Mo Po 'er said disdainfully." There are a total of 23 True Meaning Spirit Swords in this array. When I was 13, I could already activate six. This year, I can already activate nine … Follow me. "
Saying this, Mo Po 'er led Luo Zheng towards the formation.
After the Wang Family's second young master finished throwing out Shenwu coins, it was his turn to test.
"If Wang Family's second young master passes, we'll also throw out Shenwu coins to celebrate."
"Wang Family's second young master will definitely pass …"
After those True Gods tasted the sweetness, they all began to speak words of praise.
Wang Family's second young master laughed, "Of course."
But when he walked towards the formation, Mo Po 'er walked in front of him and said flatly, "There's an esteemed guest who wants to test first. You can wait at the back."
"Esteemed guest? What do you mean? "
Wang Family's second young master paused. He looked around and saw Luo Zheng behind Mo Po 'er. Naturally, he knew who the esteemed guest was.

Chapter 2005
Ruo Mu Region's Wang Family was considered a vassal of Sword Tribe.
As a first-tier family, Wang Family had a huge business and had a deep relationship with Sword Tribe for so many God Epochs.
Second Young Master saw that the young man from Sword Tribe cut the queue and his face turned ugly.

"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"

"Pfff! Pfff!"
Some of the swords pierced Luo Zheng's body, and his body was immediately pierced through.
However, this was ultimately an illusory world. After his body was pierced, it rapidly recovered. However, the pain was incomparably real, causing him to involuntarily grimace in pain.
Outside the array.

Mo Po 'er's face was calm.
He didn't tell them the method to pass through this array in advance, and didn't even reveal the simple rules. Naturally, he couldn't be as familiar as the eldest young master of the Wang Family, who immediately grabbed four swords of true intent.
Mo Po 'er did this because he wanted to see Luo Zheng's true potential.
If Luo Zheng really couldn't even pass this stage, or if it was extremely difficult and he barely passed, he wouldn't need to waste his energy on Luo Zheng.
In the illusion, Luo Zheng's body was still pierced by those long swords.
"The first True God's face is full of pain, and his body is trembling because he can't avoid these long swords …"
"These long swords are so dense that even I can't avoid them. That young master of the Wang Family shouldn't be able to avoid them either. There must be a trick …"
Luo Zheng pondered.
Actually, Luo Zheng had confidence in dodging by using the Dao of Nature and Eight Turns Flying Smoke. However, at this stage, Luo Zheng definitely wouldn't do so.
"The young master of the Wang Family levitated a sword …"
"So many long swords are exactly the same …"
"Mo Po 'er said that the true intent of Dao contained in the sword of true intent is slightly stronger than the true intent of Dao shrouded in a non-offensive area."
"I understand. That kid is testing me."
Luo Zheng's eyes were blazing.
He didn't dodge these long swords anymore. Instead, he felt the dense swords.
Although it was painful to be pierced by these long swords, it wasn't unbearable!
After being pierced by dozens of swords, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. "It's you!"
"Shua!"
Then, Luo Zheng's hand reached out in an instant and grabbed one of the countless long swords!
This was the sword of true intent hidden within!
At the same time …
One of the twenty-three sword of true intent in the array suddenly buzzed and floated up. The tip of the sword pointed at Luo Zheng and began to spin slowly.
"Since you've caught the first one, you already know the principle. The rest should be fast, right?" Mo Po 'er smiled slightly.
"It's just the first one. It's too ordinary."
"So slow. I've already caught the third sword of true intent. Hehe."
The two young masters of the Wang Family continued to mock him coldly.
Naturally, they didn't know that Luo Zheng hadn't even understood the basic rules before being sent into the array by Mo Po 'er.
After catching the first sword of true intent, all the long swords on this path instantly disappeared.
"So it's like that …"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a faint smile as he grabbed the sword of true intent and walked forward.
Actually, the principle of the array used by the Sword Tribe wasn't difficult. It was to create countless illusory long swords. These long swords all contained the same True Intent of Dao. It all came from the aura of a non-attacking place.
However, there were twenty-three sword of true intent. Because they had been sent into Ruo Mu's core to absorb, they contained a little more True Intent of Dao.
If someone was sensitive to True Intent of Dao, they would be able to identify these twenty-three real swords from the dense long swords!
"Eh …"
However, after Luo Zheng caught the sword of true intent, he felt that something was amiss.
The True Meaning Spirit Sword in Luo Zheng's hand didn't calm down. Instead, it continued to hum. The amplitude of the hum wasn't small, and there was even a faint whine. As it continued to whine, it wanted to break free from Luo Zheng's hand and fly away.
Luo Zheng noticed this. Mo Po 'er, the three white-robed true gods, and the two Wang brothers also noticed this.
"What's wrong with this sword of true intent?"
"It seems like it's trying to break free from Luo Zheng's hand!"

"Troublesome!"
Luo Zheng grabbed the sword of true intent in his hand tightly. He didn't give it any chance to escape and continued forward!
Before he could even take a few steps, even more true meaning spirit swords shot towards him.
This time, Luo Zheng learned his lesson. He lowered his body and flashed away. With his keen perception, he was able to distinguish the subtle differences between these long swords. Then, his hand reached out like lightning. Without any suspense, the second sword was caught in his hand. The densely packed long swords once again disappeared without a trace.
However, the second sword of true intent was exactly the same as the first one. It still wanted to break free from Luo Zheng's hand.
Why would Luo Zheng care about this?
He clamped the sword of true intent under his armpit and rushed forward again.

Chapter 2006
Mo Po 'er had said that if one caught three true meaning spirit swords, they would pass the test.

If Yu Taibai brought her into a non-attacking area, would she enter the core of Ruo Mu like those rich people, or would she climb up step by step through the God City?

Actually …

If one were to hold on to those long swords in order to grab one true intent spirit sword, by the time one grabbed the sixth or even the seventh true intent spirit sword, one's soul wouldn't be able to withstand the pain. In the end, one would be like the first Real God who failed. He would be kicked out of the array because he fainted.
In order to grab more true intent spirit swords, one had to figure out the technique.
Mo Po 'er had practiced in the array many times before she gradually figured it out. But in such a short time, Luo Zheng seemed to have mastered it.
In the array …
Luo Zheng's figure dashed forward at high speed.
After observing for a while, he realized that there was a "gap" in the path. In other words, there was a blind spot between the fourth and fifth sword.
The blind spot was very narrow. But if one were to stand in the blind spot, the long swords on both sides of the path wouldn't be able to touch Luo Zheng …
So, after he rushed into the area where the fifth sword was, he didn't stop. Instead, he ran on the path and hid in the blind spot between the sixth and fifth sword. After observing for a while in the blind spot, he quickly locked onto the True Meaning Spirit Sword. Then, he found a way to return among the flying long swords. At the same time, he grabbed the fifth true intent spirit sword from the rain of swords!
He went back and forth on the path. The people outside the array looked at him. He only took two steps forward and one step back, and he got a true intent spirit sword.

…
…

This guy's speed didn't decrease but increased instead. It was really too abnormal …

True Gods who had cultivated the True Meaning of Dao to a certain level were not commonly seen even among the wealthy families.

Chapter 2007

It was actually very easy to obtain the first three true meaning spirit swords. However, the fourth to ninth true meaning spirit swords were more difficult.
The difficulty of obtaining the tenth to seventeenth true meaning spirit swords would increase by another level!

To many Real Gods, the probability of actually participating in the Dao Contest was too low. Most of them were just here to try their luck, exchange Sanskrit, or enter the Ruo Mu to experience. It was also very beneficial.

This person that the City Lord had brought was not on the same level as them.

Mo Po 'er glanced at Mo Dian, then said lightly, "Elder Dian, are you still asleep? It's not like you don't know about this damned array. After seventeen swords, the swords on that path are mixed with Crimson Fire Thunder. Although this thing can't cause any damage to the soul, the pain is unbearable even for a Perfect Perfection … "

In the history of this Sword God City, there were three Real Gods who had seized more than seventeen swords.
Two of them had seized eighteen swords, while the other had seized twenty.

As for those who had seized twenty-three swords, not a single one had appeared.

Luo Zheng charged forward again.
When he was halfway through this path, he already felt that something was amiss.
The rain of swords on both sides of this path contained traces of crimson lightning, and the speed of these swords actually increased.
"Pu!"
Before he could charge into the first blind spot, he was struck by a Sword of True Intent.

In front of this pain, his strong willpower seemed to become a joke.
Even so, Luo Zheng still endured the intense pain and escaped into the blind spot, his body trembling uncontrollably.
"What the hell …"

He originally wanted to take all the Sword of True Intent in one go, but now he understood that this wasn't an easy task.
If he had been pierced through three times by those red swords, he was afraid that he would have fainted straight away …

Luo Zheng also knew that he had already done enough. He had already far exceeded the requirements of climbing up to Ruo Mu.

Chapter 2008
With Luo Zheng's current speed, it was impossible for him to completely avoid this rain of swords.

The other thing he could rely on was the True Intent of Nature.
Originally, he didn't want to use the True Intent of Dao now.
But since he had already reached this step, he didn't plan on giving up.
With that thought, Luo Zheng started to gather his strength. At the same time, a tiny wisp of the True Intent of Dao started to slowly revolve around his body …
The True Intent of Dao wasn't a cultivation technique. It was just a kind of deep understanding of the Dao.
When this deep understanding was combined with other cultivation techniques, it would naturally produce miraculous effects.
Luo Zheng had already tested this point.

"Everyone get down! It's dangerous! "

"Xiu xiu xiu xiu xiu …"

Only during the Dao Battle would the Sword Race send three white-robed experts to set up a temporary formation to select True Gods.

He stood at the edge of the formation in a daze.

Chapter 2009
That Wang Family's eldest young master twitched on the ground for a while before he recovered.

He wanted to argue with Luo Zheng but was stopped by Mo Po 'er.
"Blame this on my Mingsword God City. If you want to blame someone, you can only blame this formation for being careless," Mo Po 'er said lightly.
Facing this acting City Lord, Second Young Master Wang was very helpless. He could only glare at Luo Zheng hatefully before turning his head away.
After sending the two Wang Family's young masters away, Mo Po 'er smiled at Luo Zheng and said, "Congratulations, you are the first true god in the history of Mingsword God City to obtain all the swords."
"Is there a reward?" Luo Zheng stared at Mo Po 'er and asked.
"Hehe," Mo Po 'er's face revealed a smug expression. "Of course there is no reward, but this means that you can go further than Ruo Mu! However … I will send a letter up and list me as your referral. I believe the elders of the Sword race will pay extra attention to you … "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng wasn't disappointed.
In his environment, he had to grab every opportunity.
"Then can I get close to Ruo Mu now?" Luo Zheng looked at the west gate not far away.
Mo Po 'er opened up a path and extended her arms. "Please!"
"Rumble …"
One of the white robed true gods opened a crack in the west gate.
Luo Zheng nodded at Mo Po 'er and Mo Dian before walking towards the west gate …
"City Lord, why don't you investigate his background and identity?" Mo Dian looked at Luo Zheng's disappearing figure in confusion.
Mo Po 'er smiled and said, "This kind of person is destined to have an extraordinary background. I bet he is most likely a direct descendant of a saint, or at least … a secondary saint's descendant."
"A descendant of a saint or secondary saint." Elder Dian didn't understand. "If that's the case, why didn't he just come through the floating island? Using our floating island will save us a lot of trouble … "
"It's hard to say." Mo Po 'er shook her head and speculated. "Not all the Saints are on the floating island. Perhaps he has his own difficulties. When the selection is over, I will naturally go and take a look."
After passing through the gate of the Famous Sword God City.
A wide coastline appeared in front of Luo Zheng. Some unknown seabirds were resting on the beach. Probably because they lacked natural predators, they didn't hide when they saw Luo Zheng.
A tendril thousands of feet wide extended all the way to the seaside. This tendril was like the claws and teeth of a giant beast. It was like a path leading to the sky.
After observing for a while, Luo Zheng strolled towards the rhizome.
The moment Luo Zheng stepped on this root, he felt an extremely strong life aura. This aura could almost contain all living things, and even infect all living things on the tree.
This root was hundreds of thousands of miles long, and one couldn't see the end of it.
"I need to speed up …"
Then, he sped up and ran forward.
When Luo Zheng came into contact with the huge Ruo Mu, a gentle rhythm was transmitted to him from under his feet.
This trace of rhythm continued to move forward and forward, as if it was a meridian in the human body. It extended towards one of Ruo Mu's branches. After making a turn in this branch, this trace of rhythm converged on Ruo Mu's secondary trunk.
It continued to extend along the secondary trunk and finally reached the main trunk of the Ruo Mu!
In a breath's time, this rhythm extended for a distance of 70 million miles …
And in the center of the Ruo Mu's main trunk, there was a tree hole thousands of feet wide. In the depths of this tree hole, a skeleton sat holding a gray longsword.
The moment that rhythm was transmitted, a faint light suddenly appeared in the skeleton's empty eye sockets.
This light only existed for an instant before it disappeared ….
…
…
North of Famed Sword God City, 130,000 miles away, in Swordforge God City.
In front of the array used to filter True Gods, True Gods were also lined up in a long line. And because Swordforge God City's location was convenient, there were naturally more True Gods here!
Today, most of the True Gods had a trace of reverence on their faces.
The one taking the test in the array was a young girl in purple clothes.
This young girl stood on her tiptoes, and with an incomparably graceful gait, she slowly walked forward in the array. Beside her, there were already 16 True Meaning spirit swords floating!
The young girl's performance was indeed very dazzling, especially with her beautiful figure and face. Any True God couldn't help but take a few more glances.
But these True Gods weren't in awe of her, but rather the youth standing beside the array.
"A grand Perfection True God is actually bringing someone to take the test. How unreasonable!"
"That little girl is also amazing. In just a short while, she picked up 16 True Meaning spirit swords!"
"Shh … do you know who he is?"
"It's said that he's the master of Buzhou Spirit Mountain, Yu Taibai!"
Yu Taibai looked indifferently at Luo Yan in the array, and a trace of emotion appeared in his heart.
In just a few years, she had already been reborn, and her performance had greatly exceeded his expectations.
In a short while, Luo Yan picked up 17 True Meaning spirit swords.
That incomparably beautiful face had a leisurely smile as she walked out of the array.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The 17 True Meaning spirit swords surrounding her slowly returned to their original positions.
"I passed!"
With a relaxed expression, Luo Yan skipped over to Yu Taibai.
"Do you still have energy left?" Yu Taibai asked with a smile.
Luo Yan tilted her head and thought for a moment, then curled her lips and said, "En, I should be able to get all of the remaining swords, but I'll have to suffer a bit …"
Hearing this, the True Gods who were still in line were instantly speechless.
Yu Taibai only smiled. He knew that Luo Yan was speaking the truth.
"Eldest Senior Brother, you promised that Big Brother would definitely come!" Luo Yan stared at Yu Taibai and said seriously.
Yu Taibai turned his head and looked at the slowly opening door. Ruo Mu's enormous roots appeared in front of him. "He'll definitely come. Who knows, maybe he's already climbed up Ruo Mu …"
"Then let's go quickly!"
Luo Yan, like a nimble little rabbit, rushed out of the door without consulting anyone.
Yu Taibai's face revealed a trace of worry.
Luo Zheng's absence at Buzhou Spirit Mountain had disrupted Yu Taibai's plans.
Luo Zheng hadn't been able to comprehend the things his master had left behind. Now, all the pressure fell on Luo Yan.
This was like playing chess. Sometimes, one wrong move would lead to many wrong moves. Especially since his master's situation wasn't optimistic, there was no room for any mistakes.
Even if Yu Taibai could calculate all things using the Great Yan Divine Dao, he wouldn't be able to find any clues.

Chapter 2010
Luo Zheng ran on the rhizome for an unknown number of miles.
On both sides of the rhizome was the vast ocean. Sometimes, he could see the faint shadows of other rhizome.
As he went deeper, the surrounding scenery became more and more desolate …
Along the way, he would occasionally meet other Real Gods. They would look at each other and then maintain a hurried pace as they flew along the rhizome.
Seven days later …
The rhizome that was about ten thousand feet wide under his feet converged to an even wider branch.
After reaching the main branch, the number of people from the Sword Tribe gradually increased.
Every few hundred miles, Luo Zheng would meet one or two Real Gods from the Sword Tribe.
These Real Gods from the Sword Tribe sat at the edge of the branch and stabbed their swords into the branch. They closed their eyes and looked as though they were enlightened.
"Is this the unique cultivation method of the Sword Tribe?"
As Luo Zheng ran, he sized up these Real Gods from the Sword Tribe.
They were all in a meditative state and did not pay attention to their surroundings. No one paid attention to Luo Zheng …
He continued to travel for another four days.
Luo Zheng saw an even wider secondary branch.
"No wonder this non-attacking place is called the Ruo Mu Region. Just this Ruo Mu alone is so huge that it is boundless. I have been running at this speed for more than ten days and I still haven't reached the main branch …"
This time, the length of the main branch was even more terrifying. As he continued to fly, he spent almost half a month's time.
One month later.
Luo Zheng finally reached the end of the secondary branch. What appeared in front of him was a brown plain …
On the brown plain, there were countless sword-shaped buildings. As far as the eye could see, it was still boundless.
"How huge is this Ruo Mu …"
Luo Zheng felt that his imagination wasn't enough for him.
He ran for more than a month before he reached the main branch of the Ruo Mu. Looking at the main branch now, it was boundless and its size was beyond imagination.
Not far ahead, there were two people from the Sword Tribe in white robes. These two people from the Sword Tribe were upper-level Real Gods.
Some Real Gods were queuing up to receive something from these two people from the Sword Tribe.
Just like Luo Zheng, these True Gods had spent more than a month to get here after passing the first round of screening.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng silently followed behind these True Gods.
After a while, it was Luo Zheng's turn.
One of the members of the Sword Clan sized up Luo Zheng. Then, he took out a yellow longsword and handed it to Luo Zheng. "Those who enter our Sword Realm must always hold this token. If you throw it away or leave your body, the Sword Realm will sense it and expel you immediately. Remember this!"
This place was the core of the Sword Race, and the True Gods that participated in the Dao Struggle came from all over the Divine Region. There were all sorts of people, and the Sword Race was naturally much stricter.
After Luo Zheng received the token, the man from the Sword Tribe pointed at a spacious building in the distance and said, "Gather your group of True Gods there and wait for our Sword Tribe to receive you."
Luo Zheng nodded. Then, he walked towards the spacious building. As he walked, he scrutinized it.
There wasn't any information recorded on the token. There was only a simple number. The number he received was 9400.
"There are less than ten thousand True Gods that passed the first round of screening?"
In Luo Zheng's mind, the famous long queues in Sword God City appeared.
As long as one could obtain three Sword of True Intent, they would pass. He didn't expect the probability of passing to be so low.
However, he had left the Sword God City for more than a month. He guessed that more True Gods had passed the first round of screening and were still traveling day and night on the tree branches.
Luo Zheng held the token and entered the spacious building. He saw many True Gods sitting or standing in the hall.
The moment he stepped in, these True Gods' gazes swept past Luo Zheng.
To be able to reach this step, there was simply no lower True Divinity.
Although the first round of screening had nothing to do with cultivation, the stronger one's talent, the sharper one's senses, and the higher the chance of passing.
In this room, ninety percent of the people had the cultivation of an Upper True God.
Hence, when they looked at Luo Zheng, they were more or less surprised.
Luo Zheng knew what these people were thinking. He met their gazes and found a corner in the hall. Then, he leaned against it and waited quietly.
Just as he stood still, a voice came from beside him. "Hey, kid! How did you pass the first round of screening? "
The person who spoke was a dark-skinned man with a black face. This man squinted his eyes and looked at Luo Zheng with interest. He was also interested in the only Lower True God.
"Luck," Luo Zheng replied briefly.
"Hehe, I'm afraid it's not as simple as luck, right?" The dark-skinned man laughed. "Tell me, how many swords did you obtain?"
"I can't tell you," Luo Zheng shook his head.
Hearing Luo Zheng's stiff tone, the dark-skinned man looked unhappy. His face darkened and he said, "In the first round of screening, ninety-nine percent of the people can be eliminated. In the second round, ninety-nine percent will be eliminated. You may be lucky once, but I'm afraid you won't be lucky twice. If you really pass by luck, I'm afraid you'll be the first to be eliminated!"
Luo Zheng shrugged and didn't say anything else.
For ten to ten hours, the people from Sword Tribe didn't arrive.
Luckily, these True Gods were very calm. Occasionally, they would hear some whispers. Most of them closed their eyes and ignored everything that was happening around them.
Outside the hall, True Gods who had received their tokens gathered one after another.
Although the hall was spacious, after thousands of people had gathered, the space seemed to be cramped.
About twenty hours later, on the other side of the hall, an elder in a white robe finally appeared.
The moment the elder in a white robe appeared, he attracted everyone's attention.
"Great Perfection!"
"It's a Great Perfection True God from Sword Tribe!"
"…"
There were very few Great Perfection True Gods in the Divine Territory.
Even among the first-tier families, there weren't many who had Great Perfection True Gods.
However, Sword Tribe was ranked second among the wealthy families in the Divine Territory. It wasn't strange for a Great Perfection True God to appear in Ruomu Region.
"Thank you, everyone, for coming to Ruomu Region from thousands of miles away. Sorry for making everyone wait," the elder said with cupped hands.
This Great Perfection True God was very polite, but it made the group of True Gods feel awkward.
"To be able to pass the first round of screening, you can say that you're very sensitive to True Intent Spirit Swords." The elder continued, "However, the quota for Sword Tribe is too limited. Therefore, according to tradition, we must hold a second round of screening! If anyone of you can pass the second round of screening, then you'll be indispensable in this round's Dao Battle! "
Hearing the elder's words, the True Gods' eyes instantly lit up.
Most of these True Gods were elites from first-tier and second-tier families …
If they participated in the Dao Battle, they would be able to compete with those elites from wealthy families. They might even be able to comprehend their own True Intent of Dao!

Chapter 2011
There were very few True Gods who had seen the True Intent of Dao in the Divine Territory.
However, there were many legends regarding the True Intent of Dao!
Especially the True Gods who came to participate in the Dao Battle and passed the first round of selection. They were even more fascinated by it.
These True Gods were originally extremely talented people. However, due to various reasons, their cultivation had been stuck at the True God Realm.
Even for a wealthy family, it was extremely difficult to nurture a Paragon. It required a large amount of resources. If they did not have other opportunities, they would probably never be able to step into the Paragon Realm …
However, that illusory True Intent of Dao became their life-saving straw!
It was said that the True Intent of Dao was the great comprehension after standing at the end of the Divine Dao. That was the ultimate profound meaning of this world. If one could completely comprehend the True Intent of Dao, they could directly cross the Divine Dao and reach the other shore.
All kinds of legends and versions circulated around these people.
Later on, there were many different opinions as to what the True Intent of Dao was used for.
Some True Gods by chance really deciphered a few lines of the True Intent mental cultivation method. Although it was only one or two lines and was a remnant of the remnant, when recited according to the mental cultivation method, one could really feel all kinds of wonders and be fascinated.
However, just a few lines of the True Intent mental cultivation method, how powerful could it be? Those remnant lines merely led these True Gods to catch a glimpse of the end of the Divine Dao. It could not give them any substantial help, but it exuded a fatal temptation.
If the non-offensive areas in the Wood Domain truly existed, then it would be a complete proof of the True Meaning of the Dao.
Whether it was a Sage or a Paragon, they could not avoid the True Intent of Dao …
It was also because of this that the Wood Region became the place that this group of True Gods yearned for the most. It was also because of this that the Sword Race took advantage of this and announced a Dao Battle every few years!
Many True Gods who had studied Sanskrit would participate in every Dao Battle. However, most of them could not even pass the first round of selection.
As for the second round of selection, the number of people eliminated would be even greater …
There were tens of thousands of True Gods in this hall. Those who could pass were probably only a few people.
"However, there is one thing that this old man still has to remind everyone. In the second round of selection, our Sword Race had no choice but to use some methods. Naturally, there is a certain amount of danger. Some True Gods who have participated in the Dao Battle before should know," the Sword Race elder said lightly.
Hearing this, these True Gods' hearts suddenly trembled.
Although this was a non-offensive area, it did not mean that it was absolutely safe.
The Sword Race had occupied Ruo Mu for so many years. Their understanding of non-offensive lands was much deeper than what the outside world could imagine!
There were many examples of people dying in Daoist battles in the past …
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly as if he had understood something. He thought to himself, "A non-attacking place means that you can't attack, but it doesn't mean that you can't kill people."
Just like how he had captured those true intent spirit swords in Famed Sword God City, but when those true intent spirit swords were repelled, they could still injure the eldest young master of the Wang Clan!
"If that's the case, then the True Meaning of the Dao in the non-offensive area should be suppressing the desire to kill. In other words, it's impossible for me to kill someone unless I can break free from the True Meaning of the Dao in the non-offensive area. However, that doesn't mean that there's no way to kill someone …"
While Luo Zheng was deep in thought, the old man from the Sword Race said, "Everyone, please follow me."
With that, he took the lead and walked out of the hall.
The True Gods also followed behind in a hustle and bustle.
The black-faced man walked in front of Luo Zheng and turned his head to look at Luo Zheng with a mischievous smile. "With one look, I can tell that you're a rookie participating in the selection for the first time. What Elder Lu said just now was not a joke. If you don't pay attention to the second round of selection, you'll die. It's not too late for you to withdraw now!"
He deliberately dragged out his words to scare Luo Zheng.
This was the seventh time this black-faced man had participated in the Dao Battle, and the fifth time he had entered the second round of selection. It could be said that he was very familiar with the Sword Race. It was a pity that he had not been able to participate in the Dao Battle seriously even once. He was rather regretful about this and felt extremely unbalanced in his heart.
Every time he met a rookie like Luo Zheng who had a low cultivation level and was participating in the Dao Battle for the first time, he would ruthlessly attack him to satisfy the unbalanced feeling in his heart.
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng did not care about his words at all. Instead, he asked, "Elder Lu? Shouldn't this Great Perfection Stage Sword Race's surname be Mo? "
"Yes, most people in the Sword Race have the surname Mo. However, after making great contributions to the Sword Race, or cultivating to the Great Perfection Stage, or some core members of the Sword Race, they all have their own titles. This Elder Lu is naturally called 'Sword Slayer,'" the black-faced man could not help but explain.
"Oh, thank you." Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
"What are you talking about! I'm telling you that if you don't withdraw from the second round of selection, you're dead meat … "The black-faced man realized that he had been led astray by Luo Zheng and said angrily.
Luo Zheng only rolled his eyes and ignored this person's existence again.
"Sword Slayer …"
I see.
Speaking of which, Luo Zheng still had the skeleton of a Great Perfection Stage Sword Race member in the imperial jade seal that he had not returned to the Sword Race.
The owner of this skeleton was called 'Sword Attendant' …
If he had the chance, Luo Zheng would definitely return the skeleton of the Sword Attendant to the Sword Race.
How could the black-faced man know that Luo Zheng was thinking about this?
Although Luo Zheng did not pay attention to him, he kept whispering in Luo Zheng's ear. For some unknown reason, he kept exerting pressure on Luo Zheng.
"Why do you talk so much nonsense!"
All of a sudden, Luo Zheng frowned and reprimanded loudly.
The crowd that had been quietly moving forward suddenly stopped and focused their gazes on Luo Zheng and the black-faced man.
Even the old man leading the way, 'Sword Slayer', frowned and turned his head back. He only said lightly, "Don't make a noise."
Luo Zheng shrugged and did not speak anymore.
On the contrary, the black-faced man's face was really dark with a hint of red.
If they were outside of this place, he was afraid that he would directly kill Luo Zheng. But here, just as the killing intent formed in his heart, it was completely erased by the invisible momentum of this place.
In the end, he could only glare fiercely at Luo Zheng and did not say anything.
Under the guidance of 'Sword Slayer', the crowd saw a downward slope appear in front of them. Following this slope all the way down, there was a dark entrance.
Standing at the entrance, 'Sword Slayer said lightly, "This is the place where our Sword Race cultivates. Everyone knows that our Sword Race is benevolent, which is why we were able to build this place. But they don't know that our Sword Race can also sacrifice our lives … This place is a place of absolute death."
The True Meaning of Dao that shrouded the entire place was extremely extreme.
In most areas, the True Gods could not even fight with each other under the cover of this momentum.

Chapter 2012
The Sword Race was a relatively peaceful race in the Divine Dominion.
At least, this race rarely caused any disputes in the Divine Dominion.
Even as the second most powerful clan in the Divine Dominion, their presence was not strong. This was because of their race's way of being benevolent.
Not killing indiscriminately and not being addicted to killing was a state of mind that almost every Sword Race member had to cultivate. It could be said that the Sword Race members had a strong sense of justice.
However, those who truly understood the Sword Race understood the terrifying power hidden behind this race.
"Even our Sword Race members often die when cultivating in the Death Trap. Everyone, don't try to be brave in there. If you can't withstand it, immediately crush the sword token in your hand! Our Sword Race members will save you. "Jian Lu glanced at everyone and waved his hand." Remember, the Death Trap will ignite the killing intent in your heart. However, you must not kill each other. Otherwise, don't blame the Sword Race for being ruthless. Everyone, please! "
With a response, this large group of True Gods went straight into the Death Trap …
The black-faced man glanced coldly at Luo Zheng and went in as well.
Luo Zheng strolled leisurely and walked at the back.
When Jian Lu saw that everyone had entered the Death Trap, in the sky, a small emerald bird flew over and circled towards him.
There was a small jade slip hanging on the bird's legs.
Jian Lu reached out his hand and the small emerald bird landed on his old finger.
"Chirp chirp!"
The small emerald bird chirped twice.
Jian Lu removed the jade slip and revealed a puzzled expression. "Mo Po 'er's letter? That kid just became the City Lord of Sword God City and stopped causing trouble. He stopped for a few days. What's with this letter? "
"Crack."
Jian Lu reached out and crushed the jade slip. A strand of consciousness flowed out from the jade slip.
"What? Someone in Sword God City took 23 swords? "
It was very rare to be able to take 23 swords, even in the Sword Race.
"Surname Luo, name Zheng, Luo Zheng …"
Jian Lu muttered for a while.
"Where have I heard this name before …"
After a short while, Jian Lu suddenly opened his eyes. There was a trace of surprise in his eyes.
"That brat with Sword Token No.9400!"
"Luo Zheng … He's the one who stirred up the floating island!"
As a Great Perfection Stage expert of the Sword Race and one of the Seven Great Sword-Bearing Elders, Sword Killer did not move around the floating island often. Instead, he stayed in Ruo Mu.
However, news of what happened on the floating island, especially major events, would often be sent back.
After hearing this name, he recalled what had happened during the ranking of the floating islands …
Anonymous cultivator, Forbidden Land of Time, Imperial Jade Seal.
One of the seven Sword-Bearing Elders of the Sword Race, the Sword Attendant, had died in the imperial jade seal!
Because of this, the Sword Maniac and the Sword Maniac lost their temper and demanded to track down Luo Zheng's whereabouts.
The fallen members of the Sword Clan had to return to their clan for burial even if they were billions of miles away.
Especially Sword Attendants at the Consummate Realm. They absolutely couldn't be buried in a foreign land …
Most importantly, the death of the Sword Attendant was only Luo Zheng's side of the story. How could the Sword Tribe allow a Sword Bearing Elder to die so casually?
Looking at the pitch-black entrance, Sword Killer even had a slight impulse to drag Luo Zheng out and ask him clearly.
However, he restrained himself in the end.
The Dao Battle this time was different from the past. Everything was for the sake of the Sword Race.
…
The moment Luo Zheng stepped into the Killing Spot, he felt a drastic change in his aura.
Previously, when Luo Zheng entered the non-attacking place, he felt that the non-attacking place gave out a huge aura. It was the irresistible aura that caused all the Real Gods in the non-attacking place to be unable to gather the slightest bit of murderous intention.
However, after he entered the Killing Spot, this aura still existed. However, the pressure that Luo Zheng felt was not suppressing his killing intent, but releasing it!
An intense desire for battle was ignited, and it flowed continuously through Luo Zheng's blood.
"What a huge contrast!"
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
Under the influence of this aura, Luo Zheng faintly felt an impulse in his heart.
He actually wanted to burn his life force and erupt with his strongest strength, sacrificing himself!
"Is this the other side of the True Meaning of Dao?"
This feeling was somewhat similar to when Luo Zheng used the Emotion Severing Divine Path, but it was fundamentally different.
It seemed to be of the same origin as the God Slaying Divine Path, but there was still a huge difference.
The most important thing was that this True Meaning of Dao actually fused two completely opposite auras. It was indeed very strange.
As he pondered, Luo Zheng slowly walked down the stairs in the tree hole …
This tree hole was the interior of Ruo Mu's main pole.
The cross section of Ruo Mu's main pole was incomparably huge, so the world inside must be unimaginably huge. Could it be that the entire core of Ruo Mu was a Killing Spot?
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
The True Gods continuously flew along the wide tree hole, and the True Gods quickly distanced themselves from each other.
Luo Zheng leisurely walked for a short distance, then he quietly sped up …
At the beginning of the journey, there were no changes.
However, as the group ventured deeper, tiny vines began to appear around the tree hole.
"Be careful, don't get wrapped up by these vines!"
"This is Ruo Mu's spiritual root. If you get wrapped up, don't even think about escaping!"
Just as a True God issued a warning, "Swish, swish" sounds could be heard continuously.
This huge cave was like the stomach of a monster. All the vines waved at the same time, sweeping towards the True Gods.
If the living beings of the God Realm were to be ranked, the four Divine Trees would definitely be the top living beings in the God Realm.
However, among the four Divine Trees, only the Northstar Divine Tree was adept at transformation and could transform into a death zone. The other three Divine Trees would not take the initiative to attack living beings.
Especially Ruo Mu.
Cultivating on Ruo Mu, one could feel the strong vitality of Ruo Mu. Under its nourishment, one could greatly increase one's cultivation speed.
Thus, as Luo Zheng walked, he saw many members of the Sword Clan stabbing their swords into Ruo Mu to cultivate.
However, in this Killing Spot, a part of Ruo Mu was also affected by the True Meaning of Dao. This area naturally became extremely dangerous.
Countless vines spread out at an extremely fast speed, and in an instant, they wrapped around many True Gods!
Swish! Swish!
"Ah …"
Not far in front of Luo Zheng, an upper-level True God's leg was wrapped up by vines. In an instant, seven or eight vines tightly wrapped around him. No matter how he struggled, he could not get out of these vines.
Moreover, the more this upper-level True God struggled, the stronger the vines became. Soon, a crisp sound could be heard coming from the True God's body. Even his bones were being twisted and broken!
"Crack!"
Helpless, this upper-level True God could only break the sword token in his hand. He did not dare to move in the slightest. When he gave up resisting, the vines no longer increased their strength.

Chapter 2013
Almost every True God had been attacked by these vines, and Luo Zheng was no exception.
These vines were like agile snakes. As they wriggled, they swarmed toward Luo Zheng.
Whoosh! “
Luo Zheng could not fly like the other Upper True Gods, so he could only rely on his agility to avoid them.
He twisted his body slightly and shot toward the side of the cave. He was as agile as a rabbit.
However, these vines seemed to have eyes. They moved quickly in the direction Luo Zheng had dodged. They intertwined with each other and formed a net that blocked Luo Zheng's path.
Whoosh, whoosh.
At the same time, seven or eight more vines extended from the ground behind Luo Zheng, blocking his path of retreat.
"Hehe, I've already said that Lower True Gods are just looking for death by coming here," the black-faced man said with a cold smile as he stood in mid-air and stared at Luo Zheng.
There were no restrictions in the Killing Grounds. Upper True Gods naturally had a great advantage.
Not only were they far stronger than ordinary Middle True Gods and Lower True Gods, but more importantly, they could fly freely and were more agile.
Therefore, in the second round of selection, ninety percent of the True Gods were Upper True Gods. There were only a few Middle True Gods.
As for Lower True Gods, Luo Zheng was the only one.
Hearing the black-faced man's words, Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all. With a tap of his foot, he turned into a sharp arrow and shot forward.
"You're walking right into the trap?"
Seeing this, the black-faced man almost thought that Luo Zheng had given up.
These vines were Ruo Mu's spiritual roots and were almost indestructible. At least in the many Path Battles that the black-faced man had participated in, being entangled by the vines meant being eliminated. No True God had been able to cut off the vines with their own strength.
There had been a True God who had not crushed his sword token to seek help from the Sword Tribe after being entangled by these vines. In the end, he had been strangled to death by the vines!
Therefore, many True Gods would crush their sword tokens once they were entangled by the vines and give up on the second round of selection.
There would be more opportunities to fight for the Dao in the future, but there was only one life.
However, just as the net made of vines was about to envelop Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly became blurry. At the same time, a unique aura spread out from his body!
"Whoosh …"
Then, the black-faced man saw Luo Zheng's body suddenly become soft. His body twisted into an indescribable shape and "flowed" through the gaps between the vines!

Chapter 2019

The elites from various wealthy and powerful families, as well as the upper-level Real Gods who were selected from the grassroots Real Gods, had all gathered there.
Most of the upper True Gods had a trace of uncontrollable excitement in their eyes.
Under Yu Taibai's lead, Luo Zheng and Luo Yan also stepped into the spacious main hall.
The moment the three of them entered, countless gazes were cast toward them. The originally noisy environment in the hall suddenly quieted down.
Most of the participants in the hall presumably knew Yu Taibai. Of course, they also knew Luo Zheng …
Nan Longhua stood at the edge of the hall. She was originally chatting with several other upper True Gods. When she saw Luo Yan, her eyes suddenly lit up. "Look, the extraordinary woman I was talking about, it's her! I don't know which first-tier family she's from. If it wasn't for her, I'm afraid I wouldn't have been able to pass the second round of selection. I'll go over and say hello first! Hehe! "

Chapter 2023
The True Gods in this hall were seated according to their order. Yan * Qing * Fu *
The Sword Tribe did not welcome Sages from other wealthy families to Ruomu Region.
Therefore, the wealthy families only sent Great Perfection Sages to escort their elite disciples.
Those gathered on the steps of the hall were all wealthy families' disciples. There were about 200 upper True Gods and a few Great Perfection Sages among them.
Below the steps, there were more than 100 upper True Gods.
Most of these 100 upper True Gods came from first-tier families. Many families had Great Perfection Sages or even Quasi-Sages. They were considered overlords in the Divine Realm.
However, in the eyes of the wealthy families' disciples, they were still considered grassroots.
