"The two of you, we meet again!" Nan Longhua said with a smile.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly at him. His eyes were fixed on the stairs.
There were many familiar faces on the steps …
For example, Dongfang Taiqing, Mu Xuerong, and Han Tianxiao.
Nan Longhua noticed Luo Zheng's gaze and thought that Luo Zheng was going to run up the steps. She hurriedly said, "Our seats are here. The seats above are for the wealthy families …"
Before he could finish his sentence, a clear, crisp, and cold voice rang out.
"What a coincidence. You've been hiding for two years. If you have the ability, then hide like a turtle for the rest of your life!"
Mu Xuerong was a Great Perfection Sages, but she could not directly participate in the Dao Battle. This time, she had come through the floating island and was wearing a light red palace dress.
Although her attire lacked some experience and murderous aura, this light red palace dress added a bit of bright and moving color …
However, the upper True Gods in the hall more or less knew that Mu Xuerong had a bad temper and was not to be trifled with. They only stole a few glances before looking away.
They hadn't expected that after the three of them entered the palace, Mu Xuerong would use a cold tone to make things difficult for them. They didn't know who these people were, but they actually dared to provoke the Mu family, and even dared to provoke the Mu family's Paragon?
Nan Longhua turned and saw Mu Xuerong walking down leisurely. She was shocked.
When she had first entered the hall, she did not recognize Mu Xuerong. It was only through the whispers of others that Nan Longhua knew this woman was the famous Mu Xuerong.
Nan Longhua's expression was very ugly. She asked in a low voice, "How did you provoke the Mu family?"
"It's none of your business," Luo Zheng reminded him lightly.
Nan Long Hua's mouth twitched. Although it would be shameful to retreat at this time, he knew that he could not afford to offend those people on the platform. If he didn't deal with them properly, it would bring disaster to the Nan Long Family. Therefore, he could only obediently retreat to the side.
Luo Zheng's gaze met Mu Xuerong's. He didn't say anything, but he stretched out his hand and made a grasping motion towards Mu Xuerong's face.
This action caused many people to be baffled …
However, Dongfang Taiqing, Tang Wan, and Fang Henshao understood the meaning behind this action.
When Luo Zheng had lifted Mu Xuerong's chin and said those words, it was probably the greatest humiliation Mu Xuerong had ever suffered in her life.
Even when Mu Xuerong had been repeatedly defeated by Yu Taibai in the Arena of Gods, she had never been so angry!
After being released from the Imperial Jade Seal by Luo Zheng, Mu Xuerong had been depressed for a long time before she was able to pull herself together.
"You!"
The blood vessels on Mu Xuerong's snow-white neck rapidly surged with blood.
Her beautiful face began to contort.
A strong killing intent gathered in her heart, but under the influence of the True Meaning of Benevolence in the non-attacking area, her killing intent quickly dissipated.
This process made Mu Xuerong feel extremely uncomfortable!
"What is the meaning of this?"
"How could a single action make Mu Xuerong so angry?"
Whether it was the upper True Divinities from the great clans or the lower True Divinities from the great clans, they were all whispering to each other.
This was especially true for Nan Long Hua. He thought that Luo Zheng was a mere lower True Divinity. In front of Mu Xuerong, he was as insignificant as an insect.
How could a single action make Mu Xuerong so angry?
Although the disciples of the great clans had more or less heard of what had happened in the Sea of Time, what had happened in the Imperial Jade Seal had not spread. They only knew that Luo Zheng had taken control of the Imperial Jade Seal and captured all the Paragons in one fell swoop. They didn't know what had happened in the Imperial Jade Seal.
After a while …
Mu Xuerong's emotions finally calmed down.
She raised her head and sneered, "Sooner or later, you will fall into my hands! At that time, I will cripple you and lock you up with my stupid little sister. I will torture you in front of her and make her understand what reality is. "
"Mu Ning? She's been locked up? "Luo Zheng frowned.
Mu Xuerong snorted and didn't answer Luo Zheng's question.
She didn't want to use this matter to provoke Luo Zheng. After all, in her eyes, it was a family scandal that shouldn't be spread.
Mu Xuerong was very disappointed with what had happened to Mu Ning in the Sea of Time. After she saw through Mu Ning's thoughts, she became even more furious.
She personally ordered Mu Ning to be locked up. Even now, Mu Ning was not allowed to take a single step out.
However, Mu Ning's personality was just as stubborn as her older sister. Even now, she refused to lower her head and admit defeat.
Mu Xuerong's gaze slowly shifted to Yu Taibai. She coldly said, "Your calculations are quite good. Unfortunately, you are destined to lose this time. Have you calculated your own fate?"
Yu Taibai's expression was as calm as water.
Ceng Jingbai Yu Taibai's strength was at the top of the Perfect True Gods.
Among the Perfect True Gods of the rich and powerful families, the only one qualified to fight him was Dongfang Taiqing.
Now that he had complete control of Buzhou Spirit Mountain, Yu Taibai's strength had increased by another level. He had completely pulled away from the Perfect True Gods and was able to fight against a Saint.
"Lose?" Yu Taibai only calmly asked, "How many times have you lost to me? I can't remember. Do you still remember?"
Hearing this, Mu Xuerong's gaze darkened. Then she thought of something and sneered, "Then I wish you success this time." Then she retreated to the steps.
On the steps, the upper True Gods of the Mu family, Dongfang family, Tang family, Fang family, and other families all looked over with hostile gazes.
Luo Yan followed behind Luo Zheng very quietly.
She was no longer that ignorant girl. She knew that her brother had almost died at the hands of this woman. She also knew that the other party had made ample preparations for this Path Battle …
When Yu Taibai brought Luo Zheng and Luo Yan up the steps and found a seat for the Luo family, Luo Yan suddenly leaned over and whispered, "Brother, during the Path Battle, I'm afraid that this group of people will target you."
Luo Zheng nodded and smiled, "Of course."
It wasn't the first time he had been targeted. It could be said that it had long become a habit.
"Yan 'er will protect big brother," Luo Yan said with a serious face, "Yan' er has the strength now. When we enter the killing zone, big brother will follow me. If anyone dares to make a move, Yan 'er will … kill them all."
A wisp of an unquestionable expression appeared on her pure, white, and gentle face.

Chapter 2024
Ever since Luo Zheng entered the hall, the atmosphere in the hall had changed.
More than half of the upper True Gods in the hall were participating in the Dao Battle for the first time. Similarly, they didn't think that the Dao Battle this time would be any different.
However, some of the insiders seemed to have guessed something when they saw Yu Taibai appear …
Perhaps the Dao Battle this time really was a little different!
The strange atmosphere in the hall lasted for a while until Jian Wuhen led the Sword Tribe members into the hall.
Jian Wuhen had a faint smile on his face. After entering the hall, his gaze landed on Dongfang Taiqing.
Then, he smiled slightly. "I'm sorry, but the quota given to the wealthy families by the Sword Tribe this time … will be reduced by half!"
"What?!"
The faces of the Perfected True Gods suddenly changed.
The upper True Gods were dumbfounded …
Especially Dongfang Taiqing. His eyes shone brightly as though he wanted to use his gaze to pierce through Jian Wuhen.
"According to the agreement, the Sword Tribe has always given each wealthy family six quotas. What do you mean by reducing it by half?" Dongfang Taiqing said coldly.
Dongfang Taiqing had a calm temperament. Naturally, he wouldn't be like Mu Xuerong, who grumpily argued with Luo Zheng and Yu Taibai. He was someone who only looked at the results.
He knew very well what this Dao Battle meant …
In this Dao Battle, what the Dongfang Family couldn't obtain, Yu Taibai wouldn't be able to either.
Therefore, the upper True Gods he brought were the top experts of the Dongfang Family!
The goal of these upper True Gods wasn't to obtain the inheritance of that skeleton. Instead, they were trying their best to eliminate all possible targets who could obtain the inheritance.
Naturally, this included Luo Zheng.
Jian Wuhen chuckled and glanced at Dongfang Taiqing indifferently. "What a joke. When did the Sword Tribe make such an agreement with the Dongfang Family? Does it have anything to do with the Tang Family? Does it have anything to do with the Mu Family? "
Jian Wuhen's aggressive questions stumped the group of Great Perfection Sovereigns.
Indeed, the Sword Tribe didn't make any agreements with the wealthy families.
Any True God in the God Territory had the right to participate in the Dao Battle. The Sword Tribe had never rejected anyone. As long as they could pass two tests, that was enough.
However, for some reason, the Sword Tribe suddenly gave the wealthy families many quotas.
Every floating island had six spots, and these spots were all selected through different selections.
However, this was something that the Sword Race had taken the initiative to give to him. It was just that the human heart was like this. After a long time, they began to take it for granted.
However, they had forgotten that the Sword Race was the one who had taken the initiative to give it to them. If they didn't want to give it to them, they naturally wouldn't give it to them …
"To be honest, I won't give you a single spot. Telling you to scram is my duty," said Jian Wuhen with a sneer.
Dongfang Taiqing, Mu Xuerong, and the others frowned …
The members of the Sword Race were generally very amiable. Even the Sages would not fall out with the group of Great Perfection Sovereigns.
Seeing Jian Wuhen's attitude, they understood that the Sword Race had already made their choice and stood on Yu Taibai's side.
Mu Xuerong wanted to say something tough, but Dongfang Taiqing pulled her aside and shook his head.
When one was under the eaves, one had no choice but to lower one's head. So what if Mu Xuerong tried to argue for what was right? If they angered Jian Wuhen and he really didn't give them a single spot, that would be the biggest problem.
The quota for each wealthy family would be halved … They still had quite a number of people, so at least they still had a good chance.
"It's already not bad to have half the spots," said Dongfang Taiqing sullenly.
"Hehe, that's good!"
Jian Wuhen glanced at all the gods in the hall and then said loudly, "Since everyone is participating in the Dao Battle, I'm sure you all understand the dangers of the Killing Grounds. The Dao Battle is the most dangerous Killing Grounds. What's protecting you in the Killing Grounds is not your strength, but your True Intent of the Dao. If you only have a few words of True Intent of the Dao, you can leave now. Don't curse me when you're about to die!"
"No one wants to leave, right?" Jian Wuhen asked again.
Although almost no True God was willing to give up this rare opportunity, the Sword Race still had to ask the same question every time.
After asking, Jian Wuhen waved his hand and said, "Let's go!"
Then, everyone left the hall one after another and arrived at the plaza at the entrance.
After Jian Wuhen left, he clapped his hands lightly.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Flying was prohibited on the Death Lands.
However, that was only for outsiders.
The Death Lands were so huge that it would take ten days to half a month for the Sword Race to run one round. If they were to run from one end to the other, it would take up to half a year.
The Sword Race could use a special flying sword Dharma treasure …
Jian Wuhen clapped his hands and countless flying swords immediately descended.
"Everyone, pick a flying sword. These flying swords don't need to recognize an owner. Just insert your divine sense into them and you'll be able to control them," said Jian Wuhen.
Luo Yan glanced at them and jumped lightly. He stood firmly on one of the flying swords.
Luo Zheng also slowly walked up to a flying sword and injected his divine sense into the sword body …
Buzz!
He felt the flying sword tremble slightly. Then, it gently floated up.
About half of the upper-level True Gods from the wealthy families had to stay.
There was no other reason. Jian Wuhen had prepared so many flying swords that the remaining True Gods couldn't go …
Seeing Jian Wuhen's arrangement, Dongfang Taiqing understood that Jian Wuhen had probably been plotting with Yu Taibai for a long time.
"Gui," Dongfang Taiqing suddenly shouted.
In front of him, a white-haired youth about thirty years old turned around and looked at Dongfang Taiqing.
Dongfang Gui was his younger brother …
However, he was specially selected by Dongfang Chunjun.
There was no other reason. Dongfang Gui was the most outstanding of the upper-level True Gods.
It could be said that Dongfang Gui was the most powerful person below the Great Perfection True Gods!
His goal was to kill the people Yu Taibai had brought with him …
They were Luo Yan and Luo Zheng.
Dongfang Taiqing couldn't guess who Yu Taibai would let inherit the skeleton. Thus, the best way was to kill both of them.
No matter whether Dongfang Gui could kill Luo Zheng or Luo Yan in the end … he would die.
This was probably the last time he would see his younger brother.
"Don't worry." Dongfang Gui's gaze swept past Luo Zheng and his sister. There was no contempt in his eyes. Then, he firmly stepped onto a flying sword.
When all the True Gods were ready on their flying swords, Jian Wuhen stepped on a flying sword and shot forward. At the same time, he left behind a voice, "Follow me!"
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Luo Zheng and the others also stepped onto their flying swords and flew out of Sword Transformation City with Jian Wuhen.

Chapter 2025
The surface area of Ruo Mu's trunk was too wide.
Looking from front to back, they couldn't see the end of it. Everyone sped along on their flying swords as if they were flying on an ordinary plain.
The only difference was that the ground below wasn't grassland, desert, or beach, but grayish-brown Ruo Mu's surface …
Although the Sword Transformation Divine City was the core city of the Sword Race, it wasn't located in the center of Ruo Mu, but a little to the north.
Now, everyone was heading south. After about three to five incense sticks of time, they saw a crack.
This crack was just a crack on Ruo Mu, but in front of everyone, it was like a huge canyon.
Jian Wuhen gently twisted the direction of his sword and began to fly along the crack.
The True Gods followed closely behind …
As they continued forward, the crack became wider and wider. At the same time, more and more cracks appeared in front of everyone.
These cracks intersected with each other and finally formed a huge gap. Only then did Luo Zheng realize what was going on.
This looked like a wound on Ruo Mu … Was it caused by that giant worm?
The huge gap was hundreds of miles wide. Standing on the flying sword and looking down, they couldn't see the bottom.
After a while, Jian Wuhen stopped in the middle of the gap. His words proved Luo Zheng's guess.
"We've reached our destination. Everyone, this is as far as I can send you. Below us is a huge hole with a vertical height of 30 million feet. It's very dangerous to go up. Those who want to give up can turn back now!" Jian Wuhen said indifferently.
None of the True Gods on the flying swords spoke. Only the whistling of the wind could be heard.
Since they had already come this far, no one would choose to give up.
Luo Yan's tall body stood upright on the flying sword. His face was filled with excitement.
Dongfang Gui, as well as the upper True Gods of the Mu Family, Tang Family, Fang Family, and other Wealthy Families, looked at Luo Zheng and Luo Yan darkly …
"Then, everyone can go down." Jian Wuhen's gaze passed through the crowd and looked at Luo Yan and Luo Zheng. He smiled and said, "I wish you good luck."
"Whoosh …"
"Whoosh …"
As the flying swords circled around, they began to descend.
Jian Wuhen watched as the Real Gods became smaller in his vision. Eventually, they turned into small black dots and disappeared into the huge gap.
He waited at the gap for a while, and then another twelve flying swords came from another direction!
These people were all dressed in white. Clearly, they were disciples of the Sword Race. Moreover, they were all upper True Gods.
"Greetings, Lord Wu Hen!"
The leader of the Upper True Gods knelt down on one knee on the longsword. The longsword didn't shake at all. Clearly, his Sword Controlling Technique had been cultivated to the peak.
"Greetings, Lord Wu Hen …"
The other eleven upper true gods also knelt down on one knee.
"You may dispense with the formalities." Jian Wuhen waved his hand gently and said, "The disciples of the various wealthy families have already gone down. It's your turn now. You are the most outstanding disciples of my Sword Race. I hope you won't disappoint me. "
"We will do our best," the leading upper true god said seriously.
"Jian Ao, if you can obtain the inheritance, you must do your best. This may be our last chance." Jian Wuhen continued, "If you can't obtain it, then do your best to assist the siblings Luo Zheng and Luo Yan! You must be most careful of the Wealthy Alliance, especially the Dongfang family … "
"Remember!" the True Gods replied in unison.
Jian Wuhen raised his hand, and streaks of blood light flickered.
They were crimson blood swords. The swords were suffused with a demonic red glow. One look and one could tell that they were some kind of evil sword.
The Sword Race was very particular about using swords. They disdained using evil methods and rarely used such evil swords.
When the True Gods saw these blood swords, their faces all trembled.
"I'll bestow twelve Disaster Swords to you. You can use them at critical times. You know the consequences of using these swords." Jian Wuhen revealed a hint of reluctance.
The Disaster Swords were invented by a consummate stage genius of the Sword Race 30 Divine Epochs ago. Because of this, that consummate stage genius was banished by the Sword Race. All of the Disaster Swords were sealed and forbidden from being used.
These swords were too evil. Using them would result in death. Moreover, they would be tortured to death by all kinds of disasters. It was too inhumane.
However, the Disaster Swords could greatly increase one's strength in a short period of time.
Jian Wuhen understood that this was their last chance, so he had no choice but to unseal the Disaster Swords and bestow them to the upper true gods.
Every upper true god came up to receive a Disaster Sword. They knew that after receiving the sword, they would become the Sword Race's sacrificial warriors. More or less, there was a hint of sorrow on their faces.
"Go." Jian Wuhen turned his head and didn't look at them.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
The twelve upper true gods of the Sword Race also entered the gap.
"However, those who cultivate the Dao of the Sword are righteous and not evil for the sake of …"
Jian Wuhen's voice drifted far away with the wind and reached the ears of the Sword Race disciples …
…
…
After Luo Zheng entered the gap, the surrounding scenery dimmed.
After descending for about ten thousand feet, the true intent of kindness outside Ruo Mu could no longer be transmitted in. Instead, it was replaced by the true intent of evil.
"We've entered the absolute killing zone!"
Luo Zheng instantly felt as if the blood in his body was boiling. A powerful battle intent slowly emerged.
"Mm." Luo Yan nodded slightly.
She closed her eyes slightly and then opened them again.
A faint purple light shone from the depths of her pupils.
Because of this faint purple light, her beautiful face was instantly tainted with a hint of strangeness.
After entering the absolute killing zone, the other true gods also became vigilant. They stepped on their flying swords and slowly descended. At the same time, they spread out their divine senses and carefully observed their surroundings.
Even the absolute killing zone on the surface of Ruo Mu contained considerable danger. Moreover, this was the core of Ruo Mu's core.
The surroundings were quiet …
In this space, there wasn't even any air current.
"Look, a giant worm!"
An upper True God pointed at the cave wall by the side.
A giant worm appeared in everyone's line of sight.
This worm's length couldn't be seen at a glance. Its body was also ridiculously huge.
This vertical cave was already very wide, but this giant worm's fat and bloated body took up almost half of the space.
"This giant worm is sleeping …" a true god said softly.
"Don't be afraid. These giant worms have never woken up. They grow in their sleep and die in their sleep," another true god said.
As they continued descending on their flying swords, a second giant worm lying on the wall appeared in front of everyone's eyes.
As they continued descending, more and more giant worms appeared …
Thirty thousand feet …
Ten thousand feet …
Fifty thousand feet …
Rows of giant worms were sleeping. No one knew how many giant worms were sleeping in this abyss-like vertical tree hole.
When they descended to seventy thousand feet …

Chapter 2026
As they continued to descend, the stench became stronger and stronger …
Luo Yan's eyes flashed with purple light. She quietly warned, "Brother, there's danger. Slow down."
With that, Luo Yan lightly tapped the tip of her foot, and the sword moved down a body position.
If danger erupted, she could immediately block in front of Luo Zheng …
Seeing Luo Yan's small action, Luo Zheng didn't know whether to laugh or cry. He wasn't that weak in his sister's eyes, right?
After descending another five hundred feet, everyone finally saw the source of the stench.
"Heavens, a giant insect died!"
"So this giant insect's body rotted!"
A normal giant insect was pure green in color. It looked like a green vegetable insect that had been magnified thousands of times.
However, this giant insect had turned grayish-brown. Its hard shell was damaged and filled with holes of various sizes.
"Why did this giant insect die?" Someone revealed a curious expression.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
At this moment, everyone suddenly heard the sound of something breaking through the air.
It turned out that it was the sword race disciples that had rushed over.
After the sword mastiff rushed over, his gaze quickly fell on Luo Zheng and Luo Yan. He faintly smiled and nodded to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng didn't know Jian Wuhen's arrangements, but he knew that the sword race was standing with Yu Taibai this time. He also nodded in response.
"These larvae are the larvae of giant insects. From larvae to adults, they need dozens of god eras to hatch. In their insect form, they won't move at all." The sword mastiff glanced at the rotting insect and frowned. "This larva … won't resist. It should have been parasitized by another creature. A portion of its flesh was swallowed, and after it died, it began to rot …"
"What creature?" A True God immediately asked cautiously.
"Tooth cicada," the sword mastiff indifferently replied.
"Aren't cicada-type vicious insects very noisy? This tooth cicada can't make a sound? "A True God asked.
The Exploding Cicada, Night Light Cicada, and Water Worm Cicada were all very powerful. However, they left a deep impression on people because of their ear-piercing sounds.
This was especially true for the Explosive Cicada, whose most powerful attack was its suicidal attack.
When the fist-sized cicada exploded, the sound waves it produced were enough to turn everything within a hundred miles of the Divine Realm into dust.
If it was placed in the universe, it could probably destroy a great world.
Hearing the sword mastiff say 'tooth cicada' and how quiet this place was, there were naturally people who were suspicious.
"Yes." Sword Mastiff knew that some people would question him, so he only reminded them, "Tooth Cicadas don't make any noise, but they are more troublesome than other insects. Our Sword Tribe only has the ability to protect ourselves. Everyone, please be careful …"
Just as he finished speaking.
Suddenly, a soft popping sound came from the rotten giant worm's body.
In this kind of environment, everyone was originally a little nervous. Coupled with the Sword Mastiff's words, everyone held their breaths and focused. They were immediately startled by this sound.
Everyone could only see that a piece of hard shell on the giant worm's decaying body had exploded, and a little bit of yellowish-brown corpse gas spurted out …
"It's just ordinary corpse gas, hehe," a dark-faced old man said with a smile.
Many corpses would emit a rotten corpse gas after being stored for a long time. Moreover, this giant worm's body was so huge that its tissues had been constantly decaying and changing. It was already filled with corpse gas, so it was normal for it to crack under pressure …
Just as the dark-faced old man finished speaking with a smile, a black light flashed in the darkness at an astonishing speed.
"Whoosh, pa!"
The dark-faced old man's entire body trembled slightly. Then, his arms drooped down, and a small round hole appeared on his forehead, with blood gushing out.
"Hehe, hahahaha …"
Suddenly, the dark-faced old man began to laugh wildly. He stood on the flying sword, swaying nonstop as he laughed maniacally.
"Huff, huff …"
At the same time, an extremely powerful energy faintly shot out from his abdomen. That was the precursor to his dantian exploding!
The inner world of an upper True God was already very perfect, and the space inside was also very large. If it exploded, the power would be unimaginable.
From the moment the black light shot into the dark-faced old man's mind to his dantian exploding, it had only been an instant. No one could stop it in time.
"Let's go!"
"This guy is going to explode!"
The other True Gods all fled and shot downwards.
Some upper True Gods had even abandoned their flying swords. Flying was not restricted in the Death Grounds, and Sword Kinesis Flight was a burden to them.
"Puchi!"
Seeing that the dark-faced old man was about to explode, a figure quickly rushed over. He reached out and gently grabbed the dark-faced old man's head, directly twisting it off!
The dark-faced old man was left with a headless body. The rapid energy reaction in his dantian slowly dissipated, and the self-explosion was stopped.
Then, the headless body gently flipped over and fell directly from the sword into the endless abyss …
At this time, everyone could clearly see the figure. It turned out to be the upper True God of the Dongfang family, Dongfang Gui!
"After this tooth cicada shoots into the mind, it can instantly swallow the soul, then gain control of the body and directly explode your dantian." Dongfang Gui's gaze swept across everyone and finally stopped on Luo Zheng. "If anyone is parasitized by this tooth cicada, don't blame me for being merciless."
From Dongfang Gui's point of view, it would be best if Luo Zheng or Luo Yan were parasitized by the tooth cicada. That way, he could logically kill them.
Everyone's face sank slightly.
No one wanted to become like the dark-faced old man and become a brainless body.
However, this tooth cicada's speed was too fast. There was almost no time to react. Although this giant diamond-shaped worm was a larva, its body was tens of thousands or even hundreds of thousands of feet long. It was too dangerous to continue down!
Jian Ao's brows slightly furrowed.
He had to be especially wary of this Dongfang Gui. This person was an upper True God of the Dongfang family. The True Gods of the Tang family, Fang family, and other Grand Alliance should also listen to him. Just now, Dongfang Gui's speed was so fast that Jian Ao was very wary.
At this time, everyone heard a soft "pop" sound again.
Almost all the upper True Gods' hearts suddenly jumped at the same time. Every nerve in their body tensed up.
"Xiu!"
Another jet of black light shot out.
"Shua!"
This time, Jian Ao saw it clearly.
Seeing that jet of black light still shooting out in midair, Jian Ao had already made his move.
His sword was extremely fast.
From drawing the sword to attacking and then sheathing the sword, it was originally three actions.
However, when it came to Jian Ao, it became one action …
When he sheathed his sword, everyone heard a crisp "ding" sound. The jet of black light shuttling back and forth in the air split into two. Jian Ao had cut it in half.

Chapter 2027
The sword mastiff's sword was unimaginably fast.
If it was used by an upper true god, no one would be able to dodge it …
"These tooth cicadas aren't very scary. It's fine as long as they don't enter your brain. Everyone, follow me down," the sword mastiff said to the true gods.
After saying that, he lightly stepped on the long sword under his feet, and his whole body shot down vertically, toward the ground below.
Although Jian Wuhen had instructed the sword mastiff to protect the Luo siblings, if they couldn't even make it through this place, then there was no value in protecting them.
After all, there were many dangers along the way. They were too weak and didn't have the right to get close to the skeleton.
They might as well place their hopes on their own people …
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
The remaining eleven sword clan disciples followed the sword mastiff and dived down.
The other upper true gods, such as Nan Long Hua, saw the sword mastiff's valiant attack and instantly felt that he was brave and reliable. They would be able to protect their lives if they followed him, so they all followed.
"Brother, let's go." Luo Yan smiled as if he didn't think much of the sword mastiff's attack. Then, he added to Luo Zheng, "No matter what happens, just hide behind me!"
Luo Zheng smiled and followed behind Luo Yan.
The speed at which these two hundred upper true gods were descending became faster and faster. Some upper true gods even kicked their flying swords to the side and allowed themselves to fall freely. They were plummeting downwards.
The tooth cicadas were hidden in the body of the larvae. As long as a living being passed by, they would turn into a black light and shoot out.
The sword mastiff at the front held the sword sheath in one hand and the sword hilt in the other. His eyes were bright.
"Pop …"
Every time the larvae's skin broke open.
The sword mastiff's right hand moved slightly and a sharp sword light shot out, cutting the tooth cicadas in half.
At the beginning, there weren't many tooth cicadas. Every time they descended a thousand feet, one or two tooth cicadas would jump out.
After descending for a while, more and more tooth cicadas shot out of the larvae's body. The sword mastiff's attack speed also became faster and faster. Occasionally, there would be a fish that slipped through the net. However, the sword clan disciples would take advantage of the situation and attack …
Looking at the sword clan's disciples being so fierce, the upper true gods following behind them were instantly relieved.
"This place is only so-so."
"Those Tooth Cicadas are only fast. As long as I raise my guard, I can also kill them …"
"Eh? The Tooth Cicadas have all disappeared?"
An Upper True God said. Only then did everyone realize that the Sword Mastiff had stopped attacking.
Luo Zheng glanced at Ruo Ling Xiao, who was hanging on the cave wall, and frowned.
It was unknown how long this larva-like body was …
Since the insect was dead, its body should have been eaten by the Tooth Cicadas. Logically speaking, these Tooth Cicadas should be all over the body of the Ruo Ling larva. It was impossible for them to suddenly disappear.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't know that Sword Mastiff was also thinking about the same thing.
It was not the first time the Sword Mastiffs had entered this place, and the Tooth Cicadas were not unique to this place.
Before the Sword Tribe took over Ruo Mu, the Tooth Cicadas were already widespread.
If these Tooth Cicadas hunted some large ferocious creature, they would immediately kill the creature's soul. Then, they would gradually erode the creature's body and reproduce in the body.
According to the Sword Tribe's understanding of the Tooth Cicadas, these ferocious insects even had their own tribes. Different tribes of Tooth Cicadas would fight with each other and use the creature's body as a battlefield!
The Tooth Cicadas would only leave after every piece of the large creature's body was completely devoured.
The Ruo Ling larva was so huge. Looking at the extent of its decay, it must have been dead for a long time. This situation was not right.
When the Sword Mastiff descended another few thousand feet …
He suddenly heard a "pop" sound.
The Sword Mastiff instinctively looked at the Ruo Ling larva.
"Pop, pop, pop, pop, pop …"
The Ruo Ling larva's skin was constantly being peeled open. Many small black cicadas crawled on the surface of the Ruo Ling larva, motionless.
At this moment, everyone could clearly see the original appearance of these Tooth Cicadas.
There were circles of red patterns on the jet-black shell of the Tooth Cicadas. A pair of wings were like two sharp swords. There was also a pair of awl-like spikes on the head of the Tooth Cicadas. Those were their long teeth.
These Tooth Cicadas did not attack immediately!
However, many of the upper True Gods' faces were already pale. There were simply too many of them!
Seeing this scene, the Sword Mastiff's face suddenly turned ugly. He finally recalled something …
"The Tooth Cicada King has been born. Everyone … I wish you all the best!"
The Tooth Cicadas themselves did not have intelligence. However, the Tooth Cicada King did.
These small ferocious insects were not scary individually. However, if there were a large number of them, they would be very difficult to deal with.
If a Tooth Cicada King was born from the Tooth Cicadas and commanded these Tooth Cicadas, it would be a disaster.
In history, the sword race had killed many Tooth Cicada Kings. Every time a Tooth Cicada King appeared, many sword race disciples would be killed or injured!
The Sword Mastiff did not expect that a Tooth Cicada King would appear in this Dao battle.
Just as he finished speaking …
Even more Tooth Cicadas crawled out of the Ruo Ling larva's shell. Their skin was on the rotten body, awaiting orders. They did not attack.
"Charge down!"
"Huff, huff …"
Following the Sword Mastiff's command, no one hesitated. They all shot down at their fastest speed!
At the same time, all the Tooth Cicadas transformed into streaks of black light and shot toward the upper True Gods …
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Every Tooth Cicada was a lethal hidden weapon.
They transformed into thousands of black lines and charged toward the True Gods!
"Be careful!"
"Pa!"
Just as one of the upper true gods shouted out a warning, a small bloody hole appeared on his forehead.
"Boom!"
A breath later, that upper True God's dantian flashed with a blue light. His entire body suddenly exploded and transformed into a huge fireball.
No one paid any attention to this self-destructing upper True God …
In such a chaotic situation, every True God could only protect themselves. They could only dive down.
The purple light in Luo Yan's eyes grew brighter and brighter. As an upper True God, she could also fly. Naturally, she abandoned the flying sword under her feet and grabbed Luo Zheng's hand.
"They're here!"
Not far away, sixty to seventy streaks of black light arrived in an instant. Her curved eyebrows slightly furrowed.
Luo Zheng was already prepared to attack …
These Tooth Cicadas were only formidable in terms of speed. If he really wanted to kill them, they might not be able to stop him!
However, he heard Luo Yan spit out a single word.
"Destroy."
Luo Zheng felt that something strange was happening.
The Tooth Cicadas that were shooting over seemed to have lost their lives in the instant that Luo Yan spat out that single word. They all plummeted to the ground.

Chapter 2028
"Is this the true Absolute Command?"
Luo Zheng's face was full of astonishment.
What he knew about Luo Yan was only the Extreme Purple Yin Physique.
Luo Zheng didn't know much about what Luo Yan had cultivated in the Realm of the Gods.
A few days ago, when Luo Zheng asked, Luo Yan had been evasive …
Apart from this, when Luo Zheng asked about his mother, Luo Yan also answered vaguely.
The current Luo Zheng couldn't see through this sister of his.
But even if it was the Absolute Command, there had to be energy fluctuations …
For example, when the "Grand Ruling Art" was cultivated to the extreme, it could also be used to create laws with one's words. However, that was a special technique that merged the divine path and spiritual attacks together. If one looked at it separately, it was still a kind of spiritual attack.
Now, Luo Yan had decided the life and death of these tooth cicadas with a single word. Such a technique.
As the tooth cicadas shot down, Luo Zheng looked at the back of his sister's snow-white neck and fell into deep thought.
"Rumble!"
"Rumble …"
Apart from Luo Zheng, no one else had noticed Luo Yan's technique just now.
The scene was really too chaotic.
Upper True Gods were constantly being possessed by tooth cicadas and then self-destructed.
Under the self-destruction, it was very likely that other True Gods would be injured. Once injured, they couldn't escape the attack of the tooth cicadas, which triggered a chain of self-destruction …
But the ones who died were the upper True Gods of the first-tier clans.
The rich and powerful clans had made considerable preparations to participate in the Dao Battle this time.
In the face of such a sudden crisis, they naturally used their life-saving techniques one after another!
For example, a flock of palm-sized birds appeared on Dong Fang Gui's chest. There were about twenty of them.
These birds flew back and forth, shuttling back and forth around him like shuttles. Whenever a tooth cicada approached, these birds would stretch out their slender beaks and gently pick up the tooth cicadas, bringing them into their beaks.
As for the three upper True Gods of the Bai Clan, they formed a triangle. Under the three of them, rays of sharp light shrouded them.
No matter how fast the Tooth Cicadas collided, the sharp triangular light remained unmoved, completely isolating all the Tooth Cicadas outside …
Although the True Gods of the first-tier families were well-prepared, their inheritances were much weaker than those of the wealthy families.
Some upper True Gods used all of their methods, but they were still unable to stop the Tooth Cicadas. In the end, the Tooth Cicadas drilled through their heads and detonated their dantians. Even in death, they were unable to leave behind a complete corpse.
Rumble … Rumble …
The speed at which everyone was descending was getting faster and faster, and the sounds of Dantian exploding were getting more and more frequent.
"Everyone, hold on a little longer!"
The Sword Mastiff gritted its teeth and shouted.
Every time he attacked with his sword, there would be dozens of silver lights flashing, instantly cutting hundreds of Tooth Cicadas into pieces.
But even more Tooth Cicadas turned into black lights, like hidden weapons in the dark night, sweeping towards them …
"Pu!"
The Sword Mastiff suddenly heard a muffled sound coming from not far away.
"Mo Chen!"
He saw Mo Chen of the Sword Race tremble, and a small bloody hole appeared in the center of his forehead.
Sword Mastiff's heart turned cold. The sword in his hand trembled as he slashed at Mo Chen.
Before Mo Chen could self-detonate, that ray of silver light had already chopped his head off …
The Sword Race had finally lost its first disciple!
"How could this be!"
Mo Chen was not weak. Although he did not belong to the same lineage as the Sword Mastiff, they were on good terms.
Seeing Mo Chen die, the Sword Mastiff was extremely frustrated.
In the past, the path at the beginning of the Dao Contest had been relatively smooth. Under normal circumstances, there would not be much danger at all. Not long after, they had encountered such a huge crisis. Could it be that everyone was really going to die in this Dao Contest?
Swish swish swish swish …
As they continued forward, the number of Tooth Cicadas did not decrease. Instead, there were more of them.
The black Tooth Cicadas were like a storm, shooting at the True Gods without any blind spots …
No matter how fast the Sword Mastiff's sword was, it was impossible for him to kill all the Tooth Cicadas. As long as there were a few fish that escaped the net, his own life would be in danger.
Just as the Sword Mastiff was in despair …
Luo Yan had already arrived at the bottom of the group from the top.
"The Luo siblings are fine." Seeing that the siblings were unharmed, the Sword Mastiff was slightly comforted.
He had originally thought that these Tooth Cicadas were a test for the siblings. He did not expect that the siblings would be so smart and not be harmed by the Tooth Cicadas.
"Hide behind me! Don't be rash! "the Sword Mastiff shouted loudly.
Her slender fingers gently intertwined and placed by her mouth. Then, she spat out another word.
"Entangle."
This time, Luo Zheng still did not make a move.
He let Luo Yan rush to the front, but he was also constantly observing the surrounding movements. Once a Tooth Cicada shot out, he would immediately make a move.
At the same time, Luo Zheng was also secretly observing Luo Yan.
"She spat out another word, and her voice does not have any fluctuations of emotion. It's as if she didn't say it …"
The moment Luo Yan spat out that word …
In the space around everyone, small whirlpools appeared.
Hundreds …
Thousands …
Tens of thousands …
These whirlpools were only the size of a fist, but they emitted an extremely strong gravitational force. Countless Tooth Cicadas were firmly sucked in, fixed in the air, and slowly rotated.
She maintained the same posture, and the vortex continued to appear around everyone.
Countless Tooth Cicadas shot out, but in the end, they all inevitably fell into the small whirlpools …
Originally, those upper True Gods were extremely nervous. Now that they suddenly discovered that these mysterious small whirlpools had appeared around them, a strange expression appeared on their faces. Many people did not even know where these whirlpools came from!
"It's her!"
"It's the woman below!"
Very quickly, someone discovered Luo Yan.
Although there was still not the slightest fluctuation of energy on her body, these people immediately confirmed it.
"This woman … is powerful, very powerful. She saved me five times before!"
This time, Nan Longhua was also frightened, but when he saw Luo Yan make a move again, he immediately became excited.
"You …"
Jian Ao looked at Luo Yan not far away, and the expression on his face was extremely interesting.
The magical power that Luo Yan used might not be considered powerful, but it was extremely incredible!
He suddenly understood that the Sword Tribe must have given sufficient consideration to be able to assign the task of inheriting the skeleton to the Luo siblings. Jian Wuhen was not a fool.
The strength of this pair of siblings was definitely beyond his imagination!
As they continued to descend, whirlpools constantly appeared around everyone, attracting thousands of Tooth Cicadas. Although these Tooth Cicadas were not killed, these Tooth Cicadas were unable to move.
This state lasted for the time it took to burn an incense stick.

Chapter 2029
The speed at which the upper True Gods were descending gradually slowed down.
Nan Longhua and the other upper True Gods heaved a sigh of relief at the same time …
They were really unlucky this time. They had just entered the tree hole in the middle of Ruo Mu when they encountered such a crisis.
"It's really a narrow escape!"
"I almost died. A tooth cicada almost slid across my forehead!"
"I wonder how many people died?"
The upper True Gods all had expressions of joy on their faces.
"There were a total of 47 explosions. Including the one whose head was ripped off by Dong Fang Gui, a total of 48 people died," said an upper True God not far away.
Not all of the upper true gods were panicking.
Some upper True Gods had experienced unimaginable hardships and were indifferent to life and death. In such a dangerous situation, they still had the time to take note of these things.
Luo Zheng looked at that upper-level true god with some surprise. Just now, Luo Zheng was also counting the number of people who had died.
The number of people who died was indeed 48. Among them, 43 were from first-tier families and five were from wealthy families.
The upper True God was very thin and small. His appearance was not conspicuous at all. His pair of soybean-sized eyes were bright and full of expression. Luo Zheng did not know which family he came from.
The long sword under Jian Ao's feet turned gently and turned into a ray of white light. He came in front of Luo Yan and cupped his hands to Luo Yan. "Thank you for just now. If you had not taken action, I'm afraid there would be more people who died."
Jian Ao was basically certain now.
The most important person in the Luo family should be Luo Yan.
According to Jian Ao's calculations, if Luo Yan had not taken action, at least half of the sword clan's disciples would have died in the face of so many tooth cicadas. Jian Ao himself might even have died!
After all, the tooth cicadas were the most numerous. The densest part of the journey was all because of Luo Yan's protection.
"Mm." Luo Yan's response was somewhat indifferent.
Sword Mastiff didn't care about Luo Yan's attitude. Since they had been chosen by Jian Wuhen, the 12 most outstanding upper True Divinities of the Sword Race were already prepared to die.
After returning, he used a true essence sound transmission to say to the remaining 10 sword race juniors, "The next mission is to protect Luo Yan. You can give up on anyone else. If anyone tries to attack Luo Yan, you must do everything in your power to kill them!"
The disciples of the Sword Race understood. Their thoughts were similar to the sword mastiff's.
"What about Luo Zheng?" A disciple of the Sword Race asked via voice transmission.
Sword Mastiff glanced indifferently at Luo Yan, who was standing behind Luo Zheng, and smiled faintly. "Although this kid's reputation on the floating island is resounding, and he has even defeated a group of Paragons before, he's still just a lower True God in the end. Don't mind him too much."
It wasn't that the Sword Mastiff was looking down on Luo Zheng.
Although it was indeed inconceivable that Luo Zheng had managed to snatch the imperial jade seal from a group of Paragons, everyone believed that Luo Zheng had succeeded by chance.
However, everyone believed that Luo Zheng had only succeeded by chance. It was definitely not a reflection of his strength. Any Great Perfection expert could easily kill Luo Zheng …
Even the upper True Gods didn't put Luo Zheng in their eyes.
In comparison, Dongfang Gui and the upper True Gods of the Wealthy Clan Alliance were completely different.
Dongfang Gui's lips moved slightly. Clearly, he was also giving instructions. His eyes occasionally glanced at Luo Yan with a faint trace of fear …
"Yan 'er, you're amazing."
After everyone continued to descend, Luo Zheng praised her.
Luo Yan's indifferent expression changed. Her beautiful face immediately bloomed with a brilliant and proud smile. "Yan 'er, this isn't the only area where you're amazing!"
Luo Zheng was very curious about her divine ability. However, he also knew that this wasn't the time to discuss this. Thus, he didn't pursue the matter.
After passing by that rotten larva, the vertical tree hole regained its tranquility.
Everyone maintained a uniform speed as they descended …
After experiencing the battle just now, everyone maintained a considerable amount of vigilance.
"Drip, drip …"
As they continued to descend, the sound of water dripping suddenly reached everyone's ears.
"What the hell is that?"
Someone muttered.
In this damned place, they could encounter all sorts of random things. They were already somewhat jittery.
However, when Jian Ao's gaze swept down, his face revealed a pleasantly surprised expression.
"It's the Aqua Spring! I didn't expect it to really appear … "
Hearing Jian Ao's words, the other upper True Gods' eyes slightly jumped.
The Aqua Spring was born in the middle section of the Aqua Spring. It took a hundred years for a pot to be produced. This item was extremely rare even within the Sword Clan.
Releasing the Aqua Spring in one's inner world could benefit all living things within one's inner world. Not only that, this spring water was the favorite of all alchemists and blacksmiths. It could be said to be the Water of All Spirits …
Thus, its price in the Divine Realm was extremely high!
Some people within the Sword Clan guessed that there would be a large amount of Aqua Spring inside the Aqua Spring.
However, in the past Dao Battles, no True God had discovered the Aqua Spring!
"Drip, drip, drip …"
As everyone descended, they saw small protruding platforms around the cave walls. There were depressions on these platforms, and the depressions were filled with aqua green liquid.
Every time a drop of Aqua Spring dripped down, the liquid would create a slight ripple.
"So much!"
"Why is there suddenly so much Aqua Spring …"
"…"
Some of the True Gods became agitated and rushed forward.
The disciples of the Sword Tribe frowned.
The Aqua Spring was extremely precious even to the Sword Clan. Since there was so much Aqua Spring here, it should belong to the Sword Clan.
However, in the Dao Battles, if they were to interfere, it would probably cause a dispute …
Luo Zheng's figure flashed and stood at the edge of a platform.
Luo Zheng had seen this Aqua Spring before.
Back in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, when the Craftsman Saint forged his body, he used up a bottle of Aqua Spring.
Although Luo Zheng was unable to move at that time, he could see that the Craftsman Saint was clearly distressed when he took out the Aqua Spring.
One could imagine the value of something precious to the Craftsman Saint.
After breaking through to the Lesser True God Realm, Luo Zheng's cultivation stopped growing.
For other True Gods, after condensing their Godhood, they would continue to expand their inner world.
At the same time, the clouds formed by the True Essence Sea in their inner world would condense within the Godhood … As long as they made another breakthrough in the Divine Dao, it would be extremely natural for them to condense the Godhood of an Intermediate True God.
However, all of this became complicated when it came to Luo Zheng.
The Primal Chaos energy could not condense into the Godhood …
Did he have to search for the brown mud to continue to advance?
Now, he couldn't even figure out what the Shenwu Coins could do for him …
He had heard that if the Aqua Spring was drunk directly, it would greatly benefit the living beings in his inner world. Naturally, he would give it a try.

Chapter 2030
There were more than twenty platforms of various sizes filled with the spring water.
Luo Zheng's reaction was relatively quick. As soon as he arrived, he occupied the largest platform, which naturally attracted the attention of others.
The one who spoke was the Tang Family's upper true god, Tang Xiuqi.
This person was eight feet tall and had bronze skin. Among the upper true gods in the Arena of Gods, he was ranked in the top thirty. His strength was not to be underestimated.
The Tang Family was good at refining tools, and Tang Xiuqi's Tang Family was famous for it!
He valued the value of the spring water more than the other true gods.
Tang Xiuqi's reaction was slightly slower. Seeing these guys occupy the platforms, he immediately became anxious.
When they looked again, Luo Zheng had actually occupied the largest platform, so how could they restrain themselves?
"Idiot …"
Seeing Tang Xiuqi go up to Luo Zheng to cause trouble, Dongfang Gui cursed in his heart.
Before participating in the Dao Battle, the Paragons of the Wealthy Alliance and the accompanying true gods conspired together.
Now was not the time to make a move …
Although the Dongfang Family was the leader of the Wealthy Alliance, Dongfang Gui was only an upper true god.
The Tang Family's Tang Xu and Tang Jie were still fine …
An impulsive guy like Tang Xiuqi might not listen to him!
"Where are you?"
Luo Zheng glanced at Tang Xiuqi indifferently.
Luo Yan, who was floating behind Luo Zheng, quietly leaned on Luo Zheng's shoulder. A trace of ridicule appeared on her face. "Asking my brother to scram? Are you looking for death?"
Tang Xiuqi's expression immediately sank. Although Luo Yan's method was very special and could actually entangle all the Tooth Cicadas, her true strength was hard to say. He was not necessarily afraid of this pair of siblings!
However, he did not have much confidence in fighting one against two.
He raised his head and said, "Tang Jie, Tang Xu! Are you planning to watch the show? "
Not far away, two people shot over.
One of them was a middle-aged man with a flushed face. It was Tang Jie. The other was a handsome youth, Tang Xu.
"I really don't know who's the one courting death …" Tang Xiuqi summoned his two brothers. Naturally, his confidence was greatly boosted. He looked at Luo Yan and grinned evilly.
However, Tang Jie and Tang Xu were much calmer than Tang Xiuqi.
"Shuqi, although this Wakamu Spring is precious, it's not very important!" Tang Jie said.
"Yes, we have more important things to do," Tang Xu said bluntly.
Their so-called important task was to prevent others from obtaining the inheritance of the skeletons in the Land of Dao Struggle.
Whether or not he could obtain it was secondary, but he definitely couldn't let anyone else obtain it, especially the people from the Luo Clan …
Unfortunately, Tang Xiuqi's temperament was extremely stubborn. Even if these two brothers of his didn't help him, they actually advised him to retreat. He couldn't put down his face for a while.
"The Tang family doesn't have cowards like you," he scolded angrily. Unexpectedly, he took the initiative to make a move against Luo Zheng. He was thinking that as long as he made a move, Tang Xu and Tang Jie wouldn't just stand by and watch.
Once the two of them made a move, the True Gods of the Dongfang Family and the Fang Family would most likely have to make a move as well.
In any case, it wasn't a bad choice to get rid of these two siblings first.
Although Tang Xiuqi looked brainless, he had actually calculated all of this.
However, Tang Xiuqi had miscalculated one thing. He still underestimated Luo Yan's strength.
Just as Tang Xiuqi's voice fell, a smile suddenly appeared on Luo Yan's face. The purple light in his eyes was like a snake's tongue, constantly flicking in and out.
This purple light was an extremely dangerous light …
"If you're not a coward, you can make a move," Luo Yan suddenly said with a smile.
"Is that so …" How could Tang Xiuqi endure Luo Yan's provocation?
Just as he was about to make a move, he heard Luo Yan softly spit out a word.
"Break."
At this moment, Tang Xiuqi stretched out his arm and the shadow of a sledgehammer slowly condensed in his hand. This sledgehammer was definitely Tang Xiuqi's life treasure.
However, the shadow of the sledgehammer had only condensed halfway …
He felt a warm feeling coming from his neck.
Then, his body suddenly became light. From his neck down, his body was actually separated from his brain. The warm feeling was caused by the blood crazily spurting out the moment his neck was broken!
An upper True God's bones were more solid.
Even if a small finger bone was broken, it would produce a loud sound.
It wasn't easy to cut an upper True God's neck. After all, an upper True God's bones were comparable to a grade-less grandmist treasure.
Now, after Luo Yan spat out a word, his neck was strangely broken. Everyone, including Luo Zheng, was stunned at this moment.
Luo Zheng also didn't expect Luo Yan to make a move without any warning. This sister …
If he had a weapon in hand and moved at the fastest speed, Luo Zheng was ninety percent sure that he could cut Tang Xiuqi's neck off.
However, Luo Zheng still couldn't understand Luo Yan's method.
How did this guy's neck break …
"Xiuqi!"
"Xiuqi!"
Tang Jie and Tang Xu's expressions changed drastically.
Seeing Tang Xiuqi's head separated from his body, Tang Jie shot down and grabbed Tang Xiuqi's body.
On the other side, Tang Xu also grabbed Tang Xiuqi's head.
Being separated from one's body wasn't fatal to a True God. However, if it was separated for too long, it would hurt the body and internal world …
"There's no need to save him." Luo Yan said softly with a cold expression. She aimed at Tang Xiuqi's head and spat out the word, "Destroy."
When her voice was softly uttered …
Tang Xiuqi's eyes were still open, and his face was filled with fear. In an instant, all signs of life disappeared from his head.
The pupils of that pair of eyes that were filled with fear instantly dilated. All of the life force in that pair of eyes dissipated. That person had actually died.
"This …"
"You!"
The two Tang Family's experts fell into a panic.
Jian Ao and the Sword Race's experts stood guard at the side. When they saw Luo Yan's method, their faces were filled with disbelief.
Just spitting out two words was enough to kill an upper True God. This woman's method was too …
Dongfang Gui, who was not far away, was still able to maintain his composure. However, the True Gods of the Fang, Leng, and Mu families all had ugly looks on their faces. If Luo Yan was really that strong, then the mission given to them by their superiors would be impossible to complete.
Luo Yan's expression was calm.
However, that pair of purple glowing eyes was filled with coldness.
She slowly sat on the edge of the platform and looked at the True Gods present. She said indifferently, "No matter what your goal is, if you provoke my brother, you'll end up like this guy."
Nan Longhua looked at Luo Yan and felt a chill in her heart.
This woman had looked so gentle and cute before. Who would have thought that once she was provoked, she would be so terrifying …

Chapter 2031
Luo Yan was like a goddess who ruled over life and death, cold and powerful.
However, the moment she sat down beside Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng noticed that her fingers trembled slightly.
Noticing this small detail, Luo Zheng faintly smiled and quietly said with a true essence sound transmission, "Yan 'er is a bit nervous, don't force yourself. Big Brother can handle it."
Luo Yan hadn't experienced much killing, and this was probably her first time killing a True Divinity. It was inevitable that she would feel a bit uncomfortable.
Perhaps this was Yu Taibai's teaching that caused Luo Yan to behave like this.
It was really hard on her …
"Yan 'er isn't nervous." Luo Yan also replied with a true essence sound transmission, "Big Brother should quickly absorb this Aqua Spring. Yan' er doesn't need it."
Seeing her serious expression, Luo Zheng faintly smiled.
Then, he placed his hands into the Aqua Spring.
This Aqua Spring was the life essence of the Aqua Spring and could fuse with all things.
As Luo Zheng's hands touched it, the dark green liquid naturally gathered in Luo Zheng's hands.
The Aqua Spring on the platform was being absorbed by Luo Zheng at a speed visible to the naked eye.
Luo Zheng didn't spread the spring water throughout his body. Instead, he guided it through his meridians into his dantian.
Dongfang Gui also occupied a platform on the side.
On the edge of the platform, there were two other upper True Divinities of the Dongfang Family.
One was called Dongfang Qi and the other was called Dongfang Xi.
The three True Divinities of the Dongfang Family were also carefully selected by Dongfang Taiqing.
"Big Brother Gui, this Aqua Spring …" Beside Dongfang Gui, the youthful Dongfang Xi said.
"No need to leave it for me. You can absorb it yourselves." Dongfang Gui shook his head. He didn't care about this Aqua Spring.
Dongfang Qi saw that Dongfang Gui had a lot on his mind and was looking at Luo Yan. He quietly asked with a true essence sound transmission, "Big Brother Gui, are you still worried about that Luo Yan? What kind of power is she using? It doesn't seem to be the Divine Dao. There aren't even any fluctuations of Dao. It's really strange! "
Who knew that Dongfang Gui would lightly shake his head, "Wrong. I'm not worried about Luo Yan."
"Not Luo Yan?" Dongfang Qi was stunned. He rolled his eyes and asked, "Then you must be worried about the Sword Race?"
This time, the Sword Race had suddenly halved the number of wealthy families, and the number of Sword Race members had doubled. This was equivalent to declaring the Sword Race's position. Their goal was to help the Luo siblings obtain the inheritance!
Looking at the leading Sword Mastiff, it was not an existence to be trifled with.
"No." Dongfang Gui continued to shake his head.
"Then who are you worried about?" Dongfang Qi's face was filled with confusion. He knew that Dongfang Gui was very resourceful. Although he was only an upper True God, he was highly regarded by the Dongfang family. Naturally, his foresight was much better than ordinary True Gods. Dongfang Qi's gaze then landed on Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng was currently absorbing the Wakamu Spring. Only then did he ask, "Could it be that Luo Zheng?"
"Correct." Dongfang Gui's eyes narrowed slightly.
Dongfang Qi did not know whether to laugh or cry. "Brother Gui, although this Luo Zheng is very powerful, his cultivation is there. Do we need to be afraid of him?"
Dongfang Xi, who had been absorbing the Wakamu Spring, also raised his head and said lightly, "When he was a God, he was able to defeat a lower True God. This kind of strength is indeed shocking. Now that he has become a lower True God, he is at most comparable to a peak middle True God, such as Dongfang Yuhao and Mu Xin."
Dongfang Yuhao and Mu Xin were ranked first and second in the middle True God tier of the Pantheon Jade Wall.
Dongfang Xi felt that his evaluation was already very high.
Dongfang Gui nodded. "But … since he entered the tree hole, he has not made a move."
"With that powerful sister, why would he need to make a move?" Dongfang Xi shook his head and began to absorb the Wakamu Spring again.
Dongfang Gui understood what they were saying, but he still felt that something was wrong.
Just now, when he saw Tang Xiuqi make a move, he did not stop him. There was another reason. He wanted to use Tang Xiuqi to observe Luo Zheng's current strength.
But before Luo Zheng could react, Tang Xiuqi was killed by Luo Yan.
Now, the two from the Tang family were still staring at the Luo family siblings with bloodshot eyes. But they were afraid of Luo Yan's strength, so they did not dare to make a move.
"Tang Jie, Tang Xu, come here," Dongfang Gui said via true essence.
Tang Jie and Tang Xu both looked at Dongfang Gui.
"If you want revenge, you have to take your time," Dongfang Gui said indifferently.
This time, although the members of the Aristocratic Alliance had given their own missions, there was no leader among them. For example, Dongfang Gui could only command Dongfang Xi and Dongfang Qi.
Now, Dongfang Gui realized that he had to gather these people together …
…
Within Luo Zheng's inner world, a green rain began to fall.
These raindrops all fell around the World Tree …
Now, the World Tree of Life once again formed a huge piece of land around it. As it began to absorb the raindrops, the entire tree began to sway and sway rapidly.
"Rumble …"
"So fast!"
This World Tree was originally several thousand feet tall.
Now, it was rapidly growing …
All the branches began to extend, and the trunk began to swell. The roots buried deep underground began to wriggle, and the surrounding ground began to crack.
In the blink of an eye, the World Tree had grown from several thousand feet to several hundred thousand feet tall, and the roots buried deep underground had increased by ten times!
"Eh?"
As the World Tree began to swell, he could clearly feel his inner world expand by a bit!
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng's incarnation flashed, and instantly arrived at the edge of his inner world.
He focused his gaze, and a trace of confusion appeared in his eyes. With a light flick of his finger, a thin line passed by the edge of his inner world.
There were a total of 1,080 golden Sanskrit characters surrounding his inner world. These Sanskrit characters were arranged in an equal distance, and each one was 13,000 miles away from each other, not an inch more, not an inch less …
However, under the measurement of that thin line, there was actually an extra 16 inches of distance!
The distance between each golden Sanskrit character had increased by 16 inches, so the distance between the 1,080 golden Sanskrit characters had increased by more than 17,000 inches.
"My inner world has expanded," Luo Zheng concluded.
As his inner world continued to expand, the distance between the Sanskrit characters surrounding his inner world also increased.
This expansion was very small, so Luo Zheng himself couldn't detect it. But he was still able to confirm it.
"Ruo Mu's spring is only Ruo Mu's life essence. How can it expand this world?"
Luo Zheng's face was filled with confusion …
The world within his dantian was very different from that of ordinary people, so Luo Zheng had always been the one to figure out how to break through to the next level of cultivation.

Chapter 2032
However, this Wakamu Spring was too rare.
Even within the Sword Tribe, there probably wasn't much left.
Just now, Luo Zheng had absorbed about ten pots of Wakamu Spring. Even if the Sword Tribe had accumulated it, it would probably take more than a thousand years.
Moreover, his entire world had only expanded slightly.
Relying on this thing to expand his internal world was somewhat unrealistic.
After dispersing his internal incarnation, Luo Zheng once again stepped onto his flying sword. Together with Luo Yan, they once again followed the True Gods down.
As they continued to descend, the number of water chestnut worms around them decreased until finally, all of them disappeared …
The surroundings were completely barren, without the slightest bit of life to speak of. However, the evil intent released by the killing zone was becoming more and more intense!
Many people's eyes were bright red and emitted a strong murderous aura. It was as if they would fight at any moment.
Especially Tang Jie and Tang Xu. Because of their enmity with Luo Yan, they couldn't control themselves a few times and seemed to want to fight with Luo Yan. However, after Dongfang Gui fed each of them an elixir, they forcibly restrained themselves.
After confirming that it was safe, some True Gods sat upright on their flying swords and simply closed their eyes to rest. They just needed to maintain a certain speed of descent.
"Brother, look. There are many rings on the cave walls below!" Luo Yan pointed down and said.
Luo Zheng looked in the direction Luo Yan pointed. Indeed, he saw milky white rings encircling the wide cave walls.
"What are these?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Growth rings." Luo Yan smiled slightly.
She obviously knew more than Luo Zheng.
In other words, before going to the Wakamu, Yu Taibai had already passed on a lot of information to Luo Yan.
"Are they the growth rings of the Wakamu?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes. The Wakamu will grow a branch every god era. At the same time, there will be an additional circle of growth rings inside the main trunk. These huge growth rings represent the age of the Wakamu," Luo Yan explained seriously.
Some of the True Gods not far away heard this and found it rather novel.
After all, not everyone was qualified to enter this place. Although this information was not important, it was impossible for it to be spread outside.
The Four Great Divine Trees were the foundation of the Realm of the Gods, and they were born very early. From this, one could infer the approximate time when the Realm of the Gods was born …
"Yan 'er knows a lot," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Hearing his brother's praise, Luo Yan tilted his head and said proudly, "Of course!"
Everyone continued to descend through the rings.
A full two days and two nights …
Sword Mastiff warned, "We're almost at the bottom. Everyone, slow down!"
Hearing the warning, the True Gods were slightly startled.
Diving down from the top of Ruo Mu, everyone maintained an extremely high speed.
If he didn't slow down and fell to the bottom like this, the impact would probably tear his body apart!
Fortunately, the Sword Mastiff had issued a warning in advance.
Everyone slowed down their speed and slowly descended. After about an hour, everyone finally saw the bottom.
"We're here!"
"This is the land of Dao Struggle!"
"It's really difficult …"
After Luo Zheng reached the bottom, he used the heel of his foot to lightly knock on the flying sword under his feet. The flying sword spun one round and landed in his hand.
Previously, Luo Zheng's Great Thousand Heavy Sword had been damaged in the Sea of Time. Even though the flying swords given to him by the Sword Race were ordinary, they were still graded grandmist artifacts. They could be used as a temporary replacement.
"Since everyone has arrived at the land of Dao Struggle, do not wander around. You must follow us," Sword Mastiff said to everyone.
"Hmph, everyone says that the land of Dao Struggle hides a great inheritance of the Paramita Realm. Your Sword Race has left this inheritance unchecked for so many years. What can we find if we follow you?" One of the upper True Gods of the Bai Family chuckled.
Then, the other two True Gods of the Bai Family chimed in, "That's right. The Sword Race probably won't be able to obtain it. That's why every Dao Struggle is misleading others. That's why no one has been able to find it after so many years!"
The Bai Family was the same as the Han Family. They didn't belong to the Tyrant Alliance and didn't have much relationship with the Sword Race.
The three of them had also obtained some information. This time, they had come with the goal of obtaining the inheritance …
Hearing this, Sword Mastiff sneered coldly, "Since that's the case, your Bai Family can do as you wish!"
"Big brother, let's go!"
The three True Gods of the Bai Family said coldly and chose to leave in different directions.
At the bottom of the Ruo Tree, the messy roots formed layers and holes. The environment was complex and tangled. In the blink of an eye, the three True Gods of the Bai Family disappeared from everyone's sight.
"Everyone, if you want to move on your own, you can choose to leave now," Sword Mastiff said to everyone.
The upper True Gods present were basically the most powerful group of people in the Deity Domain.
Hearing this, another group of two and three True Gods of the Bai Family chose to leave …
Sword Mastiff hoped that Dongfang Gui would leave with his people.
Unfortunately, Dongfang Gui, the Tang Family, Leng Family, Mu Family, Fang Family, and the other True Gods of the Tyrant Alliance didn't move. It seemed like they were going to move with them.
"Old fox." Sword Mastiff's gaze faintly glanced at Dongfang Gui. When he noticed Dongfang Gui's mocking gaze, he turned his head away.
"Childish," Dongfang Gui said softly.
His biggest goal in this trip wasn't to inherit the inheritance, but to prevent anyone from obtaining the inheritance. Naturally, he wouldn't be so impulsive as to explore the Land of Struggle of Dao.
Sword Mastiff took the lead and chose a direction. He led the group of more than a hundred people forward.
The bottom was filled with roots of various shapes. Apart from that, there were also some strangely shaped plants that grew in dark and damp places.
"Gulp …"
"Gulp …"
After walking for a short while, a strange sound suddenly came from not far ahead.
Some True Gods looked over and saw a cluster of pale yellow ganoderma.
The ganoderma was the size of a millstone and neatly arranged together. There were more than twenty of them and they looked very attractive.
"This ganoderma has spiritual energy!"
"It's definitely not an ordinary item!"
Nan Longhua and the other True Gods were slightly tempted. Before anyone could react, they rushed forward to step on it.
Sword Mastiff saw this and turned his head to say, "Junior Brother Mo Nan?"
"Yes." Mo Nan, who was behind Sword Mastiff, took a step forward and the longsword in his hand shot out like lightning.
"Crack!"
A bolt of lightning that completely merged with the sword gleam, or rather, the lightning had completely merged with the sword gleam.
The sword gleam instantly shot out a distance of more than a hundred feet and directly pierced through the ganoderma.
"Crackle …"
As the azure lightning surrounded the ganoderma, the ganoderma began to distort crazily. At the same time, it let out shrill cries.

Chapter 2033
Wisps of dark green miasma drifted out from the twisted ghosts.
The miasma flickered with phosphorescent light, clearly containing deadly poison.
"This is a Soul Nibbling Ganoderma, an extremely ominous item. Even a Perfection Stage expert wouldn't be able to withstand the poisonous miasma. Are you sure you want to pluck it?" the sword mastiff said calmly.
Seeing these ferocious ghosts and hearing the sword mastiff's words, Nan Longhua and the others turned pale.
"The poisonous miasma is drifting over. If you want to die, then stay here." The sword mastiff continued to lead the sword tribe disciples forward.
The other true gods followed behind the sword mastiff.
Dongfang Gui and the others' expressions turned ugly when they saw the ganoderma.
All the wealthy families had collected some information about the Land of Dao Struggle, but they didn't know as much as the sword tribe.
In such a dangerous place, one could die if they weren't careful. If the sword mastiff deliberately played tricks, it would be impossible to guard against it.
"There's a fork in the road ahead. Take the right!"
A huge tree root appeared in front of them. This tree root divided the road into two sides, and the right side led to a narrow cave.
After the sword mastiff finished speaking, it dived in without hesitation …
At this time, no one had a choice. Luo Zheng and Luo Yan also bent down and followed the sword mastiff into the cave.
After shuttling through the cave for a while, they arrived at an open area.
Only then did the scenery around them change …
The originally soft and damp ground had turned into a mottled bluestone road. Although the surroundings were still filled with huge tree roots, one could faintly see some collapsed walls in the gaps between the tree roots. However, those walls could not withstand the erosion of the tree roots and were riddled with holes by the tree roots that grew in all directions.
After walking along the mottled bluestone road for a while, the road ahead became more and more spacious. A faint layer of mist shrouded the area, limiting the gods' vision.
"The bottom of Ruo Mu is actually so spacious …"
Luo Zheng also didn't expect the Land of Dao Struggle to be so spacious.
On second thought, the top of Ruo Mu was also incomparably huge. They couldn't see the end of it. Since Ruo Mu's trunk was so wide, it wasn't surprising that there was such a large space at the bottom.
They hadn't even walked a few miles when they saw an arc on the bluestone road. It extended all the way from the far end and blocked the path of the true gods.
Jian Ao turned his head and indifferently glanced at the crowd. He then said: "After crossing this line, you will arrive at one of the most powerful prohibitions in the Land of Dao Struggle. Depending on your comprehension of the True Meaning of Dao, how far you can go and whether you can survive will depend on your own ability. If you haven't comprehended much True Meaning of Dao, you can wait here."
"My comprehension of the True Meaning of the Dao …"
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered.
Since the Sword Race called this place the Dao Battle Ground, it couldn't be like those forbidden grounds where one could conquer it with their own strength.
In the end, he still had to rely on the True Meaning of the Dao?
It was just that there should be many ways to use the True Intent of the Dao. For example, the True Intent of Dao Technique was completely different from the True Intent of Yin Yang Chaos.
The True Meaning of Good and Evil in One Thought was different …
Why did the skeleton set up such a place for the battle of Dao?
"Brother, you can wait here." Luo Yan blinked and said, "You can follow Yan 'er for the rest of the journey."
Before coming to the Sword Race, Yu Taibai had already informed Luo Yan of some information. Clearly, in Luo Yan's eyes, his brother didn't have the qualifications to continue exploring the Land of Dao Struggle.
"Why can't I go in?" Luo Zheng asked with a smile.
Luo Yan thought for a moment and answered softly: "Because this place requires an extremely deep comprehension of the True Meaning of Dao. Otherwise … it's very easy to get hurt!"
"Extremely deep comprehension? To what extent does one have to comprehend it? "Luo Zheng asked again.
"This …"
Luo Yan couldn't answer for a moment.
After all, in the Land of Dao Struggle, no one had obtained the inheritance of the skeleton.
Those who had entered before had all failed. No one had succeeded.
Even Luo Yan herself wasn't confident. What if the Nirvana Realm expert's requirement was to completely comprehend one type of True Meaning of Dao?
"When I was in the Immortal Mansion, I also studied the True Meaning of Dao." Luo Zheng suddenly smiled and said, "It might not be as good as what Eldest Senior Brother taught me, but it shouldn't be a big problem to enter!"
Even so, Luo Zheng was still somewhat curious.
Could it be that the True Meaning of Dao also had a law that allowed one to understand all other laws? Cultivating one type of True Meaning of Dao would allow one to obtain the recognition of another type of True Meaning of Dao? Thinking about it, it didn't seem very possible …
"Brother, you also cultivated the True Meaning mental cultivation method?" Luo Yan looked at Luo Zheng with a face full of suspicion.
Luo Zheng nodded. "Don't worry, I won't be so rash."
Seeing that Luo Zheng was determined to enter, Luo Yan couldn't say anything. She also knew that she couldn't stop him.
She could only sigh slightly and say: "Then … Brother, you must follow me obediently. I will ensure your safety!"
"Yes!" Luo Zheng nodded with a smile.
On the other side, Jian Ao and the other True Gods had crossed the line.
Luo Zheng and Luo Yan also crossed the line with one step.
When Luo Zheng had just crossed the line, he instantly felt an extremely complicated aura.
There was a strong rhythm contained within this aura, as if two completely different auras were interchanging.
"The True Meaning of Good Will and the True Meaning of Evil Will have merged, and instantly became extremely wonderful …" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
In the previous breath, that True Meaning of Dao could still cause the murderous intent in Luo Zheng's heart to surge violently. In the next breath, all the murderous intent was completely annihilated by the True Meaning of Good Will …
