This state alternated repeatedly and was rather wonderful.
At first, everyone was somewhat unaccustomed to it. Some True Gods even had their faces flushed red. The True Meaning of Good and Evil Will could really affect their emotions. This kind of emotional fluctuation was too great, and it was devastating to the state of mind itself.
Luo Yan was the first to adapt.
In other words, there was no adaptation process for her at all.
When the True Meaning of Evil Will descended, the purple light in her eyes bloomed slightly, and the light in her pupils lengthened accordingly.
When the True Meaning of Good and Evil Will arrived, the purple light in her eyes began to dissipate, and the light in her pupils shortened accordingly …
"Brother, can you adapt?" Luo Yan turned her head to look at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng closed his eyes and sensed for a while. His state of mind had indeed undergone intense fluctuations.
"Dao of Nature …"
"Turning eternal thoughts into eternity, all laws are natural, not comprehending ordinary laws …"
The True Meaning of Good and Evil Will was to divide one's thoughts into two thoughts, good and evil.
In the face of this endless rhythm that affected the state of mind, Luo Zheng also slightly operated a trace of the True Meaning of Nature of Dao.
He did not resist this rhythm. Instead, he followed his nature and went along with it naturally.
When he opened his eyes again, he had already returned to normal …
"You adapted so quickly!" Luo Yan's eyes revealed a strong sense of curiosity.

Chapter 2034
Even Sword Mastiff, who was familiar with this place, took an incense stick's worth of time to completely adapt.
As for the others, they were even slower.
Some grass-roots True Gods were able to reach this place because they had cultivated some kind of True Meaning of Dao.
However, the True Meaning of Dao circulating in the Divine Territory was either incomplete, with only a few hundred words, or was deciphered incorrectly.
In addition, due to their perception, they could not adapt to this constantly moving True Meaning of Good and Evil …
They all sat down cross-legged and tried to adapt, or even resist, or use other methods to deal with it.
However, the aura of this True Meaning of Dao could not be erased by any method. Otherwise, it would not be able to suppress even a Saint in a non-offensive place.
"Ugh!"
An upper True God suddenly vomited and staggered out of the thin line.
Bang!
The other upper True God's eyes rolled back, and he fell to the ground with a plop. He had actually fainted.
At first, it was the True Gods of first-tier clans who withdrew. Later on, even some disciples of wealthy families had no choice but to withdraw.
Entering the Land of Dao Struggle, one had to have a certain level of comprehension of the True Meaning of Dao. Otherwise, it would be difficult to move forward.
However, the True Gods who dared to enter the Land of Dao Struggle were more or less prepared.
After less than an hour, nearly seventy percent of the True Gods had adapted to this place's aura. Even Nan Long Hua was among them.
As for the remaining 30%, they could only wait anxiously outside ….
"Everyone has adapted to the True Meaning of Dao here. Follow me," Sword Mastiff said indifferently, his gaze faintly falling on Luo Zheng.
He felt a small blow to his heart.
This time, in the Land of Dao Struggle, they had adapted to this place's aura in an incense stick's time. It could be said that this had broken his record.
The disciples of the Sword Clan had a natural advantage in the Land of Dao Struggle.
For example, on a normal day, they could fight against the True Meaning of Good and Evil.
There was a training ground above the Ruo Mu. The Sword Clan had dug a large pit on the Ruo Mu. The disciples of the Sword Clan frequently entered and exited. In one breath, they were in a non-offensive place, and in the next breath, they were back in a deadly place. Under this alternating movement, they could simulate the environment where the True Meaning of Good and Evil alternated in the Land of Dao Struggle.
Sword Mastiff was the most hardworking, so he had previously set a record of two incense sticks' time.
This time, his improvement was even greater. The time it took for an incense stick to burn was enough for him to be proud of himself. He originally thought that he was the fastest person.
But when he opened his eyes, he saw the two siblings, Luo Zheng and Luo Yan, talking and laughing as if there was no one else around. This was a bit...
Luo Yan was the first candidate recommended by Yu Taibai. Everyone in the Sword Race attached great importance to this woman.
But her older brother was a mere lower True Divinity, and he hadn't revealed any formidable techniques before, so how could he be so fast?
This pair of siblings, one is more perverted than the other …
He helplessly led the way in front. Judging from Luo Zheng's behavior, it seemed that he had overturned his own judgment.
"Brother Ghost, when do you plan to make a move? If this continues, it'll become more and more troublesome," Dongfang Xi quietly asked as the group moved forward.
Dongfang Gui frowned and shook his head, "Let's wait a little longer. It's not time yet."
"Actually, we have the advantage in numbers. It's not too late to make a move now," Dongfang Xi said.
The Sword Tribe only had a total of 11 people. Including the Luo siblings, there were only 13 of them.
With Dongfang Gui's rallying power, if the upper True Divinities of the Wealthy Clan Alliance all attacked together, they still had a good chance of winning.
"No," Dongfang Gui shook his head and immediately rejected Dongfang Xi's suggestion, "I have my own plans."
Seeing Dongfang Gui's confident appearance, Dongfang Xi could only let out an "oh".
Under the guidance of the sword mastiff, the group advanced for 30 miles. The fog ahead became thicker and thicker …
"Whoosh!"
In the fog, a huge shadow suddenly shot past.
"There's a ferocious creature!" Someone immediately shouted.
"I saw it too. It looks like a pair of wings."
"No, I saw a monster with a pair of long horns …"
What Luo Zheng saw was a person's shadow.
Hearing the other True Divinities' words, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a trace of confusion, "I definitely didn't see wrong, but it seems like everyone saw different things. What's going on?"
Many True Divinities became nervous. The shadow buried deep in the fog indeed brought them great fear …
After all, legend had it that even saints would fall in this place. Upper True Divinities like them were really like ants. If they were careless, they might be trampled to death.
"Don't panic," seeing their nervous expressions, the sword mastiff smiled faintly, "The things in the fog are all formed from the evil in your hearts. They are also the things that you fear the most in the depths of your hearts …"
"Are those all illusory things?" Luo Zheng couldn't help but ask. He faintly felt that there was something wrong with the shadow.
"On the contrary, the powerful evil intent in the Battle of the Dao will almost perfectly recreate your evil intent. Therefore, what we are facing at this stage is our own evil intent. Only by defeating our evil intent and shattering our fear can we pass," the sword mastiff said faintly.
Hearing this, many True Divinities' faces turned pale.
"How is that possible …"
"Perfectly recreate?"
"That's impossible to pass, right?"
Nan Longhua and the other True Divinities' faces were rather ugly.
No matter who it was, they all had their own fears. What they feared would also be viewed as the most evil existence.
If it was a child who had been bitten by a dog, then the thing that the child feared the most was that dog.
If it was a child who came to the Battle of the Dao, what appeared in the fog would be a dog.
However, the Upper True Divinities present were all people who had experienced hundreds of battles. They were all elites of the Divine Territory.
Something that could cause these Upper True Divinities to fear … It was almost certain that it was not something ordinary.
Hearing this, Dongfang Xi and Dongfang Qi's faces didn't look too good. Clearly, they had also seen something that terrified them in the fog.
Dongfang Xi glanced over and saw that Dongfang Gui had a trace of a smile on his face. He asked, "Brother Gui, what did you see?"
Dongfang Gui looked deeply at Dongfang Xi, then said, "These few years, I haven't been in the clan. Do you know where I went?"
"Where … Isn't Brother Gui in seclusion?" Dongfang Xi's face revealed a curious expression.
A few years ago, Dongfang Gui was often active in the Arena of Gods and was ranked first among the Upper True Divinities.
Later, Dongfang Gui suddenly disappeared and his points were emptied. Even the disciples of the Dongfang family didn't know where he went …
It was only when the Battle of the Dao opened that Dongfang Gui suddenly appeared. For a time, Dongfang Xi and the others were rather puzzled. They thought that Dongfang Gui had chosen to go into seclusion these few years. After all, it was very normal to go into seclusion for a few years.

Chapter 2035
It was not a coincidence that the Dongfang Family sent Dongfang Gui.
It was not the first time the Dao Battle was held, and it was not the first time the True Gods of the wealthy families participated.
For the Wealthy Alliance, they only needed to prevent anyone from obtaining the inheritance of the skeleton.
The simplest method was to place an undefeatable monster in the Battle of Dao ….
Dongfang Gui did not go to the Abyss Demon Region to train, but under the guidance of the Paragons, he experienced his greatest fear.
That fear took root in the depths of Dongfang Gui's heart, and then he was sent to participate in this Dao Battle!
Now, the seed of fear had sprouted under the influence of the Dao Battle.
"Heavens, in this fog …" Dongfang Xi's eyes widened as he stared blankly at Dongfang Gui.
Dongfang Gui shook his head helplessly and smiled bitterly. "We will all die."
"You still want me to come?! I want to quit! "Dongfang Xi was furious.
Although they all knew that the Dao Battle was very dangerous, it was clearly a suicide mission. It was too unkind of them to let their own family members come.
"So, before this, the family let each of you keep a fragment of your soul," Dongfang Gui said seriously. "The family nurtured us in the hope that we can repay them one day. This Dao Battle is too important, we must face it head-on."
Dongfang Xi's eyes twitched when he heard this …
"Defeating the fear in your heart is easier said than done."
"That's right. Usually, what you fear will be stronger than you!"
"I was wondering why no one had been able to pass through this Dao Battle for so many years. It's a dead end!"
Many people began to back out.
Not everyone could summon up the courage to face their own fears.
"Naturally, you won't be able to defeat them if you just face them." The sword mastiff smiled faintly. "But the courage in your hearts can also become your greatest help!"
"What do you mean?" Someone asked.
The sword mastiff pointed to the ground.
Only then did everyone see complex lines appear on the ground. These lines formed one unique array pattern after another.
"Using the True Meaning of Dao that you have comprehended, you can create a powerful clone. This is bestowed to you by the Dao Battle," the sword mastiff said calmly.
"A powerful clone?"
"You can do it with your imagination?"
"The deeper your comprehension of the True Meaning of Dao, the more powerful the clone, right?"
The True Gods seemed to have seen a glimmer of hope.
Luo Zheng also showed a look of curiosity. "You can create a doppelganger of anything you imagine?"
Sword Mastiff shook his head. "No, it's the same as fear … That doppelganger is the thing you rely on the most subconsciously, the thing that gives you the greatest sense of security."
"I see." Luo Zheng nodded.
Driven by curiosity, everyone walked towards the center of the array patterns.
Luo Yan smiled at Luo Zheng. His figure fluttered and occupied a formation pattern.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Luo Zheng immediately felt a gentle aura emitting from Luo Yan. Just from this aura, Luo Zheng was unable to determine what kind of True Intent of Dao Luo Yan cultivated. However, this aura was extraordinary, which meant that Luo Yan's comprehension of the True Intent of Dao was not shallow!
A little purple light gathered under Luo Yan's feet …
The light kept pulsing and finally formed the shape of a person.
"The one that Luo Yan trusts the most and gives Luo Yan a sense of security should be Yu Taibai," Luo Zheng thought to himself. The doppelganger was very likely to be Yu Taibai.
When Luo Zheng saw the person's appearance and clothing, he didn't know whether to laugh or cry.
That was actually himself!
This purple doppelganger wasn't the current Luo Zheng, but the Luo Zheng from the Central Domain.
Compared to the current Luo Zheng, the doppelganger was younger, and his eyes were more immature …
This was the image of Luo Zheng in Luo Yan's heart!
In terms of strength, Yu Taibai was naturally much more powerful than Luo Zheng in Luo Yan's heart.
However, ever since Luo Yan had entered the Divine Realm, he hadn't encountered any major dangers. Or, he was hiding in Buzhou Spiritual Mountain, and he was absolutely safe. Therefore, she didn't need to rely on Yu Taibai.
When Luo Yan was in the Central Domain, she had been through a lot of hardships, and her brother was Luo Yan's only hope.
Therefore, in terms of strength, the person she relied on the most was Luo Zheng.
"He really is my brother," Luo Yan said with a smile. "I wonder, between him and big brother, who's stronger? Big brother, do you want to have a friendly match with him? "
The purple Luo Zheng was holding a nameless longsword in his hand. His face was filled with a heroic spirit, and his eyes were sharp. Although his doppelganger was only at the Divine Core realm, as a doppelganger, his actual strength wasn't determined by his cultivation level!
Right after Luo Yan finished speaking, the purple Luo Zheng raised his longsword and thrust it toward Luo Zheng.
"Don't mess around," Luo Zheng said with a smile.

Chapter 2048

........

. thousands of daos, in the end, all lead to the same destination …

Chapter 2049

After the extreme simplification, it also brought about an unsurpassable speed!

Chapter 2050

Chapter 2051

Chapter 2052

Most people still didn't understand Luo Zheng's meaning.

This Land of Battle of Dao was filled with the True Meaning of Good and Evil.
According to the sword mastiff's previous explanation.
After using the True Meaning of Good to test the True God's comprehension of the True Meaning of Dao, he would obtain a powerful clone.
The True Meaning of Evil would directly transform into the fear in the True God's heart and create another fear clone in the Land of Battle of Dao.
One positive and one negative, which was considered normal.
However, from the looks of it now, although this clone was powerful, it was far from being able to unleash its full power.
Or it could be said that it wasn't very useful against those powerful fear clones.
Just now, when facing the snake spirit, all the clones were doing their best to kill the snakes, but many True Gods still fell …
"In the Land of Battle of Dao, Perfect True Gods and Saints can't pass through. As for Upper True Gods … Everyone here should be able to represent the top existences in the Domain of the Gods. Even so, it's difficult for them to pass through. In other words, that almighty person simply doesn't want to find a suitable inheritor!" Luo Zheng said.
Dongfang Gui smiled faintly, "Since that's the case, let's all go back!"

"That's right. Why are you courting death?"

Other than the bean-eyed Luo Yan and Luo Zheng from the Sword Race, almost all the other True Gods agreed with Dongfang Gui and decided to retreat.

Previously, there were people who wanted to return, but Dongfang Gui did not jump out at that time …

As for the Sword Race, Luo Zheng and Luo Yan, they had no choice but to retreat.

He and the Wealthy Clans Alliance had already fallen out. He was even more clear that Dongfang Gui and the others were fated to have ulterior motives. Naturally, he was not afraid of offending them.

Dongfang Gui's expression darkened. The aura within his body had already begun to disperse.

As soon as he finished speaking.
Dongfang Qi, Dongfang Xi, Mu Xian, Fang Jing, Tang Jie, Tang Xu … and the other True Gods from the Wealthy Clans Alliance's aura began to vibrate. At the same time, they all revealed their weapons.
Luo Zheng's words were equivalent to declaring war on them.

The Land of Battle of Dao this time was divided into two groups.

This was an outcome he could not accept no matter what.

Chapter 2053

How could he choose to leave before he completed his mission?

What kind of person was he, Dongfang Gui, to be humiliated by this Luo Zheng!

After the red light emitted by the Hundred-Touch Heavenly Snake stopped for a period of time, everyone started moving forward again.
This time, they didn't follow the path set by the Sword Mastiff, but changed directions according to Luo Zheng's request.
In the beginning, this group was led by the Sword Mastiff. They followed the predetermined path and headed straight for the end of the battlefield. This wedge-shaped path was not complicated.
Right now, Luo Zheng seemed to be the leader of this group.

Now, even he couldn't be sure.

Although the Hundred-Touch Heavenly Snake was powerful, it wasn't invincible.
And that Xing Lin …
Half of his body had been cut off by Luo Zheng's sword, and it looked like his injuries weren't light. Seriously speaking, the battlefield wasn't as difficult as they had imagined. They should still have a chance.

An extremely vicious person like Xing Lin wouldn't bow down to anyone.

They simply didn't have the ability to resist such an existence!

Ever since he was in the universe, Luo Zheng's temperament was like this.
Man proposes, God disposes.
Since he couldn't solve this situation, he could only find another way.
If there was no other way, he would do his best and have a clear conscience!
Although the battlefield was inside the sword race, the sages of the sword race had only explored it twice. Relying on the strength of the sages themselves, they didn't rely on the clone formed by the true meaning of benevolence. Therefore, the sages would ignore the details of the clone.

Now, Luo Zheng was leading the sword race forward. Dongfang Gui's group had no choice but to follow. Those grass-born true gods were even more helpless …
…
…

Accompanied by a burst of lightning and thunder.
Xing Lin appeared in front of Tu 'e.

It could track some true gods for several days and nights. It was very difficult to shake it off!
It was said that after the Nether Eagle tracked down a true god, it would release a strong "death intent". The specific manifestation was that the true god's body would emit waves of black gas.
In the beginning, the black gas wouldn't cause any harm to the true god. However, if it continued for three days, no matter how strong you were, you would die instantly. There was no room for resistance.
Therefore, any true god that was targeted by the Nether Eagle would risk their lives to kill the Nether Eagle. Otherwise, they would be the one to die.
Now, the Evil Demon Tu 'e's shoulder also emitted a raging black gas. However, this Nether Eagle probably didn't have the chance to kill Tu' e. Tu 'e's giant hand gently squeezed. The Nether Eagle let out a shrill cry and was stuffed into Tu' e's giant mouth to chew.

Chapter 2054

For existences like Tu 'e and Xing Lin, once they obtained the chance to be reborn, their ultimate goal was to obtain freedom and not be controlled by others.

At the end of the Battle of Paths.
On the huge iron chain bridge, the long-haired woman was strolling on the iron chain.

Logically speaking, as the embodiment of fear, this woman was even less qualified to cross the iron chain bridge …
Yet, she was able to walk along the chain safely.
After crossing the iron chain bridge, the long-haired woman went into a small cave.
There were countless eyes on the walls of the cave. After she entered, all the eyes stared at the woman.
The long-haired woman's expression was indifferent as she strolled forward.

Following which, she leaped and jumped down.

The deep well was only a hundred feet high. In an instant, she had stepped to the bottom.

The skeleton sat quietly on the spot without any reaction. It was just a pile of dead things.

The long-haired woman couldn't bear it any longer. With a gentle swing of her hair, she wrapped around the skeleton's chest and was about to drag it out …
With this tug, she was afraid that the skeleton would fall apart!
"Buzz …"
At this moment, the skeleton's empty eye sockets suddenly flashed with a pale yellow light, and the bones all over its body creaked.
A pair of skeletal hands quickly grabbed the strand of hair. Then, the skeleton opened slightly, and an ethereal voice came from within. "Don't pull, don't pull. These old bones can't stand being pulled like this!"

The long-haired woman had not shown herself for many Divine Epochs.
There weren't many opportunities to become the target of fear, so she asked directly.
The skeleton fell silent for a moment before replying faintly, "You're such a smart person. You should already have an answer in your heart."
The long-haired woman lowered her head in silence for a while before nodding …
She had not appeared in the Divine Domain for a long time.
There were very few people who knew of her existence …
The only time she met that youth was in the Underground World through the eye core of a spirit crow.

On one side of the deep well, ripples spread out like waves.
On the screen, Luo Zheng was leading a group of people through the fog …

The long-haired woman's eyes were fixed on the screen. There was a trace of sadness between her eyebrows. "I … didn't accompany them for even a day."

Hearing the skeleton's words, the long-haired woman's expression softened. She smiled faintly. "Although you've already stepped into the Nirvana Realm and reached a higher level of life, you may not be smarter than my husband. He sees further than you!"

We've fallen for too long and have already reached the deepest part of the Chaos. This ship has already been targeted by the Extraterrestrial Demons. "

How could they fight against that Great Demon?

"I didn't kill it just now!"

Chapter 2055
After the long-haired woman left, the skeleton returned to his original appearance.

The Giant Mountain Ape was extremely strong and had an extremely strong vitality.

The hair all over its body stood up like spikes. A terrifying energy seemed to be about to explode from the Giant Mountain Ape's body.

The Giant Mountain Ape lost its life and became an inanimate object.

The "entangle" technique only held the Giant Mountain Ape's huge head in place. It would not harm the Giant Mountain Ape.
As for the "explode" technique, it would cause a violent explosion in a fixed place. The explosion would be in the Giant Mountain Ape's head. That was why the Giant Mountain Ape's head was blown apart.
Although these two techniques were bizarre, they were still acceptable.
However, the "Annihilation" technique could cause the Giant Mountain Ape to lose its life. Wasn't this too domineering?

The Giant Mountain Ape's body was strong, but its soul was not particularly strong among Dire Beasts. Even so, Luo Yan could not destroy it.
It was only after she used the "Explosion" technique to damage the Giant Mountain Ape's mind and severely injure its soul that the "Annihilation" technique took effect.
If she was facing some living thing with a weak soul, she could control its life and death with a single "Annihilation"!

Chapter 2056
It was obvious that there was a power that separated the fog and showed them a path.

"What unique Sanskrit!"

Even Luo Zheng took out the rubbing stones from the Famous Sword God City from his Sumeru Ring and began to copy the Sanskrit characters on the stone tablet.

Almost in the blink of an eye, from where the aura rose, a blazing sword light shot out.
This sword light seemed to be able to split the heavens and the earth, and in the vast Divine Territory, almost nothing could block it.
"Phew …"
Like a light breeze, it swept past.
Tu 'e felt his right shoulder lighten.
"Boom!"

Chapter 2069
Luo Zheng stood not far away, holding a light green longsword tightly. His eyes were emotionless as he looked at Tu 'e, gasping for breath.
That longsword was only an ordinary primordial treasure. It could not withstand the power of the Dao-infused sword …
By the time he slashed out this sword light, the longsword was already riddled with holes.

Luo Zheng could not use this Dao-infused sword continuously.
When he was facing Xing Lin earlier, in order to save Luo Yan, Luo Zheng did not hesitate to slash out twice!

He was like the longsword in his hand that was full of holes …

Xing Lin had already hidden far away, looking at Luo Zheng in the distance.
Previously, he was injured by Luo Zheng's sword!
Previously, Xing Lin did not help Tu 'e because this sword had too much of an impact on him. Moreover, he submitted to Tu' e only because of Tu 'e's strength.
If this group of upper-level True Gods could really create a miracle and kill Tu 'e, Xing Lin would be happy to reap the benefits.
Therefore, when the two sides exchanged blows, he had already retreated far away …
"This sword …"
Dongfang Gui's astonished expression froze on his face.
As an anonymous person, Luo Zheng's results in the Arena of Gods were indeed amazing.
After all, he had created a record of consecutive victories in cross-level battles!
Such a genius was extremely rare even in the rich and powerful families. At least, Luo Zheng's results were unparalleled. Even those Saints could not create such terrifying results when they were at the True God Realm.
Even so, it was impossible to slash out such a terrifying sword …
This was already illogical.
That sword earlier was sufficient to slay a grand completion True God!
Seeing that Luo Yan could actually fight Tu 'e head-on, he was shocked.
What kind of people were this pair of siblings from the Luo Clan?

At that time, Dongfang Gui didn't think much of it. But now, Dongfang Gui finally felt it!
The Sword Mastiff's face was covered with a perverted ruddy color. He didn't feel any fear after being saved by Luo Zheng's sword. Instead, he was incomparably excited.

The most shocked people were not Dongfang Gui, but the two Saints of the Sword Race in the Sword Transformation Divine City.
The Sword Race's greatest pursuit was the ultimate Sword Dao.
In the entire God Realm, the four Sages of the Sword Tribe were the most dedicated in researching and cultivating the Sword Dao.
Jian Leiting and Jian Wuhen were two of the four Sages ….

Jian Leiting's eyes flickered with an intense radiance.

There were many ways to affect the might of Luo Zheng's sword …
For example, Luo Zheng was merely a Lesser True God.
For example, that green sword was an ordinary Grandmist Spiritual Treasure no matter how one looked at it.
For example, the Divine Dao …
Although the Emotion Severing Divine Dao was powerful, it was impossible for it to be this powerful.
While the two old men were shocked, they both looked at Yu Taibai.
Yu Taibai's expression didn't change much.
He knew that Divine Daos could be fused. He also knew that his master's Immeasurable Ruler was in Luo Zheng's hands.
Luo Zheng had been in the Realm of the Gods for a period of time. It wasn't difficult for him to discover the Immeasurable Ruler. However, fusing Divine Daos … that wasn't something that Luo Zheng could do at his current level.
Although Yu Taibai was very surprised by Luo Zheng's performance, he was more concerned about how Luo Zheng and Luo Yan were going to get out of this predicament!
"That's the fused energy of the Divine Daos. I reckon there are several hundred types of Divine Daos fused into one," Yu Taibai explained indifferently.
There was nothing to hide now. Furthermore, if they really obtained the inheritance, the Sword Race would completely stand on the side of the Luo Family.
"Several hundred types of Divine Daos? He comprehended several hundred types of Divine Daos? "Jian Wuhen's eyes flickered with a strange radiance.
"Luo Zheng did use several different types of Divine Daos in the Sea of Time Forbidden Area. Back then, I was very puzzled. I didn't expect him to be able to use several hundred types of Divine Daos. However, fusing Divine Daos is not an easy task!" Jian Leiting couldn't help but say.
There were always some exceptional geniuses in the Realm of the Gods that cultivated two or even three types of Divine Daos. However, fusing all these Divine Daos was not an easy task.
Only a very small number of people succeeded.
They even comprehended Extra-Dao Divine Powers after fusing Divine Daos …
However, their Extra-Dao Divine Powers could not be inherited because there were too few True Gods who were qualified to fuse Divine Daos.
Jian Leiting and Jian Wuhen were very clear about this matter.
However, now that Yu Taibai had jumped out and told them that Luo Zheng had fused several hundred types of Divine Daos, the two Sages found it very hard to understand.
"I'm not sure either," Yu Taibai said with a solemn expression. "What I'm concerned about now is whether they can leave the Battle of Dao alive."
Right now, he wasn't in the mood to discuss the fusion of the Divine Daos. He simply opened his eyes wide and relied on the Clear Vision Technique to observe every movement in the Battle of Dao. Energy constantly circulated in one of his hands as he calculated every possibility in the Battle of Dao.
Although Tu 'e was at a disadvantage now and had even lost an arm to Luo Zheng, he was still at a disadvantage.
With the strength displayed by Luo Yan and Luo Zheng, as well as the twelve members of the Sword Race, the possibility of defeating Tu 'e was still zero …
…
Tu 'e, who had lost an arm, became silent.
He grabbed the arm and connected it to his severed arm. Countless granules appeared on his arm and the wound on his severed arm healed in the blink of an eye.
Countless red lightning bolts appeared all over his body and enveloped him layer by layer.
Tu 'e, who had always been unruly, lost his temper because of this group of little fellows.
After a few breaths of silence, he finally began to calm down.
At least, he no longer treated this group of ants as "ants". Instead, he treated them as serious opponents and squeezed out a trace of "seriousness".
"Bzzt bzzt bzzt …"
Countless red lightning bolts escaped from his red skin.
Then, he took a step forward …
With just one step, he disappeared into thin air.

Tu 'e appeared above Luo Zheng.
Like an unscalable mountain, he was wrapped in endless red lightning bolts and completely enveloped Luo Zheng in an area of ten thousand feet.
"Die!"
Tu 'e's eyes were surging with murderous intent as he lamented his own stupidity.

Chapter 2070
Previously, Tu 'e had been toying with Luo Zheng, wanting to stun her with his bare hands.

When Wa Ying completely gained the upper hand, she would directly replace Luo Yan and become the master of Luo Yan's body.
At that time, the Luo Yan would become a completely different kind of creature.

Luo Yan, who was lying on the ground, was surprisingly calm. She looked sideways at Lightning, who was not far away, and curled her lips …
"It's a pity that I can't even see my brother now …" she muttered.
Nan Longhua, who was below the green stone tablet, sighed when she saw this scene. "If such a monstrous pair of siblings is going to die here, I guess I won't be able to escape either …"
Even so, Nan Longhua did not regret her choice.
Even if he escaped through the fog, he would most likely die.
How many of those True Gods who were fleeing in all directions would be able to safely leave the Dao Struggle Grounds?
Just when everyone thought that Luo Zheng was dead for sure …
A figure was running wildly in the blood-red lightning!

A golden light appeared on Luo Zheng's body.
"Absolute Beginning's Promise!"
Even Tu 'e's face was full of astonishment.
Absolute Beginning's Promise could ignore the erosion of all energy in the Divine Plane.

"Whoosh!"
After Luo Zheng rushed out, he almost exploded with all the strength in his body, and ran directly toward Luo Yan.
At the same time, he had a thought.
The doppelganger he summoned, Xun, waved his long spear and leaped up, jumping continuously on Tu 'e's body at an extremely fast speed!
Compared to Tu 'e's huge body, Xun's body was like a small flea.
However, in the blink of an eye, Xun had already jumped in front of Tu 'e, and the long spear in his hand was suddenly thrown out!
"Puff!"
Tu 'e was slightly stunned when the spear hit him in the eye.
"Go!"
Luo Zheng picked up Luo Yan who was on the ground.
He did not have time to choose a direction and could only run wildly forward …

If they could protect the Luo siblings here, Jian Ao and the rest's sacrifice would be worth it.
Under Jian Ao's lead, the twelve disciples of the Sword Tribe once again transformed into a giant sword and shot toward Tu 'e.
"Scram!"
Tu 'e was really angry now.
He slapped Xun's body and sent him flying.
Tu 'e did not even look at the disciples of the Sword Tribe who were rushing toward him. His figure flashed, and he disappeared into thin air again.

Luo Zheng carried Luo Yan and rushed for about five kilometers before Tu 'e's figure appeared in front of him like an insurmountable mountain.
"You're even more tenacious than the Dead Soul Worms in the Abyss of Demons." Tu 'e looked coldly at Luo Zheng.
Carrying Luo Yan, Luo Zheng began to retreat continuously. His state of mind had already sunk to the bottom of the valley.
It was still too difficult to fight with a demon comparable to a Quasi-sage.
He still had two Promises of the Beginning, but Luo Yan was unable to resist the blood-colored lightning. Would he still die here in the end?
"Brother." Luo Yan's lips were already pale. She forced herself to stay alert and reached out to caress Luo Zheng's face. "You've already done your best. Don't struggle anymore …"

He was different from Luo Yan. He had experienced this kind of desperate situation more than once. Even if it was a hopeless situation where death was certain, he would never give up.
"Your sister is right." Dongfang Gui's voice came faintly from behind Luo Zheng. "A futile struggle is meaningless. Welcoming death is your final destination!"
Dongfang Gui already knew that he could not escape death. Unexpectedly, he followed without fear.
Luo Zheng glanced indifferently at Dongfang Gui and sneered. "You're twisted like a maggot. Do you think everyone in the world is the same as you?"
Tu 'e laughed when he heard Luo Zheng's words. "Your so-called gods are no different from maggots in my eyes!"
Dongfang Gui sneered. "Did you hear that? Who do you think you are? What right do you have to pretend to be more noble than me? Hahaha … "
That wild laughter was extremely ear-piercing. Luo Zheng's face turned ashen.
Dongfang Gui laughed halfway.
A faint female voice suddenly floated out from the thick fog.
"Why? Because he's my son … "

Chapter 2071
It was very quiet in the fog, so the voice that suddenly drifted out was very clear.

…
Yu Taibai never thought that a savior would appear.

The Fear Incarnation had its own will and could control its own actions!
Although Master's wife's strength had been greatly reduced, it was still not a problem for her to deal with a demon.
His heart, which had been hanging high in the air, finally calmed down …
…
Luo Zheng stared blankly at the long-haired woman.
When he first entered the Dao Battle, he saw a blurry figure.
He didn't expect it to be this woman!
When he killed that monster in the Tie Family's underground, he obtained a piece of mud and a small eyeball.
Through that eyeball, he saw this woman's head!
This head looked exactly the same as Luo Yan's …
That face had reverberated in his mind for a long time.
She became my fear incarnation?
She came to the Dao Battle?
Moreover, she's my mother?
The thoughts in Luo Zheng's mind were in chaos.
In his arms, Luo Yan raised her head and looked over with difficulty.
Although she looked sickly, when she saw the woman with the same appearance as her, her eyes flashed with a trace of gentleness. "Mother … are you really my mother …"
The long-haired woman looked at Luo Zheng and Luo Yan. Her pair of bewitching red eyes also flashed with a trace of gentleness.
When she heard Luo Yan's question, she couldn't help but nod her head.
Whoosh!
Almost in the blink of an eye, the long-haired woman had already arrived in front of Luo Zheng. She reached out to touch Luo Yan's forehead.
But Luo Zheng was very vigilant.
Exerting strength in his feet, he quickly retreated backward. He stared at the long-haired woman with a vigilant expression.
He wasn't as naive as Luo Yan.
Even if she looked like his mother, she might not really be his mother.
Although Luo Zheng had never seen his mother's appearance since he was a child, in his imagination, his mother was always gentle and benevolent.
No matter how he looked at this woman emitting a bewitching aura, she didn't look like a good person. She was too different from the image in his imagination.
Moreover, in the underground world …
Luo Zheng's heart was filled with fear.
How could he let this woman get close to Luo Yan?
Seeing Luo Zheng avoiding her like this, the long-haired woman's eyes revealed a trace of disappointment and grief. But she still stared at Luo Yan and said, "Yan 'er hasn't matured yet. Activating the infant Wah's Shadow will backfire and cause her to lose her mind. That's fatal … I, I can save her."
Luo Zheng's heart jumped slightly. He really did feel that the aura of Luo Yan's soul was declining.
"Wah's Shadow? You mean that purple shadow? "Luo Zheng asked.
The long-haired woman nodded. "Yes!"
Luo Zheng relaxed a bit. In order to save Luo Yan, he had to take some risks. But he raised his head and glanced at Tue …
This powerful demon was still here. Was this long-haired woman planning to save Luo Yan here?
"But that guy is still here …" Luo Zheng said.
From the beginning to the end, she didn't seem to care about Tue at all. She completely treated this mountain-sized demon as air.
Hearing her son's words, her eyebrows knitted slightly and she smiled. "That's right. Yan 'er used Wah's Shadow because of this guy, right? Then … "
Buzz buzz buzz …
Suddenly, that hundred feet long hair was plated with a layer of bewitching red light. Her figure flashed, and all the strands of hair attacked Tue like claws and fangs!
"Woman, you …"

Those hundred feet long strands of hair actually extended infinitely at this moment and tightly wrapped around Tue, who was more than ten thousand feet tall!

Chapter 2072
Sword Mastiff and the rest were rather far away and did not hear what the woman said.

"Mm."
Luo Yan snorted and his body suddenly trembled.
His originally rapid breathing gradually calmed down, as if he had fallen into a deep sleep.
The long-haired woman probably felt that she hadn't done enough. She pinched her fingers together and emitted a soft yellow light. She was probably comforting Luo Yan's soul.
Looking at the long-haired woman and then looking at Luo Yan's face, Luo Zheng felt both strange and emotional.
These two women looked almost exactly the same, like twins. But she was actually his mother …
After treating him, the long-haired woman stroked Luo Yan's face again, her eyes full of tenderness.
Then she slowly stood up and gently breathed a sigh of relief.
Her beautiful eyes looked at Luo Zheng again. Her complicated expression turned into spirals, constantly rotating in her pupils.
Luo Zheng's mood was also very complicated.
Mother was a strange word to him. He didn't expect to meet her under such circumstances.

The long-haired woman slowly walked towards him …
The shiny black hair spread to the sides like satin, encircling him in the middle.
"Can I hug you?" the long-haired woman asked.
Luo Zheng originally wanted to refuse, but when he saw the long-haired woman's longing expression, he subconsciously nodded.
Then the long-haired woman stepped forward and hugged him. She choked with sobs and said, "I've been waiting for this day for a long, long time. I thought I would have to continue waiting …"
She was just the incarnation of fear.
These memories wouldn't be retained in her body, but this incarnation inherited all of them. Naturally, she also had all of her emotions and self-awareness.
"Why is it a long time? Luo Yan and I just …"
According to the flow of time in the God Realm, Luo Zheng had only been born for a few years.
"A long time ago, you were placed in the Great Evolution Universe. It's just that your father arranged for you to grow up in this Evolution Era," the long-haired woman said softly.
In the Great Evolution Universe, the first ten Evolution Eras were used for experiments. Only in this Evolution Era, the two seeds of hope, Luo Zheng and Luo Yan, were finally "hatched" and began to grow healthily.
The long-haired woman shook her head. "Now it seems that your father's arrangements weren't necessarily correct. Zheng 'er, your luck is too good. If I hadn't become the incarnation of fear and descended on the Dao Battle, today I'm afraid …"
She looked like a woman who would kill without hesitation.
However, she couldn't accept Luo Zheng and Luo Yan taking such a huge risk. From her words, she seemed to be very dissatisfied with Luo Xiao and Yu Taibai's arrangements.
At Luo Zheng's side, she was like an ordinary mother who had been wronged and poured out her grievances.
Although this woman was very unfamiliar to Luo Zheng, when he heard her speak softly, Luo Zheng felt a heartfelt sense of intimacy.
"It's been so long, but it's also passed so quickly. In the blink of an eye, you've already reached the God Realm. You're already strong enough to hold your own," she said with gratification. Then she raised a finger. "I don't want to forget what happened here. Help me preserve the memories here. When you see me again, return them to me."

Chapter 2073
The cocoon of light instantly entered Luo Zheng's glabella and disappeared without a trace.
After being planted with the cocoon of light, Luo Zheng felt a strange feeling in his heart.
Previously, Shi Xiaoqiao had planted a petal on him. This time, she had planted a cocoon of light.
Could it be that the cocoon of light had recorded all of his actions?
However, when he saw the long-haired woman retract her hand and reveal a gratified smile, he felt a sense of security.
Perhaps this was the feeling of home?
"Let's go. I'll take you to activate Elder Yuan's inheritance." As the long-haired woman spoke, she took the initiative to pick up Luo Yan.
At that moment, the sword mastiff and the others rushed over.
Although the long-haired woman had killed Tu 'e, the sword mastiff still did not understand the reason.
In the eyes of the sword mastiff, any manifestation of fear was an enemy. This long-haired woman gave off a bewitching and ominous feeling. Could she be a demonic being that bewitched people?
"Luo Zheng, who is she?" the sword mastiff asked nervously.
Speaking of which …
This woman could kill Tu 'e with a raise of her hand.
Even if he resisted, it would be meaningless. However, how could the sword mastiff be at ease if he did not ask clearly?
He had summoned the Calamity Sword. He would die eventually, but he still hoped that Luo Yan and Luo Zheng could escape this calamity and obtain the inheritance of the Dao Struggle.
"She …"
Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment, not knowing how to answer.
The long-haired woman gently bit her lip. Her bewitching red eyes stared blankly at Luo Zheng, faintly revealing a look of anticipation.
She naturally hoped that Luo Zheng would acknowledge her …
Luo Zheng and the long-haired woman's eyes met. His heart skipped a beat as he said, "She's my mother."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the long-haired woman's eyes narrowed slightly as she revealed a smile.
"Mother?" The sword mastiff was stunned.
The sword race disciples also looked at each other.
This matter was too unbelievable. After all, the manifestations of fear in the Dao Struggle were all unique and abnormally terrifying monsters.
How could Luo Zheng meet his mother here?
No matter how coincidental it was, it could not be so coincidental!
No, she was probably a demonic being that could bewitch people. Perhaps this woman came from the Soul Wilderness. There were indeed some powerful existences that could do this!
Seeing the Sword Mastiff's face full of doubt and disbelief, Luo Zheng couldn't help but explain, "Actually, it's not difficult to understand. There was a period of time when she became the thing I feared … At that time, I didn't know that she was my mother, so …"
With such an explanation, the sword mastiff and the disciples of the Sword Race came to a sudden realization. However, it left them speechless.
The Dongfang family was willing to pay a heavy price and used Dongfang Gui to summon a demon.
Luo Zheng was even weirder. He even called out his mother …
Jian Ao couldn't help but sigh in his heart. Other than Luo Yan, who was a little more normal, the rest of the Luo Family seemed to be a little weird.
"So that's how it is. That's good." Jian Ao forced a smile. "Congratulations, it looks like you'll be able to obtain the inheritance of Dao Struggle."
The disciples of the Sword Clan had completed their mission, but their lives were about to come to an end …
The blood-red patterns on the Disaster Sword in the Sword Mastiff's hand were shrinking bit by bit. Now, only the tip of the sword was left, about one-fifth of it.
When these blood-red patterns were completely extinguished, their lives would also come to an end.
Because of Jian Ao's words, the faces of the other eleven disciples of the Sword Clan dimmed.
In order to risk their lives, they had sacrificed their lives to summon the Disaster Sword. Now, it was time for them to suffer the consequences.
Although they were mentally prepared, how could life and death be so easy to face?
"What's wrong?"
Seeing the expressions of Jian Ao and the others, Luo Zheng was somewhat baffled.
His gaze fell on the longswords in their hands. Speaking of which, the strength displayed by the twelve disciples of the Sword Clan was indeed powerful. They could actually injure Tu 'e twice. Could it be related to the swords in their hands?
Seeing this, the long-haired woman smiled faintly. Then, she handed the sleeping Luo Yan to Luo Zheng.
After Luo Zheng was done, she walked towards Jian Ao and reached out to grab the Disaster Sword in his hand. She said faintly, "The Disaster Sword in your hands is formed from the tides of fortune. Moreover, it's the tide of misfortune. After the red light dissipates, you will bear the tribulations of three disasters and three calamities. However, most people can't even pass the first disaster. Even if you pass the first disaster, there will be the second, third, and three calamities. There's no doubt you'll die."
"What?" Luo Zheng's pupils dilated slightly. This was equivalent to paying the price of one's life.
Jian Ao smiled bleakly and pulled the Disaster Sword back from the long-haired woman's hand. "Before we used the Disaster Sword, we already knew the consequences. This is our fate. I'm afraid it can't be changed …"
The other disciples of the Sword Clan all lowered their heads. Their faces were filled with grief.
Once the Disaster Sword was used, even a Saint wouldn't be able to save it. This was the consensus of the Sword Clan.
"I remember that this Disaster Sword was created by a genius of the Sword Clan. In reality, it's a sword of misfortune formed from the Eternal Sword Intent. There's no solution to this misfortune. However, it can be transferred. I can transfer the tribulations to someone else," the long-haired woman said faintly.
"Transfer?"
Jian Ao's heart thumped wildly.
Jian Ao didn't know where Luo Zheng's mother came from. However, such a person might have some abilities that even a Saint didn't know about!
"It can be transferred?"
"Does that mean we still have a chance to live?"
"Impossible. Even Master Wu Heng can't do that …"
The disciples of the Sword Clan all had hopeful expressions.
"But who can it be transferred to?" Mo Nan suddenly said.
Once this was said, everyone fell silent.
Who would be willing to sacrifice themselves and bear the disaster of others?
However, Luo Zheng suddenly turned around and looked at someone not far away …
Everyone else followed Luo Zheng's gaze and looked over as well.
In the distance, Dongfang Gui didn't seem to have recovered from his great shock.
At this moment, his face was lifeless and his hair was disheveled. He didn't have a trace of the aura of an upper True God.
He stared blankly at the mountain of flesh and bones in front of him. His face was filled with grief. It was as if Tu E was his masterpiece and this perfect masterpiece had been destroyed by someone.
"Dongfang Gui!"
"Transfer it to him!"
"Yes! Capture him! "
The disciples of the Sword Clan looked at Dongfang Gui as if they had seen their savior.
Dongfang Gui was still in a daze when he suddenly saw the disciples of the Sword Clan charging at him as if they had been injected with chicken blood. He was shocked and wanted to escape.
However, he had been flicked by Tu E's finger and was heavily injured.
The disciples of the Sword Clan were still using their Disaster Swords. How could Dongfang Gui escape?
Soon, the disciples of the Sword Clan tied Dongfang Gui up and carried him in front of the long-haired woman.
Jian Ao asked worriedly, "Can the tribulations be transferred to a single person?"
"Yes, you need to stab this sword into his body. However, don't kill him," the long-haired woman said faintly.
The disciples of the Sword Clan all looked at Dongfang Gui with malicious gazes.
"What, what do you want to do!" Dongfang Gui realized that something was wrong.

Chapter 2074
It was all thanks to Dongfang Gui that everyone had fallen to such a state. Naturally, they did not hesitate in the slightest.
"Puff, puff, puff …"
They all stabbed their Calamity Swords into Dongfang Gui's body.
The sword techniques of the disciples of the Sword clan were naturally extremely brilliant. Although the Calamity Swords had pierced through his body, they had all avoided Dongfang Gui's vitals. He did not even bleed much.
However, during this process, Dongfang Gui also tried to resist.
His only method of resistance was to self-destruct. However, just as he was about to self-destruct, the long-haired woman discovered his intentions.
He felt a slight pain in his abdomen, and a thin strand of hair pierced through his dantian. Dongfang Gui immediately lost his connection with his dantian, and was unable to use any means of self-preservation to end his life.
The twelve swords pierced Dongfang Gui's body, making him look like a hedgehog.
He did not know what these people wanted to do, and in his despair, he had already given up resisting.
Next, the long-haired woman gently waved her hands and formed a hand seal. A peculiar aura burst out from it.
"This momentum …" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
Back then, when he faced the calamity of the Fate Destroying Phoenix Snake, he'd once felt it …
In the beginning, Luo Zheng had an unwavering belief in luck!
However, Hua Tianming's words later made him doubt the concept of luck.
Later on, he found out that everything was arranged by his father, so he completely rejected the idea of luck. It turned out that everything was just a predetermined arrangement.
"The so-called misfortune is just a special kind of energy, right? Or are the three disasters just the backlash from the Calamity Sword? "Luo Zheng could not help but ask.
Unexpectedly, she shook her head. "No, this is the punishment of fate."
"Does luck really exist?" Luo Zheng asked.
The long-haired woman's hand seals kept changing. Waves of aura flowed into the twelve swords. She wanted to transfer the Three Calamities and Three Disasters to Dongfang Gui.
At the same time, she earnestly answered Luo Zheng's question, "Of course it does. In God's Domain, this is a kind of authority."
"This is also a kind of authority?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
The long-haired woman looked at her son tenderly. "Of course, it's about authority. If you take out enough shenwu coins, you can also change your destiny on the tablet. That's not too difficult … the real trouble is this."
She pointed at the Calamity Swords and continued, "The Calamity Swords formed from the Sword Cloud's Eternal True Intent can change the Fate of even higher levels. Therefore, the Saints of the Divine Realm are destined to be powerless, and there is no solution."
"A higher level? Mother … Are you talking about creatures above their level? "Luo Zheng was taken aback.
Whenever she heard Luo Zheng call her mother, her face would always show a trace of joy, and she didn't try to hide it at all.
"You could say that. However, this Sword of Calamity isn't complete. If it was a Sword of Calamity released by a Nirvana Realm expert, it wouldn't be so easy to move it," the long-haired woman replied.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng more or less understood.
No matter how talented the Sword Tribe's disciple was, the Sword Cloud's Eternal True Intent he comprehended couldn't be complete. Of course, this Sword of Calamity wasn't that difficult to break.
However, the inheritance of the True Intent of Dao came from a creature of a higher level. It came from the vast Chaotic World outside the Divine Realm.
The creatures of the Divine Realm were unsolvable. Even a Saint's great ability couldn't break it.
If his mother could do it, did that mean she came from outside the Divine Realm?
Or did she also have the strength of a Nirvana Realm expert?
Since she had such strength, why did she hide in the underground world?
Luo Zheng's heart was full of questions.
Seeing how his mother seemed to answer everything, he really should ask her properly.
However, he had to wait until she moved the Calamity Swords' Calamity Swords first …
She crossed her hands and kept flicking her fingers on the twelve Calamity Swords.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
As the Calamity Swords moved, Dongfang Gui's face showed pain.
That formless aura flowed into Dongfang Gui's body like a tide.
After a short while …
That imposing aura completely dissipated.
As for Dongfang Gui's glabella, it was already pitch-black. Within that pitch-black patch, there was an indescribable and unknown energy hidden within.
"You can pull out your swords now," the long-haired woman said indifferently after stopping.
To be honest, she didn't care about the lives of anyone here. Whether it was Dongfang Gui or Sword Mastiff, they were like floating clouds in her eyes.
However, she noticed Luo Zheng's emotions.
If these disciples of the Sword Tribe died, Luo Zheng would probably blame himself. As his mother, she naturally didn't hesitate to act.
When Sword Mastiff and the others pulled out their Calamity Swords, the blood-red light on them had completely disappeared. At the same time, they felt their bodies relax. The formless pressure on them had completely disappeared!
The group's expression changed when they looked at her.
The Calamity Swords were unsolvable. This was a publicly acknowledged fact in the Sword Tribe.
The Four Sages of the Sword Tribe were also unable to break it. However, this long-haired woman had broken it.
They had escaped with their lives and successfully completed their mission. Right now, they were bursting with joy!
"Let's go."
She didn't look at them and left with Luo Zheng.
While the disciples of the Sword Tribe were happy, they also felt the long-haired woman's distant aura. It seemed she didn't want them to come close.
They could only follow behind her three hundred meter long hair from a distance. They didn't dare to get too close.
Only Dongfang Gui was left behind …
"She's letting me go just like that?"
Dongfang Gui stared blankly at the group of people leaving. He still didn't dare to believe it.
Not long after they left, Dongfang Gui sensed that something was wrong. It was as if some kind of formless ghost was slowly approaching.
Soon …
Luo Zheng and the others heard Dongfang Gui's mournful screams from behind them!
Such a miserable sound, even from a distance, was very frightening.
Luo Zheng, Sword Mastiff, and Mo Nan turned around when they heard the scream. They only saw a cloud of black smoke enveloping his body.
Only the heavens knew what kind of torture he suffered in that black smoke …
Luo Zheng didn't feel the slightest bit of pity for Dongfang Gui's fate. After all, he had almost killed him and Luo Yan.
He walked to his mother's side and suddenly thought of a problem. He asked a strange question, "Mother … I still don't know your name!"
It was very awkward to not even know his mother's surname and name.
"My surname is Li …" She shook her head and smiled, "Actually, Li isn't a surname. Zheng 'er, have you heard of the term' Li Min Sheng '?"
"Of course I've heard of it," Luo Zheng replied.
In the universe, mortals were called 'Li Min Sheng'.
Even in the Divine Realm, 'Li Min Sheng' referred to the Divine Citizens.

Chapter 2075
"Li people … There's actually such a meaning." Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a look of fascination.
She nodded. "Mother's original name is Luo Shui."
Among the Li people, they naturally would not call each other Li.
However, in the Divine Territory, those who knew her name would still call her Li Luoshui, or the woman with the surname Li.
"Luo Shui?" Luo Zheng was stunned, then asked, "I think there's a river in the Divine Territory called Luo Shui."
Luo Zheng did not have a thorough understanding of the Divine Realm, but coincidentally, he just happened to collect a set of the "Luo River Seven Brahma Characters" in Famed Sword God City, so he naturally had some understanding of the Luo River, a river that spanned three regions. This Luo River was already an extremely ancient river in the Divine Realm, and was also the longest river.
Hearing this, Li Luoshui smiled faintly, "Because that river was named after Mother!"
"Named after Mother …" Luo Zheng was somewhat speechless.
Looking at Luo Zheng's expression, Li Luoshui's face was full of gentleness. She said again, "Zheng 'er, do you have anything else to ask?"
"Yes!" Luo Zheng's tone suddenly became heavier.
Until now, there were many things that Luo Zheng could not see through the fog.
There were too many things that he wanted to know …
"Why did Mother hide underground?" Luo Zheng asked.
Li Luoshui looked into the distance, her face slightly gloomy. "Of course, it was to avoid certain people."
"Those Saints from the Wealthy Alliance? Dongfang Chunjun? "Luo Zheng frowned and asked.
Li Luoshui smiled faintly. "Dongfang Chunjun can be considered half of one for now. As for those Saints, they are not …"

However, thinking about it carefully, with Mother's current state, she could easily kill Tue, who was comparable to a Quasi-sage.
Perhaps those Saints were really nothing in front of Mother.
"Since the birth of the Divine Territory, there were only two people who could truly be called geniuses in Mother's eyes. One was your father, and the other was Dongfang Chunjun." Li Luoshui smiled. "Both of them could enter the Nirvana Realm with their own abilities. At that time, Dongfang Chunjun should have already crossed the Sea of True Meaning. If I'm not wrong, he should have already stepped into the Nirvana Realm … "
Hearing this, a light flashed in Luo Zheng's eyes.
Half step into the Nirvana Realm …
No wonder Dongfang Chunjun had such charisma in the Divine Realm. He had actually cultivated to such a level.
It was said that the Nirvana Realm expert in the Battle of the Dao was only a skeleton. Just the True Will of the Dao emitted from the skeleton was already so powerful. If a true Nirvana Realm expert were to descend, how terrifying would it be?
"It's a pity that your father was too impulsive back then." When Li Luoshui mentioned Luo Xiao, a hint of longing flashed across her face, "His talent was slightly stronger than Dongfang Chunjun's. If he didn't fall, I'm afraid he would have achieved the Nirvana Realm by now."
Luo Zheng was silent.
. of Father's death.....
Fortunately, the Great Yan Universe was not destroyed. At least there was still a chance for his father to be reborn.
Mother was hidden deep underground and never saw the light of day.
Only her head was left.
How could he, as a son, bear with his parents like this?
Looking at the peaceful face of Luo Yan, who was sleeping soundly in his arms, his shoulders became heavy for no reason.
Luo Zheng finally understood why Xingwei, the old patriarch, and even Hua Tianming were unwilling to tell him the truth in the Great Yan Universe. Even when he met Yu Taibai and Luo Yan in the Divine Territory, they still had to hide it from him …
The things he faced were too difficult.
"I'm sorry …" Li Luoshui suddenly said.
Luo Zheng looked at his mother with a hint of doubt in his eyes.
"This burden is too heavy. It's my fault as a mother." Li Luoshui lowered her head and blamed herself.
She was a very sensitive person. Naturally, she noticed Luo Zheng's emotions.
Who would be willing to let their children experience such suffering to save themselves?
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly twisted. A strong aura was released. He looked at Li Luoshui and said, "As a son, if I can't bear this little burden when my parents are in trouble, how can I, Luo Zheng, have the face to stand tall in this world?"
Li Luoshui looked at Luo Zheng's serious appearance and suddenly froze.
She looked at him with a complicated expression for a while. Then she said with relief, "Zheng 'er … you've really grown up!"
Saying this, she smiled sweetly at Luo Zheng. Whether it was her appearance or her attitude, she was like a young girl. However, she did not look like a mother.
The two walked side by side. Luo Zheng did not get to the bottom of it. He was afraid that his mother would blame herself again.
However, there was an extremely important matter that he had to know.
"Mother, Zheng 'er still has something he doesn't understand," Luo Zheng said.
"What is it?" Li Luoshui asked.
Luo Zheng thought for a while and said, "That day, when I entered the underground of the Tie Family, I found out what that mud-like monster was …"
He was now stuck at the lower True God level. He had used all his methods but was unable to increase his cultivation.
The only thing that allowed him to condense a Godhead was the mud. Therefore, Luo Zheng could only pin his hopes on this.
"It wasn't mud, but a spirit crow," Li Luoshui replied. "Why do you ask?"
"So it was called a spirit crow … Zheng 'er's cultivation stopped at the lower True God level. At that time, he was able to condense a Godhead by relying on that thing. But now, he can't increase his cultivation, so I think this is the problem," Luo Zheng answered truthfully, not hiding anything.
"You relied on the spirit crow to condense a Godhead?" Li Luoshui looked surprised.
Luo Zheng nodded. "It was the Primal Chaos Secret Technique that Master bestowed upon me. Therefore, my inner world …"
"Primal Chaos Secret Technique! They really dared … "Li Luoshui's voice rose by three notches.
Even Jian Ao and the others, who were a thousand feet behind them, heard it and looked over with strange gazes.
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng looked curiously at his mother's reaction.
"And you opened up your inner world, absorbed the spirit crow, and successfully condensed a Godhead?" Li Luoshui looked a little excited.
"Yes," Luo Zheng nodded again.
"How many Sanskrit characters have been formed in your inner world?" Li Luoshui asked again.
"One thousand and eighty golden Sanskrit characters," Luo Zheng replied.
"One thousand and eighty!"
Li Luoshui's eyes revealed a look of shock. She looked at Luo Zheng in a daze, and there was a hint of surprise in her shock. She murmured, "Gu Bei was crazy enough to give you the Primal Chaos Secret Technique. You also dared to cultivate it, and you even succeeded …"
Luo Zheng curled his lips. "Cultivating this is indeed very painful."
The inhumane torture he had experienced before, even now, made his heart palpitate when he thought about it.

Chapter 2076
"Thousand Brahms Domain?"
Luo Zheng paused for a moment, realizing that he had asked the right person.
Even Gu Bei and Luo Xiao might not be able to answer this question. After all, they had created the Primal Chaos Secret Technique, and they did not know how far it would go.
Li Luoshui nodded and stared at her son, as if she was looking at some strange creature.

"Originally, all hope was lost. Perhaps this is a turning point …"
"This is the will of the Primal Chaos Ancient God! The Primal Chaos Ancient God is protecting us … "
Li Luoshui had been fine before, but now she seemed to be too happy and was completely immersed in her own thoughts.

Li Luoshui came back to her senses. Seeing Luo Zheng's surprised gaze, she realized that she had lost her composure and was slightly embarrassed.

Luo Zheng did not know this.
He only knew that this ship was constantly falling.
According to Saint Jiu's explanation, the final result was that the Divine Realm would fall …
If such a large world fell, all the living beings in the Divine Realm would probably be wiped out.
After all, apart from Saints, who could resist the Primal Chaos Aura that was pouring into the Divine Realm?
"I don't know." Luo Zheng shook his head.
Li Luoshui sighed slightly and said, "The Divine Realm was originally a ship of hope for the people. It was a ship used to escape."
"Escape?" Luo Zheng's pupils shrank slightly.
"Yes." Li Luoshui nodded and said, "Our Li Min clan was also a great ancient clan in the Mother World, but in the end, we were defeated by the Great Dihong clan. Countless clansmen were slaughtered … In a time of crisis, in order to continue our Li Min bloodline, we had no choice but to create a divine realm and wander into the endless Chaos."
When these matters were mentioned, Li Luoshui's face unconsciously revealed a look of grief and indignation.
Their people used to be so prosperous. Among them, there were nine great tribes, and a hundred thousand Chiyou Guards. They were enough to move unhindered in the mother world …
It was just that when Li Luoshui was still ignorant of the situation, the tribe had already declined. Even the chief was being suppressed, and in the end, they had no choice but to resort to such a plan.
"This God's Domain is actually a ship used to escape." Luo Zheng was somewhat stunned.
It turned out that the creatures in the depths of the Primal Chaos also had their own factions, conflicts, and massacres.
Luo Zheng thought for a moment before asking, "That's not right. If the Li Clan fled, why are there so few Li Clan members in the God Domain?"
For a tribe to escape on such a huge ship like the God Domain, it would definitely take away tens of thousands of Li Clan members.
However, Luo Zheng had been in the God Domain for so long. He knew that there were some strange races in the God Domain, as well as countless True Gods. However, he had never heard of a Li Clan.
If there was one, then his mother in front of him was most likely the only one!
Li Luoshui smiled slightly. "In front of you, aren't there two?"
"Two?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
"Me, and you." Li Luoshui smiled. "The Li Clan is divided into nine tribes, and each of them has different bloodlines. However, you possess the most orthodox Chiyou bloodline of the nine great tribes!"
Luo Zheng finally understood what his mother meant …
Since he was a descendant of the Li Clan, he was naturally also a "Li Clan member." Moreover, he had inherited Li Luoshui's bloodline.
However, he still felt that something was wrong. His gaze suddenly stared at Luo Yan in his arms and asked, "Since I am a Li Clan member, then what about Luo Yan?"
Since he and Luo Yan were both Li Luoshui's children, why didn't his mother count her in?
"She isn't," Li Luoshui said faintly.
"Mother means that Luo Yan isn't Mother's child?" Luo Zheng asked cautiously, slightly nervous.
Who would have thought that Li Luoshui would smile slightly. "Of course Mother doesn't mean that. Yan 'er is indeed your biological sister. It's just that her bloodline is very special. This also has something to do with Mother. She has another kind of bloodline …"
"So that's how it is." Luo Zheng finally relaxed.
"In the beginning, there were many Li Clans in the God Domain." Li Luoshui stared at Luo Zheng and said, "You may not have heard of the term 'Li Clan', but you should have heard of another race."
Luo Zheng muttered to himself for a moment. Suddenly, that mysterious race appeared in his mind …
"The Sacred Clan!" Luo Zheng blurted out.
"Yes," Li Luoshui said softly.
The first time Luo Zheng saw a member of the Sacred Clan, it was in the Immortal Mansion of the Northern Sage.
At that time, he had developed a strong interest in this perfect race.
Later, when Mu Haiji launched the Great Universal War, the "Sacred Clan" of the Greater Universe attacked. At that time, he knew that it was only a "fake Sacred Clan", created by Mu Haiji's imagination.
Later, he learned that the real Sacred Clan came from the God Domain.
At the beginning of the God Domain, it was this mysterious race that dominated the God Domain.
The first race to comprehend the Divine Dao tablets was the Sacred Clan. The strongest race was the Sacred Clan. Even the ones who became Saints were the Sacred Clan.
According to common logic, since the Sacred Clan was so powerful and had the initiative, the other races simply didn't have the opportunity to rise …
But in the ancient records of the Realm of the Gods, this mysterious and powerful race had suddenly disappeared overnight!
Such a strange matter had always been a mystery in the God Domain that had never been solved.
Or it could be said that this mystery originally had an answer. It was just that the answer was in the hands of an extremely small number of people and had never been made public.
Luo Zheng suddenly thought of the legends of the ancient Dao Comprehenders. He asked, "Does the Sacred Clan still need to comprehend the Divine Dao? Shouldn't they be powerful beings that surpassed their level? "
Li Luo Shui shook her head, "Even if we Li Tribe are very powerful, there are always countless weak existences within the tribe. Some of the Li Tribe's younger Li Tribe members have strength similar to the God Domain's citizens. This ship of hope contains the three thousand Divine Daos left behind by our Patriarch. We Li Tribe members naturally have to pass them down!"
Hearing Li Luo Shui's explanation, Luo Zheng finally understood.
So the three thousand Divine Daos were all created by the Patriarch of the Li Tribe!
"Then … why did the Sacred Clan disappear?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Because of the pursuers," Li Luo Shui replied, "The Dihong Clan is very clear on the principle of cutting the weeds and eliminating the roots. How could they allow the Li Tribe with the purest bloodline to escape? He sent the Xuan Yuan Guard to kill us! Even though we tried our best to resist, one of the Xuan Yuan Guards still used the 'Blood Rending Art'. "
Li Luo Shui's thoughts seemed to drift back to an extremely long time ago …
"This Blood Rending Art is extremely vicious. It's a method of extermination. As long as you obtain the bloodline of one of the clansmen, you can exterminate the entire race within a certain range. That Xuan Yuan Guard used the last of his strength to use the Blood Rending Art. After that, all the Li Tribe members in the God Domain exploded into a mass of blood …"
"So in the eyes of those who came later in the God Domain, the Sacred Clan disappeared overnight because they were all dead!"

Chapter 2077
The war back then had been extremely tragic.
But what hurt her the most was watching her clansmen turn into blood before her eyes.
All of this was because they shared the same bloodline!
At that time, she had yelled at the Xuan Yuan Guard with all her might, asking why he had implicated so many innocent people.
That Xuan Yuan Guard had given her a very realistic answer.
"Possessing the Chiyou bloodline is a sin …"
At that time, she was still ignorant. Later, she understood that it was not a sin to possess the Chiyou bloodline, but a sin to be weak!
That war …
Almost all the people had been killed.
The Chiyou Guard that had guarded the ship had also paid a heavy price.
And Li Luoshui had survived under Elder Yuan's protection.
Although the Xuan Yuan Guard that had chased after the Li Clan had an absolute advantage in terms of strength …
But the remaining people, backed by the Divine Realm, were still able to block the Xuan Yuan Guard's pursuit.
Later, she met the young Luo Xiao.
Luo Xiao did not come from the Mother World, but was a divine citizen born in the Divine Realm.
She had never seen such a confident man, and had never seen someone with such monstrous talent.
Perhaps it was because she had seen countless clansmen turn into blood, but Li Luoshui had a natural admiration for the strong. It was also at that time that she fell in love with Luo Xiao …
When Luo Xiao found out that she was the so-called "Holy Clan" and learned some of the legends of the people, it aroused great interest.
Li Luoshui spoke tirelessly, and Luo Zheng was also enthralled.
"Since you've already blocked the Xuan Yuan Guard's pursuit, why is mother still hiding underground?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Just as mother had said, the Li Clan in the Divine Realm had indeed suffered heavy losses!
But even so, the surviving powerhouses should have absolute ruling power in the Divine Domain!
It was not up to these saints to rule the Divine Realm.
"Those Xuan Yuan Guards did not die. Elder Yuan activated the Divine Realm's Origin Seal." Li Luoshui laughed coldly. "But this resistance has never stopped, and has continued to this day! Your father wanted to help me, to help the Li Clan. He was a confident person to the bone, and at that time, he was in the limelight, and had a lot of influence in the Saint Hall! Even Dongfang Chunjun was on his side! "
"What? Even Dongfang Chunjun is on father's side? "Luo Zheng had a strange look on his face.
In Luo Zheng's mind, Dongfang Chunjun and Mu Haiji were his father's mortal enemies, and they were also his mortal enemies!
Who would have thought that Dongfang Chunjun and his father were on the same side back then? It was simply unbelievable!
"Yes." Li Luoshui nodded.
Actually, this wasn't strange …
After the Li Clan was destroyed, the only thing they could hope for was to seek help from the living beings born in the Divine Realm.
These creatures had experienced countless Divine Epochs, and with the help of the resources in the Divine Realm, they had slowly become stronger. They had almost replaced the position of the Sacred Clan, which was the position of the common people.
They obtained Godhood, became True Gods, and cultivated to the peak. They became Saints through the Saint Mountain, entered the Sea of Time, and became a force that could not be underestimated.
"Then why did you …" Luo Zheng couldn't understand.
A helpless smile appeared on Li Luoshui's face. "The Origin Seal sealed the Xuanyuan Guards for countless Divine Epochs, but to them, it only takes time to break these seals, and time is worthless. As long as they are alive, the seal will be broken one day!"
"This is a hidden danger that must be eliminated." Luo Zheng nodded. "So my father wanted to kill those Xuanyuan Guards?"
In this way, there was indeed a reasonable explanation.
The Xuanyuan Guards that his mother spoke of were here to kill the common people.
If the sealed Xuanyuan Guards woke up, the entire Divine Realm would suffer in the end!
As the actual leader of the Saints, Dongfang Chunjun probably had to consider the Divine Realm. Luo Xiao's request naturally received the support of all the Saints.
After all, the Saints all saw themselves as the rulers of the Divine Realm. How could they tolerate such a huge hidden danger in the Divine Realm?
"Yes," Li Luoshui continued. "But in the end, there was a big problem. Dongfang Chunjun changed sides at the last minute."
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
He knew the result. His father was killed by Dongfang Chunjun, and he relied on the Universe of Expansion to prolong his life.
But Dongfang Chunjun should have no reason to do so!
An unnatural expression appeared on Li Luoshui's face … Luo Zheng frowned, wanting to get an answer from his mother.
In the end, she said in a low voice, "Because … of me."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng looked at his mother's youthful appearance and was silent for a long time. He seemed to understand something.
Dongfang Chunjun was an extremely conceited person.
In the entire Divine Realm, the Dongfang family was the largest clan.
Before Dongfang Chunjun, the Dongfang family occupied the Fusang Divine Tree and had already given birth to a Saint and several Quasi-Saints.
However, at that time, the Dongfang family had yet to occupy a dominant position in the Divine Realm.
It was not until Dongfang Chunjun appeared that the Dongfang family entered the Sea of Time.
Later, after Dongfang Chunjun established the Saints single-handedly, he joined forces with other wealthy families and became even more influential!
Even when Luo Xiao appeared out of nowhere and led the Luo family, an unknown family, into the Sea of Time, it did not cause any dissatisfaction from Dongfang Chunjun.
Although there were disputes between these wealthy families and some conflicts of interest, such as the distribution of Shenwu coins or disputes over family territories, there was not much hatred between them.
At that time, the Saints were still very harmonious. After all, the Saints were already standing at the peak of this world and had an unlimited lifespan.
They had plenty of time to study Sanskrit, explore the unknown chaotic world, and explore the various forbidden areas …
As for women …
For the Saints who had an unlimited lifespan, there were all kinds of beauties. Some were dignified, some were charming, and some were pure. They could be said to be as many as they wanted.
Not to mention 3,000 beauties in the harem, if they were interested, 30,000 or 300,000 would not be a problem.
Therefore, it was hard to imagine that two Saints would fight over a woman.
However, the appearance of Li Luoshui attracted Dongfang Chunjun.
No matter how one looked at it, Li Luoshui was a unique existence in the Divine Realm.
Luo Zheng never thought that the conflict between his father and Dongfang Chunjun was caused by his mother.
At that time, Dongfang Chunjun did not show it. He only watched coldly as Li Luoshui and Luo Xiao became a couple.

"In other words, after Dongfang Chunjun defected, he directly turned to the Xuan Yuan Guards?" Luo Zheng frowned. "But Mother said that the goal of the Xuan Yuan Guards is to kill all the people and destroy the Divine Realm. This is not good for Dongfang Chunjun, right?"
"What if the Xuan Yuan Guards promised him great benefits? Sacrificing a Divine Realm, he can climb higher and finally even go to the mother world? "Li Luoshui said indifferently." He's already crazy. "

Chapter 2078
