She really didn't have much say in the mother world.
"However, as long as there's a glimmer of hope, even if it's one in a billion, we have to try and fight for it." Elder Yuan suddenly said lightly. "In this aspect, I'm not as good as you, and I'm also not as good as that reckless Luo Xiao!"
After Elder Yuan finished speaking, he looked at Luo Zheng indifferently. "When your mother gave the Tengshe Sword to Luo Xiao, I was strongly against it. I wanted to see what kind of tricks Luo Xiao would play with the Tengshe Sword! I didn't expect that today, the Chengying Sword, which has followed me through all my battles, can't be kept! "
After saying that, he grasped the bronze longsword in his hand.
The moment his five fingers grasped the sword hilt, his entire aura underwent a shocking change.
He was like a withered tree that had suddenly sprouted a fresh green bud one day. The rotten aura around him had completely disappeared.
Just as he slowly raised the Chengying Sword, the True Meaning of Good and Evil in One Thought in the Land of Dao Battle also underwent a change …
At the same time, the entire Ruo Mu underwent a drastic change!
The aura that shrouded the entire Land of Dao Battle also underwent a clear change!
In Sword God City …
Some upper True Gods raised their heads and looked in the direction of Ruo Mu in the west.
"What's wrong? Why did the aura that was suppressing us change? "
"What a strong will!"
"What's wrong with the Sword Clan?"
Even within the Sword Clan, very few people knew the truth about the Land of Dao Battle.
Many True Gods even thought that the Land of Dao Battle was set up by the Sword Clan.
Mo Po 'er stood on top of the city tower, looking at the huge Ruo Mu tree in the distance. Her eyes were filled with confusion. This was too abnormal.
"Hum …"
Beside her, Mo Dian's face was also filled with shock. "The aura in the Land of Dao Battle has lost its effect! It's no longer suppressing the evil in its heart! What's wrong with Ruo Mu? "
Even Mo Dian thought that the Land of Dao Battle was formed because of Ruo Mu.
And for countless God Epochs, the Land of Dao Battle had never lost its effect, not even for a breath's time.

Chapter 2082
In a palace somewhere in Sword Transformation Divine City.
Mu Xuerong paced back and forth on the steps, not stopping for even a moment.
She cultivated the Killing God Dao, so the killing intent in her heart was naturally born, so she often gave off a murderous aura.
However, after coming to the Dao Battle, she was affected by the true meaning of benevolence, so she was unable to gather her killing intent.
Mu Xuerong hated this feeling. It made her restless.
"Can … can you sit down?"
Fang Henshao asked helplessly. Mu Xuerong's pacing was annoying him.
They had sent their True Gods into the Dao Battle, but they didn't have any effective methods to investigate the situation inside, so they could only wait here.
They didn't know what was happening inside, so they naturally felt anxious.
"I wonder if Mu Xian and the others succeeded? It's probably too difficult to kill that brat Luo Zheng. "Mu Xuerong ignored Fang Henshao and sighed.
Tang Wan sneered, "Although we've halved the number of people we sent, those True Gods are all peak Upper True Gods. Isn't it more than enough to kill a Lower True God like Luo Zheng?"
"Hmph, you make it sound so simple. It's not like there's no chance in the Sea of Time. Which one of you can kill Luo Zheng?" Mu Xuerong stared at Tang Wan and asked, "Have you forgotten the humiliation you suffered in the Imperial Jade Seal so quickly?"
Back then, in the Imperial Jade Seal, the lives of so many Perfection Stage Sovereigns were handed over to Luo Zheng.
Hearing Mu Xuerong's words, Tang Wan fell silent.
Dongfang Taiqing sat on a chair at the side, his metallic hands holding two round golden Buddha beads. The two Buddha beads spun around in his hands, emitting a faint glow every time they collided.
Dongfang Taiqing slowly said, "Don't worry. In the Dao Battle, the Luo siblings will definitely die!"
Mu Xuerong's face revealed a trace of doubt. Dongfang Taiqing had always been steady. If he said that, then he must be 90% certain. "Why are you so sure?"
Fang Henshao, Tang Wan, and the other Perfection Stage Sovereigns all looked at Dongfang Taiqing.
Dongfang Taiqing smiled, "Because Dongfang Gui will summon the strongest Fear Incarnation …"
"The strongest Fear Incarnation? What is it? "Mu Xuerong hastily asked.
"As you all know, Dongfang Gui has not been on the floating island for the past few years. His points in the Arena of the Gods have all been reset to zero," Dongfang Tai said calmly.
Dongfang Gui's reputation on the floating island had been resounding. Among the Upper True Gods, he had been ranked first. He was known as the genius most qualified to step into Great Perfection.
Therefore, Mu Xuerong and the others were still paying close attention.
However, this guy suddenly disappeared a few years ago. The Dongfang family announced to the public that he was in seclusion.
"Isn't he in seclusion? Why isn't he on the floating island? "Mu Xuerong asked curiously.
A smile appeared on Dongfang Taiqing's face. "He didn't go into seclusion. Instead, he entered the Abyssal Demon Domain."
"Enter the Abyssal Devil Domain? He's alone? "Fang Henshao was also baffled.
Normally, the Dongfang Family would gather the experts of the Wealthy Alliance to enter a forbidden area like the Abyss Devil Domain.
Even for the first and second levels, only a few Paragons and Upper True Gods were qualified to challenge them.
"No, a few of our Dongfang Family's Secondary Sages will accompany him," Dongfang Taiqing said.
This made the other Paragons even more confused. A few Secondary Sages accompanying an Upper True God into the Abyssal Demon Domain?
Secondary Sages were powerful and would definitely advance to the lowest levels of the Abyssal Demon Domain. This Dongfang Gui was practically useless. He would even become a burden at a critical moment.
While Tang Wan, Fang Henshao, and the others were still confused, Mu Xuerong had already figured it out. Her round eyes widened as she glared at Dongfang Taiqing and said angrily, "If that's the case, why did you make our Mu Family send three elites to their deaths?"
"What do you mean?" Tang Wan was still in a daze.
Among this group of Paragons, Tang Wan's brain was probably the worst.
"In other words, Dongfang Gui will summon an unsolvable fear," Fang Henshao said calmly.
"Yes, it's that demon called Tu 'e," Dongfang Taiqing said.
"Demon!"
Hearing this, anger appeared on Tang Wan's face. "Then Tang Jie, Tang Xu, and the others won't be able to come back!"
Even these Paragons would find it difficult to deal with the Great Demons in the Abyssal Demon Domain. If that was the case, everyone who entered the Dao Battle would undoubtedly die …
The other Paragons didn't look too good.
Those who could enter the Dao Battle were the elites of the various large clans. These elites had the potential to become Paragons in the future and could be considered seeds that were carefully nurtured.
It was too much to send them to their deaths like this.
"Since your Dongfang Family had such a plan, why didn't you inform us?" a Paragon complained.
At the very least, they could have replaced them with a few scapegoats.
Dongfang Taiqing replied unhurriedly, "If you were an ordinary True God, are you afraid that the Sword Race and the others wouldn't be able to tell? Either we don't act at all, or we act to the fullest! Mu Xuerong, isn't your greatest goal to kill Luo Zheng? After two years, that brat will probably be even more difficult to deal with! You can't even deal with that brat, so how can Mu Xian and the others succeed? "
Mu Xuerong fell silent.
That brat Luo Zheng was indeed a lucky person. She had a strong premonition that it would be very difficult to kill him.
In the Sea of Time, Mu Xuerong had repeatedly tried to kill that brat, but he had escaped death several times.
The Dongfang Family's method was probably the safest method.
"This time, as long as the Luo Family can't obtain the inheritance of the Dao Battle, we won't have any more worries. This sacrifice is worth it," Dongfang Taiqing said.
However, as soon as he finished speaking, a unique aura spread out from Ruo Mu.
His calm eyes revealed a trace of bewilderment.
Hum …
At this moment, Mu Xuerong seemed to have been liberated. Her killing intent soared as she said, "Has the true intent of the Dao been dispelled?"
"No." Dongfang Taiqing shook his head. "The true intent of the Dao has been refined. The true intent of good and evil in a single thought … The captain has made a move!"
"What, what does this mean?" Tang Wan asked in confusion.
Dongfang Taiqing's expression was very unsightly. "If they didn't pass through the Dao Battle, then the captain shouldn't have made a move …"
Dongfang Taiqing's understanding of the Dao Battle was similar to that of the Sword Tribe. He knew that the Dao Battle was controlled by that Paramita realm cultivator, but he didn't know the specifics.
"You mean, someone has obtained the inheritance of the Dao Battle? Is it one of us? "Mu Xuerong asked with concern.
Dongfang Taiqing shook his head. "I'm not sure yet. We can only wait for them to come out."
Even Dongfang Taiqing couldn't sit still anymore.
His goal was very simple. He didn't want anyone to obtain the inheritance of the Dao Battle.
This result would have a huge impact on the Dongfang Family's next step …
Logically speaking, the Dongfang Family had gone to great lengths to establish a demon in the Dao Battle. This shouldn't have happened.

Chapter 2083
"This is the aura of a true Nirvana Realm powerhouse!"
Luo Zheng stared at Elder Yuan in a daze.
Facing such a powerhouse, it was as if a single glance from him could consign him to eternal damnation.
Elder Yuan raised the long sword, slowly leveled it, and pointed the tip of the sword at Luo Zheng.
"Let's not talk about the powerhouses in the Mother World, just some hidden powerhouses in the Divine Realm are stronger than me! Do you have the courage to fight against them? Or, do you have the courage to fight against me? "
The aura of One Thought of Good and Evil instantly covered Luo Zheng's body.
Normally, when a True God's aura was strong, it could make people feel that the aura was substantial.
However, Elder Yuan's aura was real and solid.
At this moment, Luo Zheng seemed to be wrapped in a layer of water. Although this layer of water was soft, it contained a shocking power!
In an instant, he seemed to have sunk into winter, and in the next moment, he seemed to have sunk into raging flames.
These two completely different feelings alternated, constantly destroying Luo Zheng.
This was a test …
From Elder Yuan's expression, he was extremely unwilling to give the "Shadow Bearing" sword to him.
Even if his mother was Chiyou's daughter, he was still unwilling.
Elder Yuan still wanted to verify something from him.
Facing this powerful aura, Luo Zheng also found it difficult to resist!
His face quickly turned pale, and his soul was constantly trembling. Even his state of mind was shaken!
Li Luoshui and Luo Yan's expressions were not good, especially Luo Yan.
"Mother, brother is only a Lower True God. Although his soul is not weaker than mine, he can't resist this aura," Luo Yan said anxiously.
Under Li Luoshui's protection, she naturally did not have to face this aura.
Li Luoshui frowned and did not say a word.
Although she could help Luo Zheng make these requests, she knew that there were some difficulties that Luo Zheng had to face himself.
If Luo Zheng could not even pass this small test, Li Luoshui's help would be meaningless!
After all, this was a world where strength was respected.
No matter how big the opportunity was, how big the backer was, how big the foundation was, they were all just additional support.
Step by step, one had to rely on one's own strength!
Therefore, Li Luoshui hardened her heart and did not care about Luo Yan's worries. She only silently said in her heart, "Zheng 'er …"
"This person … is too strong!"
"No wonder he didn't take the Saints seriously. His strength is indeed a level higher than the Saints'!"
"But, I can endure it!"
"The True Dao of Nature!"
Luo Zheng suddenly closed his eyes and began to silently circulate the Dao of Nature in his heart …
As a Nirvana Realm expert, Elder Yuan's comprehension towards the True Dao Intent was naturally not something that Luo Zheng could imagine.
After all, by using the True Meaning of Good and Evil, he had already crossed the sea of True Meaning and crossed over to the other shore.
On the other hand, Luo Zheng had yet to fully grasp the True Meaning of the Dao.
However, it was impossible for Elder Yuan to conjure up all the True Meaning of Dao to test Luo Zheng. He still had to act appropriately.
"The Dao of nature is about going with the flow, following the rules of the changes of all things …"
The True Meaning of Good and Evil in One Thought was essentially two kinds of auras that were constantly alternating. He just had to adapt according to the rhythm.
Luo Zheng had experienced this feeling once on the cyan stone tablet.
However, the pressure at that time was not as strong as it was now.
Of course, the two situations were different. On the cyan stone tablet, Luo Zheng's goal was to climb higher, but now, he only needed to resist.
"No, it's not resistance, it's adaptation …"
The momentum of the True Meaning of Good and Evil was like two completely different torrents, constantly washing over Luo Zheng.
As for Luo Zheng himself, he was also changing along with the changes of this torrent.
"Brother … Brother's momentum has become so unique!" Luo Yan stared at Luo Zheng and said in surprise.
Li Luoshui also looked at her son, her face full of surprise. "He can actually completely adapt in such a short period of time!"
If one didn't have the strength to resist the torrent, one had to learn to swim in it to avoid being submerged.
Luo Zheng was like a person who fell into the water. After fiddling with the water for a while, he naturally learned how to swim!
Soon …
Luo Zheng opened his eyes again.
He faced Old Yuan and then said, "I … have never lacked courage. In the universe, I have faced almighty Divine Sea Realm cultivators and Celestial Venerable Realm cultivators. Even in the God Realm, I have faced Saints. You may be very strong, but time will fill the gap between you and me, and I believe …"
"Believe what?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's powerful words, Old Yuan's old eyes faintly revealed a look of appreciation.
"I believe it won't take too long." Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
As soon as he finished speaking, the momentum stopped abruptly.
Old Yuan looked at Li Luoshui with a smile. "It's really interesting. Luoshui, you are not the one who taught this child, but his heart is so strong. Luo Xiao … Good ability!"
Old Yuan had seen a lot of monstrous figures.
He had been in the Li Clan for much longer than Li Luoshui.
Even in the mother world, there were very few young talents with such a temperament.
Old Yuan flipped his hand and gently threw the bronze sword to Luo Zheng. "I want to know, how did your father make you grow up?"
Even Li Luoshui was very concerned about this question.
Although Luo Zheng and Luo Yan were both invited to the universe, Li Luoshui didn't know what happened in the universe.
She only guessed that Luo Xiao would definitely pile up all the resources on Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng took the "Chengying Sword" in his hand and felt that it was incomparably heavy. The weight of this sword was comparable to the Great Thousand Heavy Sword!
He smiled faintly, his face showing that he had experienced many vicissitudes of life, but he didn't answer Old Yuan's question.
"Brother has experienced a lot …" Luo Yan looked at her brother. She could understand Luo Zheng's struggles the most.
After being brought to the divine realm by Yu Taibai and learning the truth, Luo Yan also felt that his father's methods were too excessive.
It could be said that he had used all means to continuously push Luo Zheng to the edge of life and death, squeezing every inch of his strength, every bit of his courage …
When Li Luoshui heard Luo Yan say that Luo Xiao had created a cruel family and made Luo Zheng suffer, her face didn't look too good. She said with dissatisfaction, "Husband is simply making trouble. How could he treat his own son like this!"
"Haha, this Luo Xiao is really ruthless!" Old Yuan also sighed. "After being tempered like this, Luo Zheng has a temperament that ordinary people can't compare to! Don't talk about those rich and powerful children in the divine realm. I'm afraid that even I can't compare to him in certain aspects. "
Temperament had nothing to do with strength.
The reason why Old Yuan was so slack was because his state of mind had changed. Having lost his confidence, he didn't have any hope. He passively watched as the divine realm fell into an unsalvageable abyss.

Chapter 2084
Elder Yuan gazed indifferently at the Chengying Sword in Luo Zheng's hand and said, "You seem to have cultivated ninety percent of the True Meaning of Nature, and your comprehension is excellent. However, this sword has been with me for many years and has already gained sentience. If you want to use it, you must understand the True Meaning of One Thought, Good and Evil!"
Luo Zheng nodded.
There was nothing special about this longsword in his hand, except that it was a little heavier. It looked like an ordinary longsword.
It should require One Thought, Good and Evil, to use.
He bowed to Elder Yuan and said, "I hope Elder Yuan can give me some pointers."
"It's not pointers. The True Meaning of Dao is comprehended by the individual. How much you can comprehend depends entirely on your own experience. The True Meaning of Nature pursues nature and can be compatible with almost any cultivation technique. However, One Thought, Good and Evil, is different!"
As Elder Yuan spoke, he pointed in the air.
The scenery around the deep well began to change ….
Streaks of time rapidly intertwined on the walls of the well, and densely packed words appeared on the walls.
"One Thought, Evil, Enemies of All Living Beings. One Thought, Good, Salvation …."
"This is the mental cultivation method of One Thought, Good and Evil?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered.
When Luo Zheng read the words on the walls of the well in his mind, he felt an indescribable sense of awkwardness.
These words needed to be comprehended wholeheartedly before one could master them bit by bit.
However ….
Since Elder Yuan and his mother came from the Mother World, they should be considered creatures above their level.
Shouldn't creatures above their level be able to use Sanskrit directly?
Buzz ….
Before long, the words on the walls of the well flew down and gathered in front of Luo Zheng, forming a small scroll.
After Luo Zheng received the scroll, he asked, "Elder Yuan, there's something I don't understand. Are the words you use also these ordinary words? Don't creatures above their level use Sanskrit? "
Every race had its own unique words and language.
The major races in the universe, such as the Yao Ye Clan, all had different languages and words.
However, the most mainstream culture was still the language and words of the human race.
Luo Zheng thought that these languages and words were transmitted from the Domain of the Gods to the universe. When Luo Xiao constructed the Great Yan Universe, he had injected these cultures into the Great Yan Universe.
Just like how Luo Zheng constructed his own Inner World, the words, customs, and language used by the Evil Deities were similar to those of the human race in the Domain of the Gods.
Elder Yuan laughed involuntarily, "Sanskrit, Sanskrit, even for us, it's not easy to decipher it. Your mother knows this point."
Li Luoshui nodded, "Yes, the language we use was created by a man named Cangjie in the mother world. It was widely spread in the mother world. Our people also use this language, so it naturally spread to the God Realm. The language you learned in the Great Yan Universe when you were young was also passed down in the mother world."
"So that's how it is …" Luo Zheng's eyes were filled with surprise.
He originally thought that the words they used usually came from the Domain of the Gods. He didn't expect them to be inherited from the World of the Gods!
"As for Sanskrit, it's the language of the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods," Li Luoshui added.
"This Sanskrit … isn't easy to decipher in the World of the Gods? Then why can Nian 'er read Sanskrit at will? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"What?" Elder Yuan's drooping eyebrows suddenly rose.
"Who is Nian 'er? He can read Sanskrit at will?" Li Luoshui was also surprised.
Luo Zheng replied, "He's Mother's grandson. His name is Luo Nian."
"My grandson?"
Li Luoshui's eyes were filled with surprise. She naturally didn't expect that she even had a grandson.
Elder Yuan only asked anxiously, "Luo Zheng, you said that Luo Nian can read Sanskrit 'at will'?"
Elder Yuan emphasized the word "as you wish".
Luo Zheng replied with a "hmm", but he felt somewhat baffled.
Elder Yuan suddenly raised his hand, and a golden character flashed in his palm. It was a Sanskrit character with a unique shape. Elder Yuan confirmed again, "Is it this Sanskrit character?"
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded. "The characters of the True Meaning of Nature were deciphered by Nian 'er from Sanskrit. Otherwise, I wouldn't have been able to cultivate the True Meaning of Nature to this level."
Why did Luo Nian have such a talent? Luo Zheng himself was also puzzled.
Elder Yuan and Li Luoshui looked at each other, their eyes full of caution.
"The characters of the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods, even in the World of the Gods, can't be deciphered by ordinary people," Li Luoshui said cautiously. "Only people who have the Eyes of the Ancient Gods are qualified to decipher Sanskrit! Even in the World of the Gods, people who have the Eyes of the Ancient Gods are very rare! "
"In other words, Luo Nian has the Eyes of the Ancient Gods?" Luo Zheng blinked.
Luo Yan also smiled and said, "Brother, this nephew of mine is very powerful. This time, after leaving the Dao Battle, I must meet him!"
"If you say that he can really read Sanskrit at will, then he must have the Eyes of the Ancient Gods. Moreover, he can decipher the True Meaning of Nature. The level of his Eyes of the Ancient Gods is definitely not low." As Elder Yuan spoke, his gaze became more and more profound. "One has the Thousand Fan Domain, the other has the Eyes of the Ancient Gods, and the inheritance of Shadow Wa. Why did these bloodlines only erupt now?"
"This is the will of the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods. We can win the final victory," Li Luoshui said with a smile.
"Perhaps …" Elder Yuan's eyes were full of expectation. "If the Thousand Fan Domain he possesses can really mature, then he indeed has the ability to resolve the current crisis. However, the cultivation of the Thousand Fan Domain requires a large number of spirit crows. Luoshui, you will have to guide the cultivation method."
Li Luoshui smiled faintly. "Of course. Zheng 'er, Yan' er, when you leave the Dao Battle, you should listen to Yu Taibai's arrangements. However, if you have the chance, you should come find me. Zheng 'er knows where I am hiding."
"En." Luo Zheng nodded.
Li Luoshui walked forward and hugged Luo Zheng and Luo Yan in her arms, her face revealing a look of longing.
She was only an incarnation of fear. Her main body didn't know everything that had happened here. However, the light cocoon between Luo Zheng's eyebrows could record everything. When they met again, she would know.
For Li Luoshui, this was a precious memory.
"Alright, you've taken what you should have taken. You've also taken what you shouldn't have. It's time to send you up." Elder Yuan waved his hand.
"It's time to say goodbye." Li Luoshui smiled faintly, her eyes revealing a look of reluctance. She kissed Luo Yan and Luo Zheng's foreheads.
"Mother!"
Luo Yan hugged Li Luoshui tightly. Although this was the first time she had seen her mother, the closeness in her blood was irreplaceable.

Chapter 2085
Inside the huge hole at the top of Ruo Mu.
Figures rushed out from the hole, led by Mu Xian, Fang Jing, and the others.
Although they were heavily injured because of Dongfang Gui, they had already escaped.
Before Demon Tu 'e appeared, they had scattered like birds and beasts and were lucky enough to escape from the Dao Battle.
"Plop, plop …"
As soon as they flew out of the hole, they smashed into the surface of Ruo Mu. All of them looked exhausted.
"I thought we were dead for sure …"
"That damned Dongfang Gui, he wanted to drag us down with him!"
"Hmph, he's probably turned into ashes by now!"
The one these upper True Gods hated the most was not Luo Zheng, but Dongfang Gui.
They had almost been killed by Dongfang Gui. Fortunately, they managed to escape with their lives. Naturally, they felt like they had escaped death.
After resting at the edge of the cave for a while, they flew towards the Sword Transformation Divine City.
Not long after they left, the Sword Mastiff flew out as well. Behind him were 11 disciples of the Sword Clan, Soybean Eye, and Nan Longhua!
"We're back!"
Seeing the light of day again, everyone smiled.
Only one member of the Sword Clan had died. At the same time, they had helped the Luo siblings complete their goal. This Dao Battle was almost perfect for them.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
These people also shot towards the Sword Transformation City.
In the main hall of Sword Transformation City.
Jian Wuhen, Jian Leiting, Yu Taibai, and the others all looked relaxed.
They already knew the result in advance.
Although they did not know what Li Luoshui would encounter with Luo Zheng and Luo Yan, it was unlikely that Li Luoshui would harm Luo Zheng and the others.
At the bottom of the palace, Qin Wentian's eyes shone with a bright light.
Dongfang Taiqing, Mu Xuerong, and the others had ugly expressions.
"I've long heard that the Sword Clan has an observation technique that can see through Ruo Mu and see what's going on inside. You should know what happened in the Dao Battle," Mu Xuerong asked.
Jian Wuhen chuckled, "This old man has no time to look at the actions of those juniors. Whether they succeed or not depends on their own abilities. Some people only have themselves to blame for using underhanded means!"
Dongfang Taiqing's eyes twitched.
From Jian Wuhen's words, it was clear that he knew about the Dongfang family's scheme. They had probably already seen the demon.
Previously, Dongfang Taiqing was most worried that Dongfang Gui might not be able to enter the Battle of Dao. If he died before entering the Battle of Dao, then all of Dongfang Clan's preparations would have been wasted. However, this possibility was very low.
Since Dong Fang Gui had entered the land of Dao Struggle and successfully summoned the devil, those upper True Gods were dead for sure!
But whether it was Yu Taibai or Jian Wuhen, they both looked relaxed. This made Dongfang Taiqing puzzled. Could it be that they had defeated the fiends and passed the Dao Struggle?
This was simply impossible …
It was precisely because Dongfang Taiqing couldn't sit still any longer that he led the Great Perfection Stage cultivators of the Wealthy Alliance to inquire about this matter.
At this moment, a whistling sound suddenly came from outside the door.
Immediately, several people appeared at the main entrance.
"Mu Xian!" Mu Xuerong's eyes were sharp. With a single glance, she saw the heavily injured Mu Xian. "You're heavily injured?"
Mu Xian's expression was very ugly. He looked aggrieved as he glared fiercely at Dongfang Taiqing.
Noticing that Yu Taibai and the Jian Clan were here, Mu Xian understood the big picture and didn't complain to Dongfang Gui and the Dongfang Clan. Instead, he whispered, "I was implicated by Dongfang Gui and almost lost my life!"
"How did he implicate you?" Dongfang Taiqing hurriedly asked.
"Forget it." Mu Xian waved his hand. "I won't pursue this matter. In any case, he won't be coming back!"
"Dongfang Gui's strength is above yours. Since you've returned, why can't he return?" Dongfang Taiqing asked even though he already knew the answer. He needed to know what happened in the Battle of Dao Struggle.
"We encountered a devil." Mu Xian pouted and rolled his eyes.
Dongfang Taiqing was slightly relieved to hear this. However, in front of the Saints of the Sword Tribe, he still pretended to be surprised. "A devil! Then wouldn't the others all … "
"Of course they're dead!" Mu Xian impatiently replied.
This answer made Dongfang Taiqing smile. No matter what happened in Ruo Mu, as long as he was dead, he would have achieved his goal.
As for the process, Dongfang Taiqing couldn't be bothered to care.
What he didn't know was that Mu Xian was just spouting nonsense. After all, they all fled when they saw the devil. They all ran faster than anyone else and had no idea what happened.
As for Yu Taibai and the others, they watched the entire process. Seeing how pretentious Dongfang Taiqing was, they sneered and didn't say anything.
Not long after Mu Xian and the others returned.
A group of people appeared at the entrance of the hall. It was Jian Ao and the others!
When Mu Xian, Fang Jing, and the others saw Jian Ao and the others, it was as if they had seen a ghost.
"You, you all actually ran away?" Mu Xian asked.
Mu Xian and the others originally thought that they were the only survivors. However, almost all of the Sword Tribe disciples had returned!
Jian Ao saw the atmosphere in the hall and smiled faintly. "Why would our Sword Tribe run away?"
Mu Xian's face was filled with dissatisfaction. "You didn't run away? How could you possibly survive against that devil? "
"It's very simple." Jian Ao smiled.
The other Sword Tribe disciples, including Soybean Eyes and Nan Longhua, also had strange smiles on their faces.
"How did you do it?" Mu Xian stared at Jian Ao and asked.
The Perfection True Gods also stared nervously at Jian Ao. They urgently needed an answer.
Of course, they didn't care how Jian Ao had survived. What they cared about was whether anyone had obtained the inheritance of the Battle of Dao …
"Wouldn't it be fine if we just killed that devil?" Jian Ao chuckled.
Hearing this, Mu Xian's expression changed.
Dongfang Taiqing furrowed his brows. "Kill that demon? With just you Upper True Gods? "
Jian Ao wasn't afraid of Dongfang Taiqing. The two Sages of the Sword Tribe were present. He shrugged and led the Sword Tribe disciples to the top of the hall. As he walked, he said, "We didn't kill the devil, but he did die. That Dongfang Gui only has himself to blame. He still died. It's up to you whether to believe it or not."
This time, Dongfang Taiqing, Mu Xuerong, Fang Henshao, Tang Wan, and the others' expressions were extremely ugly.
They couldn't determine whether Jian Ao was telling the truth. They looked at each other and saw the anxiety in each other's eyes.
After all, the Luo siblings had yet to appear. They also hoped that the two of them would die in the Battle of Dao and never appear again!
However, in less than five minutes.
Two more figures appeared at the entrance of the hall. They were the Luo siblings, one in front and one behind.

Chapter 2086

Dongfang Taiqing and Mu Xuerong's hopes were dashed!

Mu Xuerong's killing intent soared. Unfortunately, the Dao Battle had returned to normal and the true meaning of benevolence suppressed her killing intent.
"You obtained the inheritance of the Dao Battle?" Dongfang Taiqing stared at Luo Zheng.

Under Dongfang Taiqing's lead, Mu Xuerong, Tang Wan, the other paragons of the Wealthy Alliance, and the upper true gods all walked out of the hall.

It was very convenient to return to the Sea of Time from Sword Transformation City. One only needed to use a teleporter.

As the second most powerful clan on the floating island, the Dongfang Clan and the Dongfang Clan had always minded their own business. The two clans had always lived in harmony.
However, the Sword Race's actions this time were tantamount to a showdown.
"Hmph, if it wasn't for Dongfang Chunjun's crazy plan, our Sword Race wouldn't have bothered to provoke him! If he wants to destroy the Divine Realm, he should ask if the millions of people in the Divine Realm agree! "Jian Leiting said coldly.
The Saints of the Divine Dominion more or less knew about the conflict between the Luo Family and the Wealthy Alliance.

After all, no one was willing to offend the Dongfang family for the sake of the Luo family …
It was just that they did not expect that the dispute between these two families would actually involve the fate of the entire Divine Realm.
Now that Yu Taibai had revealed this information, the Sword Race understood that Dongfang Chunjun was crazy to an incomprehensible extent!
For the sake of his own ambition, he was willing to drag the entire Divine Realm down with him!
It could be said that the Divine Realm was already in a life and death situation, so the Sword Race had no choice but to step forward.
"No matter what, we should be careful. Dongfang Chunjun's actions are often unexpected." Jian Wuhen looked worried.
Offending the Dongfang family was equivalent to offending the entire Wealthy Alliance.
According to Yu Taibai, there should be a Nirvana Realm powerhouse behind Dongfang Chunjun!
"Lord Wuhen, don't worry too much. Even if Dongfang Chunjun really falls out with the Sword Race, they might not be able to gain the upper hand," Yu Taibai said calmly. "Also, there's a response from the Xuanyue family. They won't stand on the Dongfang family's side. Once they believe in Dongfang Chunjun's ambition, I'm sure they'll make the same decision as you!"
Dongfang Chunjun's choice was equivalent to standing on the opposite side of the entire Divine Realm.
The Xuanyue family's foundation was comparable to the Sword Race's, and they were ranked in the top three among the Wealthy Clans. Among them, the Xuanyue Cult, War God Temple, and Heaven Spirit Tower each had a Saint. They were also a force to be reckoned with.
Yu Taibai's current goal was very clear.
He wanted to rope in as many factions as he could in order to avoid a one-on-one battle.
"Let's wait and see." Jian Wuhen nodded.
At their level, they could see the essence of many problems at a glance.
Now that they couldn't grasp Dongfang Chunjun's plans, they could only wait and think of a counterattack.
"The next heavy burden will depend on Luo Zheng." Yu Taibai stared at Luo Zheng.
"Enter the sun?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes." Yu Taibai nodded.
Luo Zheng had obtained the Azure Parasol, which meant that he was the new captain of the Divine Realm.
"When?" Luo Zheng asked again.

When a huge object was falling, it would take a long time to adjust.
Meanwhile, God's Domain was an unimaginably large ship. Now, it was indeed a race against time.

Luo Zheng nodded. He already knew many things from his mother and didn't have any doubts about Yu Taibai's arrangements.

Dongfang Taiqing's group returned to the Sword Race's floating island through the teleportation formation.

Chapter 2087
This Dongfang Palace was built in a unique way. It was made of a kind of translucent glass.

Pushing open the small door, he saw Dongfang Chunjun lying on a huge grayish-white chair in front of him. He held a transparent cup in his hand, and the cup seemed to be filled with blood-like wine. A sweet and enchanting smell spread from the cup and filled the entire palace.

Dongfang Taiqing was only leading the team. He couldn't directly participate in the Battle of the Dao.

Until now, Dongfang Taiqing still couldn't figure out what went wrong. How did they escape the demon's pursuit?
It couldn't be explained at all!

"Moreover, that captain was originally standing on the opposite side of us. If he sensed Luo Zheng's identity and took the initiative to pass the inheritance to that kid Luo Zheng, we wouldn't be able to stop him. Therefore, Taiqing, you don't have to blame yourself. It's naturally good if you succeed, but don't be anxious if you fail.
"Yes." Dongfang Taiqing stood up.
Dongfang Chunjun's words made him feel much better.
That Nirvana Realm powerhouse was originally on Luo Zheng's side. If he really wanted Luo Zheng to obtain the inheritance in the Battle of Dao, who could stop him?
"But this time, the Sword Tribe …" Dongfang Taiqing continued.
As soon as he opened his mouth, Dongfang Chunjun interrupted him.
Dongfang Chunjun laughed coldly. "The Sword Race will side with Yu Taibai sooner or later, but it will happen faster than I expected. Since they are so impatient, don't blame me. Some things have to change …"

…
A day in the Realm of the Gods was divided into twenty-four hours.
On this day, the huge fireball known as the "sun" rose from the far east of the Realm of the Gods and illuminated the land.
Then, it flew past the Realm of the Gods at an extremely fast speed.
When the sun passed through the Sea of Time, it was noon in the Realm of the Gods.
After a few hours, the sun would reach the far west, which was the sky above Ruo Mu. It would be dusk.
In a non-offensive area, at the peak of Ruo Mu, in Sword Transformation City …
Jian Wuhen, Jian Leiting, Yu Taibai, and the others looked up at the sky like statues.
When the sun emitted the afterglow of the setting sun, Jian Wuhen said, "It's about time. I'll bring you up."
Luo Zheng nodded.
Jian Wuhen grabbed Luo Zheng and the two figures suddenly flashed, disappearing in front of Yu Taibai and Luo Yan.
Yu Taibai could also bring Luo Zheng up into the sky, but as a Paragon, Yu Taibai's teleportation distance was far inferior to Jian Wuhen's. The sun was originally a fireball that moved at high speed, so he didn't have a good grasp of timing. In the end, he had to trouble a Sage.
Luo Zheng felt his vision darken. Then, he found himself high up in the sky.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
"What strong astral winds!"
Gusts of strong wind whistled past, slicing his face like knives. Even though Luo Zheng's mortal body was extraordinarily tough, the gusts of wind were still able to leave fine white scars on his body!
The sun above his head was magnified several times in Luo Zheng's vision. It was like an enormous fireball.
"Again!"
Jian Wuhen glanced at his position and continued to pull Luo Zheng with him.
After the second teleportation, Luo Zheng was several times closer to the sun!
Looking down, he could see the entire non-attacking area …
This non-attacking area was semi-circular. The giant Ruo Mu was like an octopus, lying in a 'small pool'. Everything looked incomparably small from this height.
After Jian Wuhen carried Luo Zheng with him for the third teleportation, scorching flames transformed into irregular sharp swords that shot down from above!
This was the Sun Essence Flame.
The Sun Essence Flame was divided into three layers. But even the outermost layer was enough to burn everything!
An ordinary True God wouldn't be able to withstand it.
They were still quite a distance away from the sun. Jian Wuhen could still endure it, but he was afraid that Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to withstand it. His hand flashed with a membrane of light, preparing to envelop Luo Zheng.
But at this moment, tiny golden Sanskrit characters began to flicker on Luo Zheng's body. He began to absorb the Sun Essence Flame!
"Using the Sun Essence Flame to refine the body?" Jian Wuhen's face flashed with astonishment. "You actually absorbed the Sun Essence Flame!"
Even Jian Wuhen didn't dare to absorb the Sun Essence Flame for his own use. This flame was so wild that even a Saint couldn't tame it. This brat was actually crazily absorbing it?
There was once a legend in the Divine Realm. It was said that there was a massive palace in the sun. Whoever could enter the palace in the sun had the qualifications to control the Divine Realm.
When the Saints had just disappeared and the people of the Divine Realm were still ignorant, there were only a few people who had been conferred the title of Saint. Some Saints really did challenge the sun.
The final result was, without exception, that they were all burned to ashes by the sun … Fortunately, the Saints were able to use the universe to make themselves undying and indestructible. In the end, they were able to recover.
But ever since then, no Saint had ever attempted to approach the sun.
Jian Wuhen watched as Luo Zheng recklessly absorbed the Sun Essence Flame. He was shocked.

Chapter 2088
Luo Zheng's body glowed with golden light, and the golden Sanskrit characters covered every inch of his skin.
As for Jian Wuhen, he waited patiently.
After about half an hour, the golden Sanskrit characters on Luo Zheng's body dimmed.
The outline of his body became more distinct, and his every movement exuded a condensed aura …
"Absorbing the Heavenly Fire can make one stronger?" Jian Wuhen was amazed by this scene.
As a Sage, he could tell that Luo Zheng's body was far stronger than that of an ordinary True God's!
Just like the shocking sword strike Luo Zheng had unleashed in the Battle of Daos.
If it were any other ordinary Lower True God, his physical body would have crumbled apart before he could even slash out with his sword.
However, Luo Zheng's body was not much of a problem!
However, Luo Zheng had shown too many incredible things, so it was easy for people to overlook the strength of his body.
"Mm." Luo Zheng nodded in response.
"I've never heard of such a miraculous technique …" Jian Wuhen sighed.
Luo Zheng could adapt to the Sun Essence Fire on the outermost layer, so he would naturally use it to temper his body.
Luo Zheng still did not know to what extent the Regal Equipment Refining Technique could temper one's body.
Ever since he entered the divine realm, it had been very difficult for him to find a suitable flame to temper himself.
However, he got the answer from his mother. All techniques related to Sanskrit were words left behind by the ancient Primal Chaos Gods. Therefore, the limits of the Regal Equipment Refining Technique were probably not limited to the divine realm …
As for the limits, he would explore them in the future.
Now, Luo Zheng had to solve the problem in front of him!
"I'll teleport you again. You'll have to rely on yourself later," Jian Wuhen said.
Then, Jian Wuhen brought Luo Zheng and teleported again …
"Swish!"
The two disappeared in the air again. In the blink of an eye, they had teleported hundreds of thousands of feet!
