Luo Zheng could only see an oval-shaped white ground, which was naturally caused by the continuous snow over the past few months …
The originally round sun had already disappeared.
They were too close to the sun, so they could no longer see the sun's full appearance. Therefore, what appeared was a sea of fire in the sky!
"Huala, huala …"
The sea of fire floated above Luo Zheng's head from top to bottom. As the blazing flames flickered, they set off one fire after another. It was a spectacular sight!
"Huhu …"
Streams of essence sun fire wove into fiery clouds, hovering in the air like whirlwinds.
Even Jian Wuhen had a cautious look on his face as he faced the Essence Sun Fire!
He pointed in the air …
An invisible sword intent shot out from his finger.
"Sea Town!"
"Huala …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng sensed that the aura within a radius of 10,000 miles had suddenly changed.
The sound of surging tides and waves crashing against each other could be heard in his ears …
A feeling of Luo of the sea Ocean Sea Sea Sea
"This is the Sword's Sword …"
"Sword" of the sword of the Divine Dao, "Of of Zheng 'of the God''s," So powerful … "
Luo Zheng sighed in his heart.
When the Sword's Sword was released, no matter how the red clouds of fire moved, they couldn't get close to the two!
They seemed to be moving further and further away from the two of them amidst the waves.
"I can only send you up to here. I can't go any higher," Jian Wuhen said seriously.
Luo Zheng nodded and took out a flying sword from his Sumeru Ring.
He couldn't fly now, so he still had to rely on the flying sword to fly.
After stepping on the flying sword, Luo Zheng took out an umbrella from his Sumeru Ring. It was the Azure Parasol he had obtained from the Dao Struggle.
The reason why Elder Yuan became the captain was because this umbrella was in his hands.
Relying on this umbrella, he was able to enter the fireball that was the sun unimpeded.
"Feng!"
After Luo Zheng opened the umbrella, a bright yellow curtain of light immediately enveloped him and the flying sword under his feet.
Luo Zheng had to be careful now. If the flying sword was melted by the Sun Essence Fire, he would be in big trouble.
"Then I'll go up," Luo Zheng said.
"I'll leave everything to you." Jian Wuhen looked indifferently at the young man.
Then Luo Zheng held the Azure Parasol and stepped on the flying sword, turning into a streak of light that shot up into the sky.
Seeing Luo Zheng's figure disappear into the distance, Jian Wuhen's face was filled with emotion.
He had never thought that the fate of the Realm of the Gods would be entrusted to these juniors.
He had never thought that the destroyed Luo Family still had such a powerful ability hidden …
He didn't know if choosing to stand on the Luo Family's side was the wrong move, but the sword race's innate sense of mission forced them to save the millions of lives of the Realm of the Gods.
… …
The fiery red clouds quickly passed by Luo Zheng's surroundings.
The light curtain formed by the Green Parasol easily isolated all the flames.
In the space under the umbrella, Luo Zheng didn't feel the heat at all. Instead, he felt a faint coolness.
It was indeed a very miraculous magic weapon.
Even though the Azure Parasol could easily block the sun's essence fire, Luo Zheng didn't dare to be careless.
The red clouds of fire couldn't burn Luo Zheng, but they formed powerful whirlpools as they crazily circled.
If Luo Zheng didn't pay attention and got sucked into these whirlpools, he would lose his balance and be swept away by these currents … …
After flying like this for more than an hour, more and more fire clouds appeared around Luo Zheng!
He was about to cross the middle layer of the sun and approach the inner layer.
At this stage, even a Saint couldn't withstand the sun's essence fire … …
"The sun's essence fire here is probably a hundred times stronger than the outer layer. If I lose the protection of the Azure Parasol, I'll probably be burned to ashes in an instant!"
Luo Zheng was also shocked by the layers of fire clouds formed by the sun's essence fire.
He didn't know how this sun was formed. It could burn in the sky for countless God Epochs and still maintain such a powerful aura.
At this moment, he deeply felt his own insignificance.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh … …"
At this moment, a sharp whistling sound was transmitted to Luo Zheng's ears.
Tens of thousands of sun's essence fire formed a huge whirlpool!
"Oh no!"
Luo Zheng's expression changed. He stepped on his flying sword and flew to the other side.
However, the range of this whirlpool was too large. If Luo Zheng was at the Great Perfection Stage or a Saint, he might have a chance to escape.
However, Luo Zheng was only a lower True God, and he was still relying on his flying sword. How could he make it in time?
"Swish … …"

Chapter 2089
This vortex pushed Luo Zheng upwards at a high speed!
During the whole process, Luo Zheng stood on the flying sword, trying his best to maintain his balance while holding the Green Parasol tightly with both hands!
"Too fast!"
Now, Luo Zheng's speed was faster than his flying speed. This vortex was the sun that pushed Luo Zheng upwards.
This state lasted for about the time it took for an incense stick to burn …
"Huala …"
The sea of fire above his head set off a huge wave of flames, sweeping towards Luo Zheng.
"Finally, I'm here!"
The Sun Essence Fire in the outermost and middle layers were just the flames that drifted out from the inner part of the sun.
The sea of fire in front of him was the inner part of the sun!
Luo Zheng didn't have time to adjust his posture. Holding the Green Parasol, he plunged headfirst into the sea of fire.
When he looked back, he saw that everything around him was crimson red, as if he was immersed in a crimson ocean.
The scorching vortex couldn't endure the sea of fire. After forming a small vortex in the sea of fire, it finally dissipated …
"The inner part of the sun is also very large. I don't know where the palace that Elder Yuan mentioned is. I can only look for it slowly."
Luo Zheng chose a direction and drove the flying sword to move slowly.
This sea of fire wasn't magma. It seemed to be a kind of red hot current. This hot current was very viscous, like an ordinary liquid. It was probably the liquefied form of the Sun Essence Fire.
The power contained in this sea of fire was even more unimaginable. After all, even a Sage would die if he touched this sea of fire!
It was difficult to drive the flying sword to escape in this sea of fire.
He could only slowly float around as his eyes scanned the surroundings ….
Luo Zheng was probably the second creature to visit this place besides the captain.
In such a harsh environment, there shouldn't be any other creatures, which made him feel very relieved.
"Keep going up!"
Luo Zheng didn't delay at all.
Two hours …
Four hours …
Six hours …
He cruised in this sea of fire for six to eight hours.
It was still an endless sea of red fire, and the surrounding scenery had not changed at all.
Although the umbrella could isolate all the flames outside, Luo Zheng felt bored and even anxious after staying in this boring environment for a long time!
"This big fireball is probably as big as a region. Based on my speed, won't it take me several years to find the palace?"
Just as he was hesitating …
Suddenly, he heard a "Da Da Da" sound behind him.
When he turned his head to take a look, he was shocked.
Outside the light curtain formed by the Green Parasol, there were fist-sized things gathering!
Under such high temperature, he couldn't release his Divine Sense, because once his Divine Sense left the light curtain, it would be completely burned away by the extremely high temperature, so he didn't notice these little things approaching.
"What the hell is this? How can it survive in such an environment? "Luo Zheng was astonished.
This was the Sun. Not to mention ordinary monsters, even Saints would be incinerated if they entered. Other living beings did not even have the qualifications to approach the Sun before they were incinerated!
He had never thought that there would be living beings in the Sun!
These fist-sized things had oval heads, and their left arms were big pincers. They seemed to be some kind of strange shrimp.
They were not afraid of the sun's essence fire!
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng didn't dare to open the light curtain, or else he would have caught some of these strange shrimps.
Since they could resist the sun's essence fire, their outer shell must be made of very rare materials.
But then again, the strange shrimps lived in this sea of fire, and their temperature was no different from the sun's essence fire. Even if Luo Zheng could bring them in, he wouldn't dare to touch these things!
The small strange shrimps followed Luo Zheng, and kept tapping the light curtain with their pincers …
"Da Da Da …"
"This little thing wants to break the light curtain and get in?"
Luo Zheng frowned.
If the light curtain really broke, he would be finished. He would immediately turn into ashes and disappear from this world forever.
"Go away!"
Luo Zheng punched the light curtain.
"Dong!"
The light curtain shook, and the strange shrimps scattered. But the shrimps that escaped stretched their bodies and stuck to the light curtain again, tapping it with their pincers.
Luo Zheng tried to drive them away a few times, but these strange shrimps found that Luo Zheng was only trying to scare them. They stopped running away, and the number of strange shrimps that gathered was increasing!
"This is troublesome …"
Luo Zheng showed a bitter smile.
Soon, the light curtain formed by the Blue Parasol was densely surrounded by these strange shrimps. There were three or four hundred of them, and they blocked most of his sight.
Looking at the strength of these strange shrimps, they shouldn't be able to break the light curtain. But he was moving slowly in the Sun, not to mention dragging these strange shrimps with him.
Luo Zheng grabbed the Blue Parasol and shook it again!
The light curtain shook, and the strange shrimps were bounced back, but they were not afraid at all. They stuck to him like sticky candy.
Luo Zheng suddenly felt a headache coming on.
Just as Luo Zheng was feeling depressed, he suddenly saw a slender shadow shoot over through the gaps between these strange shrimps.
"Those are … fish!"
Six or seven chi long big fish rushed over.
These big fish were almost as tall as an adult. They constantly swam around Luo Zheng, opening their mouths to grab a strange shrimp and then swallowing it …
"These strange shrimps … are the food of these fish," Luo Zheng was stunned.
It seemed that there were more than just these two kinds of creatures in this sea of fire. At least they could form a complete food chain!
The big fish had excellent appetites. In a short while, the strange shrimps that stuck to the light curtain were all swallowed up by them.
Perhaps they were interested in Luo Zheng, but these big fish didn't leave. Like the strange shrimps, they used their sharp mouths to tap the light curtain.
But not long after …
Luo Zheng suddenly felt that the light above him had dimmed a bit.
At the same time, the big fish that gathered around him swayed their tails and fled in all directions.
Luo Zheng looked up and saw a huge whale that was hundreds of thousands of feet long appear above his head!
Two faint green flames rolled in the eyes of this huge whale, and there were faint flames flowing on its skin.
After the huge whale appeared, it immediately opened its mouth and swallowed down.
Under the whale's swallowing, the big fish within a radius of several hundred feet were sucked into the huge whale's mouth.

Chapter 2090
Even though he was in the belly of the giant whale, the surrounding environment was still extremely hot.
Luo Zheng couldn't remove the Cyan Parasol, so he could only stay put.
Fortunately, the light curtain formed by the Cyan Parasol was really solid. Although it was tumbling inside the giant whale's body, it was still very strong.
As long as he held the Cyan Parasol well, the light curtain wouldn't be broken!
Because of this, Luo Zheng was a little relieved …
"Huala …"
As soon as Luo Zheng calmed down, he felt a squirming feeling under his feet.
He held the Cyan Parasol and continued to tumble into the darkness. He should be shuttling through the giant whale's esophagus.
When Luo Zheng finally stabilized his body, he saw a dim cyan light!
"Cyan flame?"
Luo Zheng's eyes slightly twitched.
At the same time, he saw some big fish being drawn into the cyan flame.
This giant whale didn't have teeth, so it just swallowed the fish and Luo Zheng into its belly. It didn't chew them, so these big fish and Luo Zheng were still alive and kicking.
However, when they fell into the cyan flame, they began to crazily twist their bodies. Their tough bodies kept tumbling and bouncing in the cyan flame. Soon, Luo Zheng saw these big fish being burned by the cyan flame. They melted little by little, exposing their bones and heads.
The big fish that had been burned to death sank into the green flames, and gradually melted away until not even a bone was left behind …
"The cyan flame inside the giant whale is more powerful than the Essence Sun Flame?" Luo Zheng was surprised.
These big fish could survive in the Essence Sun Flame. With their special bodies, they weren't afraid of any kind of flame. However, they were burned to ashes by the cyan flame!
"No … The Essence Sun Flame should be the most powerful flame in the God Area. This giant whale's cyan flame can't be more powerful than the Essence Sun Flame. This cyan flame is like the giant whale's stomach juice, which is used to digest those big fish. I'm afraid this cyan flame evolved from the giant whale's harsh environment over countless God Eras!" A worried look appeared on Luo Zheng's face. "I don't know if this light curtain can block the cyan flames …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng once again felt a powerful wriggling force beneath his feet, lifting him up and causing him to fall into the cyan flames.
Along with him were dozens of large fish and countless fist-sized strange shrimps.
"Hualala …"
The liquid-like cyan flame fluctuated back and forth around Luo Zheng.
The surrounding big fishes and strange shrimps were quickly melted by the cyan flame. Luo Zheng looked at the hologram nervously.
"Please don't break it …"
Luo Zheng prayed in his heart.
After a few breaths, the layer of light didn't show any signs of melting. Luo Zheng let out a sigh of relief.
This damned place was really not a place for people to stay.
Not to mention a Saint, even a Nirvana Realm powerhouse would not be able to survive without the help of this Green Parasol …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly had a question.
The only way to control the "ship" of the Divine Realm was from the palace in the sun.
The Green Parasol was the key to entering the palace. Without the Green Parasol, how could other people control the direction of this "ship"?
His mother and Elder Yuan hadn't told him about this, so Luo Zheng couldn't find an answer for the time being. Perhaps the Xuanyuan Guard had used some other method to influence the direction of the Divine Realm's flight. And after that, Elder Yuan had lost the ability to enter the sun …
Luo Zheng's imagination ran wild in the giant whale's stomach. From time to time, he would hear a loud crash. Countless large fishes, small fishes, and strange shrimps were sent into the giant whale's stomach and were quickly digested.
"This giant whale can eat," Luo Zheng sighed.
He stayed in the stomach for a few hours. This giant whale seemed to be eating non-stop …
After sighing, he began to worry.
Since this was the giant whale's stomach for digesting food, if he wanted to leave here, he would have to be digested into dregs and then expelled from the body.
In that case, he would definitely die.
However, he couldn't digest it under the protection of the light curtain!
"Things that can't be digested!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He seemed to have thought of something.
All creatures that swallowed things that they couldn't digest would vomit until they spat out the foreign matter in their stomach.
Since that was the case …
Stepping on the flying sword, he leaped into the air and shot upward.
Boom!
Under the sudden impact, he heavily smashed into the stomach wall above his head.
With this smash, Luo Zheng took advantage of the momentum and rushed down!
"Again!"
Within the soft stomach wall, Luo Zheng jumped up and down, constantly crashing back and forth.
The blue flames danced around Luo Zheng, but in the end, they couldn't break the light curtain that enveloped him. They couldn't hurt him in the slightest.
The giant whale swimming in the sea of fire was happily devouring countless living beings.
This giant whale was the king of this sea of fire. It stood at the top of the food chain and had no natural enemies.
Today, it swallowed a strange thing, but it didn't care. Any creature that was swallowed into its stomach would be digested in the end.
However, there was an exception this time.
Suddenly, it felt an uncomfortable feeling in its body. Waves of pain came from its stomach.
Even with its low intelligence, the giant whale knew that it had eaten the wrong thing. It shouldn't have swallowed that strange-looking creature …
Under the stimulation of the pain, its huge body began to convulse and twist. Its stomach began to contract violently!
Luo Zheng, who was inside the giant whale, also noticed the changes. The space around him began to shrink rapidly!
"It's coming!"
Luo Zheng held the Blue Parasol tightly with both hands and stepped on the flying sword.
Just as he got into position, he felt a shocking force under his feet, which suddenly threw him out.
Huff, huff, huff …
He quickly moved through the darkness, and as he continued to roll, the sea of fire came into view once again!
"I'm out!"
Luo Zheng's toes touched the flying sword lightly, quickly stabilizing his body.
After the giant whale spat Luo Zheng out, it didn't leave immediately. Instead, it stared at Luo Zheng with its huge eyes.
It probably wanted to remember not to swallow such a strange thing next time. Then, it wriggled its huge tail and escaped into the depths of the sea of fire.
After the giant whale left, Luo Zheng breathed a sigh of relief …
He stayed in the giant whale's stomach for several hours. With the giant whale's swimming speed, he didn't know how far it had traveled.
Just as he turned his head to look at his surroundings, he looked up and saw a unique silhouette not far from his upper right!
"That's …"
Luo Zheng focused his eyes and looked over. His eyebrows gradually raised, and his face lit up with joy.

Chapter 2091
After entering the sea of fire, Luo Zheng was still worried.
From afar, the sun looked like a small fireball, but in fact, the space inside was enormous.
Luo Zheng relied on a flying sword to fly through the sea of fire at a very slow speed!
He didn't know how much time it would take to find the palace …
But the speed of the giant whale was countless times faster than Luo Zheng's.
He had been tossed and turned in the stomach of the whale for several hours. He didn't know how far the whale had swum, but it actually brought him directly to the destination!
This could be considered a blessing in disguise!
Looking at the huge golden palace, Luo Zheng's heartbeat sped up. He didn't hesitate at all.
The flying sword under his feet flew up slightly and flew in the direction of the palace …
This palace looked very close, but Luo Zheng still spent a little over an hour before he was able to get close.
"What a majestic palace …"
Standing at the entrance of the palace, Luo Zheng sighed.
The whole palace was shining with a golden brilliance. He didn't know what metal it was made of, but it certainly couldn't be gold. That kind of soft metal would have already turned into liquid under such high temperature.
Then he drove the flying sword and floated in through the open door of the palace.
The front half of the palace was also immersed in the sea of fire. But after moving along the door for some distance, Luo Zheng saw several mysterious array patterns on the ground.
From these patterns, a faint yellow light curtain spread out, blocking the sea of fire outside.
"The faint yellow light is almost exactly the same as the light released by the Azure Parasol!"
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
When he held the Azure Parasol close to the light curtain, the light curtain of the Azure Parasol actually connected with the light curtain in the palace!
Luo Zheng entered without any hindrance …
"I came in just like that!"
The palace used the light curtain to create a larger and safer space!
Luo Zheng stood in the palace and carefully removed the Azure Parasol. There was no burning sensation. This place was safe.
Even so, Luo Zheng still didn't relax his vigilance.
There were all kinds of creatures in the sea of fire in the sun. There might be some bizarre things in the palace.
Walking through the huge corridor of the palace, he released his divine sense all the way, checking every movement in the surroundings.
Although the outside of the palace was very luxurious, the inside was empty. There was indeed nothing …
After passing through the long corridor, he went through three halls in a row and finally came to the center of the palace.
He saw seven rings connected to each other on the ground. When he looked up, he could also see seven rings corresponding to the top of the palace.
"It's here!"
Luo Zheng smiled, then walked over and stepped on the most central ring.
Other than being wary of some strange and ferocious creatures, he wasn't worried that there would be any traps here.
After all, the sun itself was a very powerful barrier. As Elder Yuan said, this umbrella was originally a key. With this key, one could easily travel through the sun. Without this key, even saints couldn't enter. Naturally, there was no need to set up any other traps.
Just as Luo Zheng stepped into the ring, Luo Zheng's expression changed.
The seven rings under his feet began to emit a faint light!
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The ring in the center floated up and wrapped itself around Luo Zheng's waist, suspending him in midair.
The other rings followed Luo Zheng's feet and wrapped around his hands. There were two rings superimposed on top of his head …
"What's this?"
Luo Zheng's face was filled with doubt.
"Buzz …"
When the seven rings were in place, the two rings on top of his head vibrated again.
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt that his soul seemed to have formed a very close connection with these rings!
Suddenly, a white light exploded in his mind, like a flash of lightning …
When he opened his eyes again, he saw an oval-shaped object that looked like a gem!
"What's this?"
Luo Zheng's face was filled with doubt.
He stared at the gemstone floating in the void for a long time. The surprise in his eyes became more and more obvious. He almost couldn't believe it!
"This is the Domain of the Gods!"
Through those rings, Luo Zheng's field of vision seemed to be at an extremely far angle. He could actually see the entire Domain of the Gods!
Because the Domain of the Gods was unimaginably huge, from east to west, it was probably trillions and trillions of miles away …
Luo Zheng's field of vision was too far away. Naturally, he couldn't see the details of the Domain of the Gods clearly. At a glance, he could only see an oval gemstone emitting a white luster.
"It's really beautiful …"
Luo Zheng stared blankly at this scene.
After a long while, he turned his head and looked to the other side …
Around the gemstone, huge brown clouds flowed. The size of these huge clouds was ten times, or even a hundred times bigger than the Domain of the Gods.
"This is the Primal Chaos Qi!"
Luo Zheng was very familiar with the Primal Chaos Qi.
There was a lot of Primal Chaos Qi in his own inner world. Moreover, in the Immortal Mansion, he had read the travel notes left behind by his master, Gu Bei. In them, Gu Bei had accurately recreated the Primal Chaos Qi. It was exactly the same as the scene in front of him.
But at that time, Luo Zheng didn't have a precise concept of the Primal Chaos Qi. He only knew that the Primal Chaos Qi was filled with huge brown clouds. But he didn't know how huge these huge clouds were because he didn't have anything to compare them with.
But now, he could see the Domain of the Gods. Comparing the Domain of the Gods to the Primal Chaos Qi, Luo Zheng understood how huge these huge clouds were!
His gaze wandered around for a long time before returning to the Domain of the Gods.
Looking down, Luo Zheng noticed that there were small black dots attached to the surface of the Domain of the Gods.
There weren't many of these black dots, but there were still fifty or sixty of them …
"These black dots look small, but they're actually huge. They're attached to the surface of the Domain of the Gods … They should be the universe of the Saints." Luo Zheng's gaze became deep.
From where he was, he should be able to see the Domain of Great Expansion, but he couldn't tell which Saint these universes belonged to.
Luo Zheng's gaze continued to move downwards. After circling around the Domain of the Gods, he looked down and saw a grayish-white world …
"That's the bottom of the Primal Chaos?" Luo Zheng was shocked.
Whether it was Yu Taibai, his mother, Yang, or Elder Yuan, they all had the same answer.
The Domain of the Gods was falling rapidly …
From Luo Zheng's point of view, that was indeed the case.
Below the Domain of the Gods was a grayish-white world. And in this grayish-white world, slender beast bones stood upright like the teeth of a demon.
The length of each bone was comparable to the width of the entire Domain of the Gods!
Luo Zheng found it hard to imagine how huge these beast bones were when they were alive.
The bottom of the Domain of the Gods was actually a huge burial ground!

Chapter 2092
Luo Zheng was very shocked. He knew that the Chaos was unimaginably huge.
But if a single bone was bigger than the Divine Realm, then how huge were these behemoths when they were alive?
In the universe, Luo Zheng had also seen huge living beings.
For example, the Ancestral Dragon's complete form was thousands of miles long …
Some behemoths were even tens of thousands of miles long or even hundreds of thousands of miles long.
But these behemoths at the bottom of the Chaos were probably trillions of miles long. How powerful were these creatures?
How did they fall and fall to the bottom of the Chaos?
Luo Zheng's gaze swept over the huge bones and saw that they were piled up haphazardly, stretching far into the distance. Their line of sight was blocked by the Chaos aura …
In other words, the number of behemoths that had fallen here would far exceed Luo Zheng's imagination.
"I don't know what's hidden at the bottom of the Chaos, but if the 'ship' of the Divine Realm really falls, the extinction of the creatures in the Divine Realm is inevitable …"
If such a huge Divine Realm fell to the bottom of the Chaos, the entire Divine Realm would definitely shake violently and completely disintegrate.
The creatures in the Divine Realm would feel that the earth would crack and mountains and rivers would collapse …
In this process, ninety percent of the creatures would be destroyed. Perhaps some powerful creatures would be able to survive.
But after the Divine Realm broke, the Chaos aura would crazily surge in.
With the toxicity of the Chaos aura, the remaining creatures would perish. In the end, only a few dozen Saints would be able to survive.
And once the Divine Realm broke, the 'universe' attached to the Divine Realm, which was the inner world of the Saints, would also be affected. It was even possible that the universe would be destroyed …
In other words, Mu Haiji and Dongfang Chunjun would also be affected. It was just that Luo Zheng didn't know what countermeasures they had.
"According to the speed at which the Divine Realm is falling, it might not even take ten years to fall. No wonder Yu Taibai thought that this matter was imminent …"
Ten years was just the blink of an eye for True Gods.
"But how do I control the direction of the Divine Realm?" Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
Elder Yuan didn't give Luo Zheng any guidance. He only told Luo Zheng that he would know everything once he entered the sun.
The Divine Realm was known as a "ship". Then, to control this ship, there should be something like a rudder …
But there were only these rings in the palace …
Now that he was wearing these rings, other than gaining an extremely wide field of vision, there didn't seem to be anything special about it, let alone controlling the direction of the Divine Realm!
"The problem must be with these rings!"
At this moment, the other four rings were still on his hands and feet.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng immediately closed his eyes...
His soul suddenly trembled and he immediately released his Divine Sense.
These Divine Senses were like the tentacles of an octopus, spreading towards the four rings.
The moment his Spiritual Sense came into contact with those rings, the four rings suddenly trembled and emitted a buzzing sound at the same time.
"This ring absorbed my divine sense!" Luo Zheng was shocked.
At the same time, he felt that the four rings seemed to have merged with his body.
The four rings seemed to have become a part of his body. It was a very strange feeling.
"Is that so …"
Luo Zheng tried to move his arm. He lowered his right arm slightly …
Just as his arm moved, the front of the Divine Realm, which was a trillion miles long, actually sank with it!
The Divine Realm was originally diving down at a certain angle. Because of Luo Zheng's movement, the downward angle tilted even more.
At the same time …
Countless living beings in the God Realm felt a slight tremor and tilt.
The tremor wasn't strong, but it was very obvious!
In the Ruomu Domain.
Luo Yan stood on tiptoe on the edge of a tree as thick as an arm, looking at the sky.
A few hours had passed. The sun's trace had disappeared from the edge of the Ruomu Domain, taking her brother to the other side of the chaos.
Now, it was night time in the Ruomu Domain. Only stars could be seen in the sky.
Although her brother had the protection of the Green Parasol, Luo Yan was still very worried.
In such a blazing place, even a Saint would be mercilessly burned by the sun's fire essence, let alone a True God like her brother.
At this time, she suddenly felt a slight tremor coming from the treetops.
She found that her body couldn't help but lean forward. Her body also slid a step on the thin branches before stabilizing herself!
"What's going on?"
Luo Yan blinked.
Then Yu Taibai appeared on the tree trunk behind her with a faint smile on his face.
"Senior brother, what was that power just now?" Luo Yan asked strangely.
Although the tilt was very slight, it was unusually smooth. This meant that the range of the power was very large. At least the entire Ruomu Domain tilted at the same time to produce such an effect.
"Just now, the entire Divine Realm tilted to a certain extent." Yu Taibai smiled. "Luo Zheng succeeded!"
"Brother succeeded?" Luo Yan's face showed surprise.
Yu Taibai nodded and smiled. "But that kid seems to have gone in the wrong direction …"
Luo Yan was facing the west of the Divine Realm. Tilting in this direction would only make the Divine Realm fall faster.
"How could he have gone in the wrong direction?" Luo Yan asked curiously.
"Maybe … he's also feeling it out …" Yu Taibai replied.
Most people in the Divine Realm had noticed this slight tilt, but most of them didn't think much of it.
Only a very small number of people understood that just now, the entire Divine Realm tilted, and the tilt wasn't small. It was only because they were in the Divine Realm that they didn't feel it clearly!
Some people were wild with joy. They realized that someone was controlling the direction of the Divine Realm. This could save the Divine Realm that was in imminent danger!
Some people's faces were gloomy. After all, they had planned for a long time, but it was broken in one move. How could they accept it?
Luo Zheng, who was inside the palace, naturally didn't think so much.
"So through these rings, I can control the direction of the Divine Realm!"
Just now, when his right hand lowered, the front of the Divine Realm tilted down. Then …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng gently raised his right hand. The rings floating above his right hand also floated up.
At the same time, the front of the Divine Realm slowly rose up along with Luo Zheng's gesture!
"Success!"
Luo Zheng's face showed excitement.
After raising his right hand, Luo Zheng quickly realized that something was wrong. Only one side of the Divine Realm rose up, but the other side was still tilted.
After understanding the pattern, he once again slowly raised his left hand.
After slowly raising both hands, the Divine Realm had completely changed its falling direction, but it still maintained its downward momentum.
After all, the Divine Realm was such a huge object. Its falling inertia was enormous!

Chapter 2093
The unimaginably huge Divine Realm had changed its posture and direction.
Such an inconceivable thing could be easily achieved by just relying on these rings.
Luo Zheng could only sigh. The person who built this Divine Realm had such a miraculous method!
At this moment, Luo Zheng still felt a sense of accomplishment.
"But what power does the Divine Realm rely on to fly in the primal chaos?"
Among these seven rings, the two on top of his head allowed him to obtain a vast field of vision.
The four rings on his hands and feet allowed him to control the Divine Realm's posture.
There was only one ring left around his waist.
This time, Luo Zheng did not hesitate at all. He released his divine sense again and immersed it into the ring!
When Luo Zheng connected with the ring around his waist, he suddenly felt four vast energy fluctuations!
"These energy fluctuations are so terrifying …"
Such a huge energy fluctuation was completely beyond Luo Zheng's understanding. It could be said that Luo Zheng could not describe how strong these four energies were!
Moreover, he could clearly sense that these four energies came from the four corners of the Divine Realm.
"Fusang, Ruo, Xunmu, and Jianmu!"
"I can use the power of the Four Great Divine Trees through this ring! These energies can move the entire Divine Realm! So that's how it is … The Four Great Divine Trees are like the power of this' ship '. They are also the oars of this ship. They can provide endless energy to allow the Divine Realm to fly in the primal chaos! "
Dongfang family, Jian family, and Xuanyue family …
Although the three families occupied Fusang, Ruo, and Xunmu respectively, they only received some protection from the Divine Trees. They could not use the power of the Divine Trees themselves.
If he wanted to use the Four Great Divine Trees, he could only use the ring around his waist to control them in this palace!
After thoroughly understanding, everything seemed to become simple.
Through the ring around his waist, Luo Zheng released the energy of the Four Great Divine Trees.
Soon, four torrents suddenly splashed out from the four corners of the Divine Realm below the primal chaos!
These four torrents had different colors, but when these torrents started splashing out, the speed of the Divine Realm's descent slowed down at a visible speed.
"What a powerful energy …"
These four forces directly dispelled the inertia of the falling Celestial Dominion. The Celestial Dominion stopped in the Primal Chaos and began to slowly rise!
"It's amazing!" Luo Zheng said excitedly.
The mission that Yu Taibai gave him could be considered completed.
All he had to do now was wait!
However, he did not leave the rings. He was still in the field of vision above the Divine Domain, watching it gradually rise.
But at this moment, he suddenly sensed a red light flashing from below …
When mortals looked up at the starry sky in the universe, they would only see the stars as small as the eye of a needle. That was because they were too far away from each other. In reality, the stars were countless times larger than what mortals could imagine.
And in the primordial chaos, the scale of distance was magnified a hundred thousand times, a million times!
That red light came from the top of a beast bone at the bottom of the chaos. Luo Zheng could see it at a glance. He could imagine how dazzling that red light was.
Luo Zheng looked over curiously and saw a red band of light spread out from the red light. This red band of light rapidly spread towards the Divine Realm at a speed visible to the naked eye!
"What is this thing? It's so fast …"
Luo Zheng's pupils opened wide.
There was still a long distance between the Divine Realm and the bottom of the chaos!
But in the time it took for a stick of incense to burn, this red light had crossed the boundless distance and rolled towards the Divine Realm!
Luo Zheng could not figure out what this red band of light was. No matter how he looked at it, it did not look like a good omen …
"Hurry up, run away!" Luo Zheng clenched his teeth.
Through the rings at his waist, he urged the four sacred trees to burst out more energy.
Unfortunately, for such an inconceivably huge object like the Divine Realm, whether it was reducing or increasing its speed, it would take a long time.
No matter how Luo Zheng urged it, the Divine Realm was still like a slow snail, slowly climbing upwards.
And the speed at which the band of light spread was much faster than the speed at which the Divine Realm was rising!
Two hours later, this red band of light finally wound around the surface of the Divine Realm. It was like a small winding snake winding around the surface of the Divine Realm.
After spending another two hours, the red band of light wound around the Divine Realm once.
Luo Zheng looked at this scene with his eyes wide open. There was almost nothing he could do!
Before he adjusted the direction of the Divine Realm, it had been falling, and it had probably been falling for many Divine Eras.
For such a long time, the red band of light did not appear.
He had just adjusted the direction of the Divine Realm to avoid the final result of the Divine Realm falling when a band of light appeared from below and entangled the Divine Realm.
It was too much of a coincidence …
"Someone doesn't want the ship, the Divine Realm, to leave …" Luo Zheng murmured.
But what kind of existence were the creatures that lived at the bottom of the chaos? What was their purpose?
Could it be that the person who released the band of light was on Dongfang Chunjun's side? It could even be the Nirvana Realm expert behind Dongfang Chunjun …
In an instant, Luo Zheng came up with countless inferences in his heart.
Originally, he was immersed in the joy of completing the mission. Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng suddenly felt that all his efforts had been in vain.
He continued to urge the energy in the four Divine Trees, trying to get rid of the band of light.
As the Divine Realm continued to rise, it gradually pulled the red band of light straight. But no matter how Luo Zheng unleashed the energy in the four Divine Trees, and even used his hands and feet to make the Divine Realm circle left and right, up and down, he could not break the red band of light. It could only maintain its current position, suspended in the chaos.
"This is troublesome …"
Luo Zheng's face was full of helplessness.
In the process of Luo Zheng's attempt, it also caused a certain degree of chaos in the Divine Realm.
After all, when the Divine Realm shook greatly, it would still cause a lot of trouble. For example, some large rivers and lakes would overflow, causing floods. Even a considerable part of the colorful seawater in the Sea of Time gushed out!
Whether it was the Gods or the True Gods, they were all in a state of panic.
Yu Taibai and Luo Yan, who were waiting in the forest, also looked at each other.
"Why is the Divine Realm shaking so violently?" Luo Yan asked.
Yu Taibai also frowned. "That shouldn't be …"
Although Yu Taibai had never left the Divine Realm, he knew more things than some Saints through his master.
It was definitely abnormal for the Divine Realm to shake so violently! What had Luo Zheng encountered in the process of controlling the Divine Realm?
After several attempts, Luo Zheng could only choose to give up in the end.

Chapter 2094
After jumping out of the rings, Luo Zheng didn't choose to leave immediately. Instead, he continued to explore the palace.
Behind the main hall, there was a large hall.
Luo Zheng pushed open the door of this large hall. After stepping inside, his expression changed slightly.
It turned out that this large hall was dozens of times wider and hundreds of times higher than the main hall with the seven rings! From the bottom to the top of the hall, it was probably thirty to forty thousand feet high …
If an ordinary mountain was moved to this large hall, it would probably be very spacious.
And in the center of the large hall, there was a huge statue erected.
"Three heads and six arms?"
This statue was thirty thousand feet high and had three heads.
The three heads looked exactly the same, but the head in front was angry, the head on the left was happy, and the head on the right was sad.
The six arms each held a different weapon.
Saber, sword, axe, dagger-axe, hammer, crossbow …
"This sword is Tengshe's sword!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
This sculpted sword was exactly the same as the sword Hua Tianming used in the Illusory Battlefield!
"This is the statue of Chiyou himself!"
Luo Zheng quickly came to an answer.
The man that Luo Zheng saw in the Temple of Time was also the figure of Chiyou himself when he established the Divine Realm. That figure didn't have three heads and six arms.
This statue of Chiyou in front of him was probably another form of Chiyou's incarnation …
He looked at the statue for a while.
If it was really like what his mother said, and he was Chiyou's grandson, then Chiyou was his grandfather.
Such a powerful person who established the Divine Realm had no choice but to let his people escape. How powerful was the enemy he faced?
Luo Zheng shook his head …
That level of competition was already beyond his imagination.
Just like those huge beast bones at the bottom of the Primal Chaos outside the Divine Realm, Luo Zheng found it hard to describe the existence of such creatures in this world.
After sizing up the statue for a while, Luo Zheng looked at the wall behind the large hall.
Behind the large hall, there was a thirty to forty thousand feet high wall. And on the wall, there were many huge words.
"These words were left by Chi You himself." Luo Zheng's gaze followed the large characters on the wall.
Just like what his mother said, even Chiyou himself did not use Sanskrit. The words he carved on the wall were still ordinary words.
However, from this scribbled handwriting, Luo Zheng could feel the unyielding and humiliation in Chiyou's heart.
"I, Chiyou, am an expert in attacking and scheming. However, I was defeated by the Seven Continents. I am unwilling to accept this. I will build a boat and send millions of people to give my people a chance to live … I hope that the Primal Chaos Ancient God will protect my people!"
The words left on the wall were indeed similar to what his mother had said.
The reason why the Divine Realm was constructed was because Chi You had prepared a backup plan for the people.
Unfortunately, Chi You did not calculate that this path of retreat would be destroyed by the Xuan Yuan Wei before it could go very far.
Now, other than him, his mother, and that Elder Yuan, how many people were there in the God Realm? How many Chiyou Guards were there?
After sighing with emotion in his heart, he wandered around the hall and didn't notice anything special, so he chose to leave …
Three days later, it was still dusk.
When the sun once again reached the peak of Mount Ruo Mu, a figure holding an oil paper umbrella and stepping on a flying sword flew out from the roiling sea of fire.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
High up in the sky, Luo Zheng stepped on the flying sword and rapidly descended.
When he got out of the range of the sun's essence fire, he removed the light curtain on the Azure Parasol and put it away.
After falling like this for six hours, the continuous undulations of Ruo Mu finally appeared in front of him.
The flying sword under Luo Zheng's feet turned slightly. After determining a certain direction, it sped away …
The space of the Divine Realm was really too big. Even if Luo Zheng flew on the flying sword, it still took him several hours. It was not until five o 'clock in the morning that he arrived above Sword Transformation Divine City.
Just as he arrived above Sword Transformation Divine City, several guards of the Sword Race hurried over from below.
"Who dares to break into Sword Transformation Divine City!" A guard shouted.
Like most divine cities, Sword Transformation Divine City didn't allow flying. Luo Zheng was in a hurry to enter the city, so he naturally forgot about this.
However, these guards didn't recognize Luo Zheng.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to explain, a figure appeared beside Luo Zheng. It was Jian Wuhen.
"Lord Wuhen!"
"Lord Wuhen!"
Although the Saints of the Sword Race often stayed in Sword Transformation Divine City, ordinary True Gods rarely had the chance to see them. At this moment, they were naturally very respectful.
"Don't be rude to the distinguished guest of the Sword Race," Jian Wuhen said indifferently.
"Yes!"
The guards cupped their hands and quickly retreated.
Jian Wuhen turned to Luo Zheng and smiled. "Everyone has been waiting for a long time." Then, he led Luo Zheng into Sword Transformation Divine City.
After a while, Yu Taibai, Luo Yan, and Jian Leiting all gathered together.
"Brother! Are you okay? "
Luo Yan was most concerned about whether his brother was injured or not.
"Under the protection of the Azure Parasol, the sun essence fire couldn't hurt me at all," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Luo Yan nodded, only then did she feel relieved.
"Luo Zheng, what's the situation? Why is the divine realm shaking so violently? "Yu Taibai asked with concern.
"At first, I did succeed," Luo Zheng said with a frown. "It wasn't hard to control the divine realm from the palace."
"What about now?" Yu Taibai asked.
"After that, a red belt of light appeared from the bottom of the chaos. That belt of light wrapped around the entire Celestial Dominion. I tried to control the Celestial Dominion to break free from the red belt of light, but it was futile in the end … Now, the Celestial Dominion hasn't fallen, but it seems like it can't break free from the belt of light." Luo Zheng repeated what he had seen.
"Red belt of light? Binding the divine realm? "
Yu Taibai's eyebrows suddenly jumped.
Jian Wuhen and Jian Leiting also looked at each other.
As Sages, they were well aware of the vastness of the divine realm. Even the universe they created was as small as an ant on the surface of the divine realm.
What kind of belt of light could bind the entire divine realm?
How long was this belt of light? How much power did it contain to be able to hold the Deity Realm back?
This was too inconceivable!
"Yes, I definitely didn't see wrong. I observed for a long time. This belt of light came from the bottom of the divine realm, the bottom of the chaos," Luo Zheng said with certainty.
Yu Taibai's expression became ugly.
The usually calm him rarely had such an ugly expression …
"Yu Taibai, how is it?" Jian Wuhen asked curiously.

Chapter 2095

They could release a red band of light that could envelop the Mother World, and they could survive in the chaos.

Yu Taibai was very calm. He smiled and said, "I didn't trick you. You were already on this boat. If one suffers, all suffers. If one prospers, all prospers. Sooner or later, you will all make this choice!"
Yu Taibai's reasoning was simple.
If they didn't help the Luo family, everyone would be finished sooner or later. If everyone worked together, they might have a chance of survival.

…

Luo Zheng was not in a hurry to find his mother.

In the Sword Transformation Divine City, a secret cultivation room was arranged for Luo Zheng.

This scroll recorded the True Meaning of Good and Evil in One Thought.
Luo Zheng's usage of the True Meaning of the Dao was still at a relatively shallow level.
For example, Luo Zheng had only managed to fuse the True Meaning of Dao with Nature.

Just like when he cultivated the True Meaning of Nature, there was nothing special about a single character. However, after reading it together, this True Meaning of Dao's mental cultivation method immediately gave Luo Zheng a considerable amount of pressure.
"Evil in one thought is the shore of mutual destruction. Mutual destruction, mutual destruction … Good in one thought is the guide of the Righteous Path …"
At the beginning, his progress was very smooth.
Perhaps it was because he had cultivated the True Meaning of Nature, but within six hours, Luo Zheng had already comprehended three thousand characters without much hindrance.
"This is much faster than when I cultivated the True Meaning of Nature!"
A tired smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. Cultivating the True Meaning of Dao consumed a considerable amount of soul power.
However, he did not know that Elder Yuan was silently watching Luo Zheng in the deepest well of Ruo Mu.
The skin, flesh, and blood that he had previously condensed had completely rotted away. Now, he had once again turned into a skeleton.

Becoming a Nirvana Realm expert wasn't something that could be casually cultivated …

Chapter 2096

There was another way, which was to use another True Meaning of Tao to erase the imprint in the Chengying Sword. But this was more demanding on the wielder. Unless he was an expert who had reached the Nirvana Realm, it was impossible to do it.
Now Luo Zheng could not do it in either way.

However, his reaction speed was far inferior to that thing's.
"Chi!"
Luo Zheng felt a sharp pain in his soul.
This pain was countless times more intense than the pain of physical injuries. Luo Zheng's soul trembled.

However, this sword was too fast. Luo Zheng wanted to dodge, but he couldn't react in time. His soul was cut again.

Luo Zheng had a bad feeling in his heart.
Although his soul was far more powerful than an ordinary True God's, he couldn't fight back in this darkness.
He could only let the sword soul cut him bit by bit.
If this continued, his soul would become weaker and weaker, and sooner or later, it would be devoured by the sword soul!
Old Yuan didn't even remind him …
Luo Zheng was quite depressed.

No matter how Luo Zheng's soul escaped, the sword soul could always accurately cut Luo Zheng's soul.
"I can't go on like this …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly gave up on dodging. He stood still and waited for the sword soul to come closer.
No matter how powerful the sword soul was, he couldn't let it kill him!

In the darkness, the sword soul came closer to him again.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes turned cold. Suddenly, rays of golden light appeared on the surface of his soul!
Golden sword shadows kept revolving on the surface of his soul …

However, the sword soul circled around Luo Zheng's mind and flew toward him again. It seemed that it wouldn't give up until it destroyed Luo Zheng's soul.
A sword was actually so troublesome …

A cold soul and an emotionless sword soul confronted each other in Luo Zheng's mind.

Chapter 2097

If this soul wanted to kill Luo Zheng's soul, it would be as easy as crushing an ant.

Just as the soul fluctuation approached Luo Zheng and was about to enter Luo Zheng's mind, Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes.
At the same time, the surface of the Chengying Sword in his hand flashed with a trace of green sword light!

The cyan sword light suddenly flashed by and chopped at Yuan Chengdao's soul …

Chapter 2098

In the sky above the Sea of Time.
A figure slowly walked out of a cave at the top of the mountain.
Because Mu Haiji had directly attacked Luo Xiao's Huan Yu, he was punished by the Governing Slate to face the wall in a giant mountain and reflect on his actions.
The punishment of the Governing Slate was not fixed, but there was still a general pattern that could be followed.
Some rules had very severe punishments, such as attacking the floating islands of other wealthy clans without permission. If the violation of such rules was serious enough, one's life would be directly erased by the Governing Slate …
However, the Sage was not afraid of this move.
To Saints, lost lives could be recreated. After all, this was the meaning of becoming a Saint.
Sacrificing one's life in exchange for the other party's floating island and the tens of thousands of disciples on it could be revived after a period of time. No matter how one looked at it, it was a worthwhile trade.
Later on, the Governing Slate had other punishments for Saints.
Just like Mu Haiji, he was locked up in that giant mountain to reflect on his actions. In other words, he was imprisoned …
Some Saints who violated the rules of the Governing Slate would be punished for a divine era, or even several divine eras.
Behind this giant mountain, there was not just one cave, but as many as several hundred caves.
Apart from Mu Haiji, there were three other Saints, including the famous "Clipping Ceremony". The "Clipping Ceremony" that had once been famous had long since declined. The floating island had long since fallen into the sea of time. The entire "Clipping Ceremony" was no longer as glorious as it had been.

However, the "Clipping Ceremony" had been punished for ten eras. Only half an era had passed, so it would be quite a long time before he was released.
Mu Haiji's attack on Luo Xiao's Universe was a rather serious matter.
According to the ninety-third rule of the Governing Slate, Mu Haiji had to be locked up for at least three divine eras!
Dongfang Chunjun could not wait that long!
Three divine eras. By the time Mu Haiji was released, the war in the divine realm would have already been decided!
Lightning flashed and thunder rumbled within the giant mountain …
Thick lightning bolts were like frightening dragons, crazily rolling, coiling, and exploding. They radiated an aura that even Saints would fear.
A figure shot out, passing through the lightning bolts.

He carried a giant ink brush on his shoulder.
This ink brush was as long as a person, and it looked like a giant broom.

In a short while, Dongfang Chunjun had already flown to the top of the giant mountain.
At the top of this huge mountain stood a tall stone slab, and on the stone slab, there were densely packed words written on it.
Every word represented a rule of the divine realm!
The consequences of violating these rules were very serious. Since ancient times, very few people had been able to avoid the punishment of this slate.
Of course …
There had been more of them recently.
For example, those stowaways …
In the past two to three years, people had frequently stowaways into the divine realm from the universe.
However, they had all avoided the punishment of the Governing Slate.
Dongfang Chunjun sent people to investigate carefully, but they did not find any traces of stowaways.
He knew that those stowaways must have come from the Great Expansion Universe, but after Yu Taibai went around the Jiang family, he was really helpless.
In the end, he simply ignored them …
The stowaways in the universe were just a group of Proving Gods. So what if they were Lower True Gods? Could they change the situation?
Even if they could, there was no time. He no longer planned to deal with Yu Taibai. It was time for this game to end.
"The ninety-third rule …"
Swoosh!
Dongfang Chunjun's figure floated up, his fingers holding the giant ink brush, and his eyes narrowed slightly.
He then patted, and a brocade pouch appeared from his waist.
The Sumeru Space in this brocade pouch was very large, equivalent to a great world …
And there were countless Shenwu coins stored in this great world!
Over the years, in addition to the Dongfang family's Shenwu coins, the Wealthy Clan Alliance would also hand over half of their Shenwu coins to Dongfang Chunjun.
In terms of accumulation of Shenwu coins, the Dongfang family had dozens of times more than any other wealthy clan.
"I am destined to write the rules. What right do you have to fight me?"

Chapter 2099
Erasing the words on the Governance Slate also required Shenwu Coins.
How many Shenwu coins did it take to write the words? Erasing the words would cost an equivalent amount of Shenwu coins.
Depending on how strong the restriction was and who the target was, the amount of Shenwu coins required was also different.
For example, the ninety-third rule only prohibited Saints from directly attacking the universe of their opponents. The restriction wasn't strong, and it wasn't targeted at any one person. Thus, the amount of Shenwu coins required to write this rule wasn't too high.
Dongfang Chunjun's ink brush continued to scribble on the giant tablet.
The Shenwu coins in his brocade pouch disappeared piece by piece, transforming into a special kind of energy that flowed along the ink brush and onto the tablet …
The words "Divine Era" in the ninety-third rule began to blur, finally disappearing from the tablet.
After erasing the words, Dongfang Chunjun checked the brocade pouch. As he'd expected, the amount of Shenwu coins he'd spent wasn't too high.
Of course, this amount was still quite shocking even for some wealthy families …
Then, he continued to wave the brush in his hand and wrote a new unit of unit, "Year", replacing "Divine Era".
According to the previous punishment, Mu Haiji would be imprisoned for three full Divine Eras. After the change, it was only three years.
The moment he wrote down the new rule, a layer of light covered the entire tablet. This meant that the new rule had taken effect.
At the same time …
In the cave behind the giant mountain …
The invisible energy that had been reverberating in the cave had disappeared.
From the moment Mu Haiji had been captured, he'd been suppressed by an enormous power.
This power only affected him. No one else could feel it.
Thus, others could enter the cave to visit Mu Haiji, but he couldn't leave.
"Hm?"
Mu Haiji's eyes flashed, and he suddenly stood up.
Soon, a figure floated in from the entrance of the cave. It was Dongfang Chunjun!
"Brother Chunjun, this is …" Mu Haiji looked puzzled.
"I've changed the rules on the tablet," Dongfang Chunjun said calmly.
"What?!" Mu Haiji was stunned.
Although he was imprisoned here, he was not completely ignorant of the outside world. Every time something big happened in the outside world, Mu Xuerong would come to report to him.
He knew that the Tyrant Alliance had suffered a crushing defeat. Not only did they fail to stop Luo Zheng, they even lost many of their people.
"You felt the tremors in the Divine Realm some time ago, right?" Dongfang Chunjun asked.
Mu Haiji nodded.
"From the looks of it, it's impossible for the Divine Territory to fall. We can only take the initiative," Dongfang Chunjun said.
"Brother Chun Jun, what do you plan to do?" Mu Haiji's eyes flashed.
Leng Clan, Mu Clan, Tang Clan …
There was naturally a reason why these wealthy families were willing to join the alliance formed by the Dongfang family.
If the Divine Realm were to fall, the millions of living beings in the entire Divine Realm would be annihilated. Only the Saints of the other major clans would be able to survive. Even their inner worlds would be destroyed.
But they wouldn't.
Dongfang Chunjun had already prepared an escape route for them.
He would directly erase the rule on the Governing Slate that limited the number of True Gods in the universe. At the same time, he would strip all the universes from the Divine Realm …
These Saints, including Mu Haiji, would lead their clansmen to follow Dongfang Chunjun and head to the vaster Mother World.
"Help me control the Abyss Demon Region," Dongfang Chunjun said calmly. "The power in the Abyss Demon Region is enough to destroy the entire Divine Realm!"
"Control the Abyss Demon Region …"
Mu Haiji's eyebrows twitched slightly.
For Saints, there were not many places in the Divine Realm that they could not go to.
For example, if a Saint were to enter the Sea of Time, it might be dangerous, but it was not absolute …
However, the Abyss Demon Region was different.
Although the Abyss Demon Region was known as a Rank-6 Forbidden Area, it was difficult for even Saints to set foot in the lowest level of the Abyss Demon Region.
"It's too difficult." Mu Haiji shook his head. "The evil faith of the Divine Realm has gathered there for countless years. Can we do it?"
"He will help us," Dongfang Chunjun said calmly.
"That Xuan Yuan Wei?" Mu Haiji asked.
"Yes," Dongfang Chunjun replied. "There's not much time left. Even if we don't act, the Luo Family will sooner or later. Luo Shui … will also act."
Hearing this, Mu Haiji's face showed a hint of helplessness.
From the bottom of his heart, Mu Haiji greatly respected his elder brother, Dongfang Chunjun. Whether it was talent, scheming, or even perseverance and patience, he was extremely outstanding. Even among Saints, there were few who could compare to him.
However, this person could not see through the word "love".
That Li Clan woman and Luo Xiao already had a son and a daughter, but Dongfang Chunjun still could not forget her. Mu Haiji could not understand this.
However, since Dongfang Chunjun was willing to do it himself, Mu Haiji could not say anything.
Everyone was in the same boat and stood on the same side. Moreover, Dongfang Chunjun never went back on his promises.
…
…
Luo Zheng cultivated in the Ruomu Region for less than half a month.
However, at this stage, he could only focus on One Thought of Good and Evil. In terms of cultivation, Luo Zheng did not improve much. He was still a lower True God.
After almost grasping the "Shadow Bearing Sword" and One Thought of Good and Evil, Yu Taibai, Luo Zheng, and Luo Yan left the Battle of Dao.
After Luo Zheng activated the Gen Token, he directly returned to the Immortal Abode.
This time, Luo Zheng had only left the Immortal Abode for a few months.
Unexpectedly, during this period of time, Xun had relied on her own comprehension to attain the Divine Dao!
However, Xun still couldn't enter the Divine Realm as she pleased. After she became a "creature of the same level," she wouldn't be torn apart by the Divine Realm, but she would still be sensed by the Governing Slate. She would be wanted by the Governing Slate.
However, this time, it was Yu Taibai who accompanied them. After he learned of this situation, he took responsibility for it.
After all, it was Luo Xiao who taught Yu Taibai about Blood Change. Yu Taibai then let Xingwei enter the Great Yan Universe to establish the Demi-Immortal Clan.
If Yu Taibai could help them change their blood one by one, they would become True Gods in the future and be able to move freely in the Divine Domain.
When he returned to the Immortal Abode this time, Luo Zheng did not see Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng. Even Aunt Hanjiu was not in the Immortal Abode.
It turned out that after Aunt Hanjiu sent Luo Zheng to a place of no attack, she quickly returned to the Takiguan Forest and helped the two Gu brothers take control of the Takiguan Forest. She had not returned yet.
As for how to take control of the Takiguan Forest, Gu Bei probably already had a plan. Yu Taibai also did not plan to interfere.

Chapter 2100
Luo Zheng stayed in Immortal Mansion for three days.
Luo Yan also met Ning Yudie, Xun and the others.
However, Luo Yan was most interested in her little nephew.
During this time, Luo Nian was still traveling and cultivating inside the gourd. In the end, Luo Yan entered the gourd and forcibly pulled him out.
According to Luo Nian's character, he wouldn't have given up on 'extorting' his aunt.
However, after Luo Yan pulled Luo Nian out of the gourd, he was very obedient, not daring to disobey his aunt.
It turned out that Luo Yan and Luo Nian were family after all. Naturally, she was merciless in teaching Luo Nian a lesson. In the world of the gourd, she taught him a good lesson. After leaving the gourd, Luo Nian also became honest.
Three days later, just as Yu Taibai was preparing to leave with Luo Zheng.
Suddenly, a red light soared into the sky from inside the Takiguan Forest. The group of people in Immortal Mansion saw it clearly.
That crimson light spiraled up like a tornado, containing a terrifying power.
Not long after, a sharp roar was heard.
"Judging from the degree of the sound's attenuation, it should be thirty thousand li away from Immortal Mansion. That's the heart of the Takiguan Forest," Yu Taibai said with a frown.
Luo Zheng's eyes twinkled, "Is it Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng?"
"Yes," Yu Taibai nodded, "Perhaps they've reached a critical moment."
"Should we go help?" Luo Zheng asked.
Yu Taibai shook his head, "You're more important!"
"But they …" Luo Zheng said helplessly.
"Master and the others have already considered the possibility of failure. This isn't something you should worry about," Yu Taibai said while staring at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's face showed helplessness. Since Yu Taibai was so insistent, he couldn't say anything.
However, not long after the voice sounded, a white streak of light flew towards the immortal mansion. It was Jing Wuhuan himself.
Jing Wu Huan had the appearance of a young boy. He was spotless from head to toe, like a character from a celestial painting. But at this moment, Jing Wu Huan's body was full of injuries. Half of his ear was missing, and one of his hands was burnt black.
After rushing into the Immortal Mansion, Jing Wuhuan noticed Yu Taibai and Luo Zheng. He was slightly surprised. "Brother Taibai, long time no see." Then he asked with concern, "How is the situation?"
Jing Wuhuan naturally asked about the Dao Battle Ground.
Yu Taibai smiled and said to Jing Wuhuan, "It was very successful."
Jing Wu Huan nodded. "That's good."
"What about you guys?" Yu Taibai pointed towards the depths of the forest.
That scarlet light was gradually dissipating, but Jing Wu Huan was the only one who could be seen, which was worrying.
"It's a bit troublesome. Fortunately, Aunt Han Jiu is holding that guy back. That Waterfall Bird seems to have evolved over the years. Honoured Master has miscalculated," Jing Wu Huan shrugged and added, "But it doesn't matter, I can handle it!"
Yu Taibai was a core disciple of the Luo clan, while Jing Wuhuan was a core disciple of the Gu clan.
Both of them were monstrous figures in the Absolute Circle. Although their masters were on good terms and belonged to the same faction, they still faintly wanted to compete.
Yu Taibai had taken control of Bu Zhou Ling Mountain before Jing Wuhuan, which had already made Jing Wuhuan unhappy. However, his master had ordered him to wait for Luo Zheng to enter the Divine Domain before he had the qualifications to enter Takeshi Xuan Forest.
So when Jing Wuhuan deduced that Luo Zheng had already entered the Divine Domain, he immediately rushed to the Waterfall Forest with Yu Shen Feng.
"I can help you, but you have to send Luo Zheng away first." Yu Taibai smiled.
Who knew that Jing Wuhuan waved his hand. "You should take care of your own matters first. We don't need you here …"
After saying that, Jing Wuhuan went straight to the Pill Refining Room.
It wasn't easy to gain control of a Rank 6 Forbidden Area.
For example, Yu Taibai had already taken control of Buzhou Spirit Mountain a long time ago, but it wasn't until recently that he had truly taken control of it.
"Let's go. This guy loves to show off." Yu Taibai smiled.
"You're really not going to help?" Luo Zheng frowned.
"I'll come back after sending you there." Yu Taibai said.
Luo Zheng accepted Yu Taibai's suggestion. His goal was to go to Liuren Domain to see his mother. Once he reached his destination, he only needed to use the Gen Token to send Yu Taibai back. This wouldn't take much time. Moreover, looking at Jing Wuhuan, it didn't seem like he was in a hurry.
If Luo Zheng went to Liuren Domain by himself, with his speed, it would take him a long time to cross one or two domains. Moreover, it was very likely that he would attract some unexpected trouble along the way.
After leaving Luo Yan in Immortal Mansion and hurriedly saying goodbye to Ning Yudie and Xun, Yu Taibai and Luo Zheng once again left Takeshima Forest.
Yu Taibai was a Great Circle Paragon, so the distance he could move through space was far inferior to Jian Wuhen and Aunty Hanjiu's. But, the frequency at which Yu Taibai used space was much higher. Luo Zheng felt himself constantly shuttling through space. The scene in front of him rapidly changed, and he had no idea how far he had traveled.
Roughly eight hours later, a large city appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
"Liuren Divine City, Han Family's ancestral land." Yu Taibai said, "Where is Master's wife?"
"West of Liuren Ascension, Tie Family," Luo Zheng said.
Whoosh!
After confirming the location, Yu Taibai once again teleported with Luo Zheng.
When Luo Zheng had first entered the Divine Realm, it had taken him half a month to travel from Liuren Divine City to Tie Family. With this teleportation, it only took him the blink of an eye.
After arriving above Tie Family, Yu Taibai flicked his finger. A milky white screen of light immediately enveloped the two of them.
Luo Zheng had returned to this place. Naturally, he didn't want to alarm anyone. The milky white screen of light could completely conceal their auras. After all, although Tie Family was only a third-rate family, it still had a middle True God overseeing it.
In midair, Luo Zheng identified the place, then said, "It's here!"
"Let's go down." Yu Taibai brought Luo Zheng down.
The moment Yu Taibai touched the layer of soil, the soil automatically melted. The two of them were like arrows shot into the ground, entering it without being noticed.
At the same time, Yu Taibai flipped his hand and threw out a white flag.
This flag flew out of the ground and stuck into the ground. A gust of wind blew, and it expanded, forming an illusory formation.
This formation flag was one of the Luo Family's most precious treasures. Apart from Great Perfection Immortals, ordinary True Gods were unable to see through it. Furthermore, it was practically impossible for Great Perfection Immortals to approach this area. Naturally, it could conceal this place.
Not long after Yu Taibai brought Luo Zheng underground, they ran into a layer of gangue.
